And Zeel the garlic-seller fell upon Mob the confectioner, and cried, “Was this so, O Dob? *Wullahy!* this glory, was it verily?” And Dob peered dimly upon Zeel, whispering solemnly, “Say, now, art thou of a surety that Zeel the garlic-seller known to me, my boon-fellow?” And the twain turned to Sallap the broker, and exchanged interjections with him, and with Azawool the builder, and with Krooz el Krazawik the carrier; and they accosted Bootlbac the drum-beater, where he stood apart, drumming the air as to a march of triumph, and no word would he utter, neither to Zeel, nor to Sallap, nor to Krooz el Krazawik, nor to Azawool his neighbour, nor to any present, but continued drumming on the air rapidly as in answer, increasing in the swiftness of his drumming till it was a rage to mark him, and the excitement about Bootlbac became as a mad eddy in the midst of a mighty stream, he drumming the air with exceeding swiftness to various measures, beating before him as on the tightened skin, lost to all presences save the Identical and Shagpat. So they edged away from Bootlbac in awe, saying, “He’s inspired, Bootlbac! ’tis the triumph of Shagpat he drummeth.” They feigned to listen to him till their ears deceived them, and they rejoiced in the velocity of the soundless tune of Bootlbac the drum-beater, and were stirred by it, excited to a forgetfulness of their fasting. Such was the force of the inspiration of Bootlbac the drum-beater, caused by the burning of the Identical.
Now, the four Kings, when they had mastered their wits, gazed in silence on Shagpat, and sighed and shook their heads, and were as they that have swallowed a potent draught and ponder sagely over the gulp. Surely, the visages of the Kings of Shiraz and of Gaf and of Oolb betokened dread of Shagpat and amazement at him; but the King of the City exulted, and the shining of content was on his countenance, and he cried, “Wondrous!” and again, “*Wullahy*, wondrous!” and “Oh, glory!”

===Chinese===

城中百姓亦是如此，他们伫立在大殿之中，面面相觑，不住眨眼发愣，时不时欢呼雀跃，带着几近癫狂的庄重手舞足蹈，连声惊叹于眼前亲历的浩大奇迹。卖大蒜的齐尔扑向糖果商莫布，大声喊道：“方才那是真的吗，多布？凭安拉起誓（Wullahy）！那等荣光，委实是真的吗？”多布泪眼朦胧地端详着齐尔，肃穆低语道：“快说，你当真就是我认识的那个卖大蒜的齐尔，我的知心酒友吗？”二人又转向掮客萨拉普，同他互通惊叹之语，接着又同泥瓦匠阿扎乌尔以及挑夫克鲁兹·埃尔·克拉扎维克交谈；他们迎上前去同独自站在一旁的击鼓人布特尔巴克搭话，只见他正凌空击打，宛如奏响凯旋的进行曲。布特尔巴克却一言不发，既不理会齐尔，也不理会萨拉普、克鲁兹·埃尔·克拉扎维克，或是他的邻人阿扎乌尔，乃至在场的任何人，只是连连凌空急促敲击权当应答；他击鼓的速度越来越快，教人看了血脉贲张，布特尔巴克周遭的骚动恰似奔涌巨流中的疯狂漩涡；他以极快的速度按各种节拍凌空挥舞，仿佛面前紧绷着鼓皮一般，除却独一神发（the Identical）与沙格帕特，万物皆已不在他眼中。众人惊骇之余纷纷后退避让，口中喃喃：“布特尔巴克通神了！他敲击的分明是沙格帕特的凯歌！”他们凝神倾听，直听得耳鸣产生错觉，为击鼓人布特尔巴克那无声乐章的急促节奏欢欣不已，心潮澎湃，甚至忘却了腹中的饥渴。这便是独一神发燃烧所赋予击鼓人布特尔巴克的狂热神力。
此时四位国王回过神来，默默凝视着沙格帕特，叹息着连连摇头，犹如刚刚饮下一剂烈酒、正在细细咂摸回味的智者。设拉子、加夫与乌尔布三位国王的脸上无不显露出对沙格帕特的敬畏与惊愕；唯有本城国王喜不自胜，容光焕发，洋溢着心满意足的光彩，高呼：“奇哉！”随后又高呼：“凭安拉起誓（Wullahy），委实奇哉！”以及：“噢，无上荣光！”

===Original===

And he laughed and clucked and chuckled, and the triumph of Shagpat was to him as a new jewel in his crown outshining all others, and he was for a while as the cock smitten with the pride of his comb, the peacock magnified by admiration of his tail. Then he cried, “For this, praise we Allah and the Prophet. *Wullahy*, ’twas wondrous!” and he went off again into a roll of cluckings and chucklings and exclamations of delight, crying, “Need they further proof of the power in Shagpat now? Has he not manifested it? So true is that saying﻿—

> “ ‘The friend that flattereth weakeneth at length;

It is the foe that calleth forth our strength.’

“Wondrous! and never knew earth a thing to equal it in the range of marvels!”
Now, ere the last word was spoken by the King, there passed through the sky a mighty flash. Those in the hall saw it, and the horsemen of the three cities encamped without the walls were nigh blinded by the keenness of its blaze. So they looked into the height, and saw straight over the City a speck of cloud, but no thunder came from it; and the King cried, “These be genii! the issue of this miracle is yet to come! look for it, and exult.” Then he turned to the other Kings, but they were leaning to right and left in their seats, as do the intoxicated, without strength to answer his questioning. So he exclaimed, “A curse on my head! have I forgotten the laws of hospitality? my cousins are famished!” He was giving orders for the spreading of a sumptuous banquet when there passed through the sky another mighty flash. They awaited the thunder this time confidently, yet none came. Suddenly the King exclaimed, “ ’Tis the wrath of Shagpat that his assailants remain uncastigated!” Then cried he to the eunuchs of the guard, “Hither with Feshnavat, the son of Feil!” And the King said to Feshnavat, “Thou plotter! envious of Shagpat!” Here the King, Kresnuk, fell forward at the feet of Shagpat from sheer inanition, and the King of the City ordered instantly wines and viands to be brought into the hall, and commenced saying to Feshnavat, in the words of the wise entablature:

> “ ‘Of reckless mercy thus the Sage declared:

More culpable the sparer than the spared;

For he that breaks one law, breaks one alone:

But who thwarts Justice flouts Law’s sovereign throne.’

===Chinese===

国王咯咯大笑，合不拢嘴，沙格帕特的胜利在他眼中犹如王冠上一颗超越群芳的崭新宝石；此时此刻，他恰似一只为硕大肉冠洋洋自得的雄鸡，又似一只因孔雀开屏受人赞美而趾高气扬的孔雀。接着他高呼：“为此，让我们赞美安拉与先知！凭安拉起誓（Wullahy），真是奇哉！”言罢他又是一阵咯咯傻笑、暗自窃喜并欢呼连连，喊道：“如今众人还需要沙格帕特体内神力的进一步明证吗？难道他还未彰显无遗？正应了那句谚语——

> ‘谄媚之友终将消磨人的意志；
> 唯有强敌方能唤醒内在的力量。’

“奇哉！世间种种奇观，绝无任何事物能与之比肩！”
国王话音未落，天空中猛然划过一道刺目的电光。大殿内的人皆亲眼目睹，扎营在城墙外三座城邦的骑兵亦险些被那凌厉的炽烈白光晃瞎了眼。众人抬头仰望苍穹，只见城池正上方悬着一小抹薄云，却无半点雷声传出；国王大声喊道：“这是神怪！此等奇迹的高潮尚未到来！静候并欢呼吧！”随后他转向另外三位国王，却见他们宛若烂醉如泥之人东倒西歪地瘫在王座上，连回答他问话的气力都没有了。国王不由叫道：“我真是糊涂该死！难道我竟忘了待客之道？我的王兄王弟们早已饥肠辘辘了！”他正欲下令大摆奢华筵席，天空中又划过一道耀眼的闪电。这一次众人笃信雷声必至，然而四周依旧一片死寂。国王突然惊呼：“定是沙格帕特震怒于谋害他的人尚未受罚！”于是他向御林军的宦官厉声喝道：“将费尔之子费什纳瓦特押上来！”国王指着费什纳瓦特道：“你这阴谋家！竟敢嫉妒沙格帕特！”此时，国王克雷斯努克因极度虚脱，一头栽倒在沙格帕特脚边；本城国王立刻下令将美酒佳肴端入大殿，并引用智者铭文中的训诫训斥费什纳瓦特：

> ‘智者曾论及滥施仁慈之害：
> 姑息者罪甚于获宥之罪人；
> 犯法者不过触犯一条律例，
> 阻挠正义者却藐视王权之尊。’

===Original===

“And have I not been over-merciful in thy case?”
As the King was haranguing Feshnavat, his nostril took in the steam of the viands and the fresh odours of the wines, and he could delay no longer to satisfy his craving, but caught up the goblet, and drank from it till his visage streamed the tears of contentment. Lo, while he put forth his hand tremblingly, as to continue the words of his condemnation of the Vizier, the heavens were severed by a third flash, one exceeding in fierceness the other flashes; and now the Great Hall rocked, and the pillars and thrones trembled, and the eyes of Shagpat opened. He made no motion, but sat like a wonder of stone, looking before him. Surely, Kadza shrieked, and rushed forward to him from the crowd, yet he said nothing, and was as one frozen. So the King cried, “He waketh! the flashes preceded his wakening! Now shall he see the vengeance of kings on his enemies.” Thereupon he made a signal, and the scimitars of the guard were in air over the head of Feshnavat, when darkness as of the dropping of night fell upon all, and the darkness spake, saying, “I am Abarak of the Bar, preceder of the Event!”
Then it was light, but the ears of every soul present were pierced with the wailing of wild animals, and on all sides from the Desert hundreds of them were seen making toward the City, some swiftly, others at a heavy pace; and when they were come near they crouched and fawned, and dropped their dry tongues as in awe. There was the serpent, meek as before the days of sin, and the leopard slinking to get among the legs of men, and the lion came trundling along in utter flabbiness, raising not his head. Soon the streets were thronged with elephants and lions and sullen tigers, and wild cats and wolves, not a tail erect among them: great was the marvel! So the King cried, “We’re in the thick of wonders; banquet we lightly while they increase upon us! What’s yonder little man?” This was Abarak that stood before the King, and exclaimed, “I am the darkness that announceth the mastery of the Event, as a shadow before the sun’s approach, and it is the Shaving of Shagpat!”

===Chinese===

“而我对你，难道不是仁慈过度了吗？”
正当国王慷慨激昂训斥费什纳瓦特之际，鼻端飘来了菜肴的热气与美酒的醇香，他再也按捺不住肚里的馋虫，一把抓起金樽痛饮起来，直喝得满面流淌出心满意足的泪水。看哪，正当他颤巍巍伸出手来、欲继续严词斥责宰相之时，天穹被第三道闪电猛然劈裂，其威势远胜先前那两道；刹那间大殿摇撼，殿柱与王座剧烈震颤，沙格帕特睁开了双眼。他纹丝不动，宛如一座石雕奇观般凝视着前方。卡扎尖叫着从人群中冲上前去扑向他，他却一言不发，犹如冻僵了一般。国王高呼：“他醒了！方才的闪电正是他苏醒的先兆！如今他将亲眼目睹列王对仇敌施加的报应。”言罢国王打了个手势，侍卫的弯刀已高悬在费什纳瓦特的头顶，恰在此时，宛如夜幕骤降般的浓重黑暗笼罩了全场，那黑暗中传来声音说道：“我乃铁棒阿巴拉克（Abarak of the Bar），命定大事的先行者！”
光芒随即重现，但在场每一个人的耳中都钻进了狂野兽类的凄厉哀嚎，只见从四面八方的大漠深处，成百上千的飞禽走兽正朝着王城狂奔而来，有的迅疾如风，有的步履沉重；靠近之后，它们纷纷趴伏摇尾，耷拉着干瘪的舌头，似在膜拜敬畏。有那温顺如创世初罪之前的长蛇，有在人群腿间穿梭蜷缩的金钱豹，雄狮亦软绵绵地拖着步子蹒跚而行，连头都不敢抬起。不多时，街道上挤满了大象、雄狮、阴沉的猛虎、野猫与恶狼，没有一头猛兽敢把尾巴竖立起来：实乃旷世奇景！国王见状高呼：“我们正置身于奇迹之中；且趁奇迹纷至沓来之际痛快畅饮！那边的矮个子是何人？”站在国王面前的正是阿巴拉克，他朗声宣称：“我便是预告命定大事（the Event）主宰降临的黑夜，宛若旭日临空前投下的暗影，此乃剃度沙格帕特（the Shaving of Shagpat）！”

===Original===

The world darkened before the eyes of the King when he heard this, and in a moment Abarak was clutched by the soldiers of the guard, and dragged beside Feshnavat to await the final blow; and this would have parted two heads from two bodies at one stroke, but now Noorna bin Noorka entered the hall, veiled and in the bright garb of a bride, with veiled attendants about her, and the people opened to give her passage to the throne of the King. So she said, “Delay the stroke yet awhile, O Head of the Magnanimous! I am she claimed by Shagpat; surely, I am bride of him that is Master of the Event, and the hour of bridals is the hour of clemency.”
The King looked at Shagpat, perplexed; but the eye of Shagpat gazed as into the distance of another world. Then said he, “We shall hear nought from the mouth of Shagpat till he is avenged, and till then he is silent with exceeding wrath.” Hearing this, Noorna ran quickly to a window of the hall, and let loose a white dove from her bosom.
Then came there that flash which is recorded in old traditions as the fourth of the flashes of thunderless lightnings, after the passing of which, hundreds of fakirs that had been awaiting it saw nothing further on this earth. Down through the hall it swept; and lo! when the Kings and the people recovered their sight to regard Shagpat, he was, one side of him, clean shorn, the shaven side shining as the very moon!
Surely from that moment there was no longer aught mortal in the combat that ensued. For now, while amazement and horror palsied all present, the genie Karaz, uttering a howl of fury, shot down the length of the hall like a black storm-bolt, and caught up Shagpat, and whirled off with him into the air; and they beheld him dive and dodge the lightnings that beset him from upper heaven, catching Shagpat from them, now by the heels, now by the hair remaining one side his head. This lasted a full hour, when the genie paused a second, and made a sheer descent into the earth. Then saw they the wings of Koorookh, each a league in length, overshadow the entire land, and on the neck of the bird sat Shibli Bagarag cleaving through the earth with his blade, and he sat on Koorookh as the moon sits on the midnight.

===Chinese===

国王闻听此言，顿觉眼前一黑，转瞬间侍卫便将阿巴拉克死死擒住，拽到费什纳瓦特身旁等候处决；刀光原本顷刻便要一击斩下两颗头颅，但就在此时，努尔卡之子努尔娜（Noorna bin Noorka）步入大殿，她头蒙面纱，身着华美的凤冠霞帔，身旁跟随着数名蒙面侍女，人群纷纷左右分开，让出通往王座的道路。她开口道：“请暂缓动刀，宽宏大量的众王之首啊！我便是沙格帕特定下的新娘；我注定归属于命定大事的主宰，而大婚之辰乃是施恩宽宥之时。”
国王困惑地望向沙格帕特，然而沙格帕特的目光却宛若望向另一个世界的虚空。于是国王道：“大仇未报之前，沙格帕特绝不会开口说半句话，在此之前，狂怒令他保持缄默。”努尔娜听罢，飞快奔至大殿窗前，自怀中放飞了一只白鸽。
紧接着，古老相传的那道无雷闪电的第四道亮光骤然降临；待这道闪光闪过之后，苦苦守候于此的数百名托钵僧（fakir）便永远失去了视力，再也见不到人间的任何景象。那道强光穿透大殿横扫而过；瞧啊！当列王与百姓重拾视力望向沙格帕特时，只见他的半边身躯已被剃得精光，刮净的那半边面庞恰似明月般皎洁生辉！
自那一刻起，接踵而至的鏖战便不再属于凡人的尘世。正当在场的所有人被惊愕与恐惧惊得动弹不得之际，神怪卡拉兹（the genie Karaz）爆发出震怒的狂吼，如一道漆黑的暴风霹雳般自大殿一端疾射而下，一把抓起沙格帕特，卷起狂风冲上云霄；众人见他在天际俯冲腾挪，躲避着天穹降下的道道厉闪，时而抓住沙格帕特的脚踝，时而揪住他残存半边头顶的毛发，护着他不被雷电击中。这一追一逃足足持续了整整一个时辰，神怪倏然停顿了一瞬，径直向着大地一头扎下。紧接着，众人见神鸟库鲁克（Koorookh）的双翼遮天蔽日，每只翅膀足有一里格之长，笼罩了整片大地；希布利·巴加拉格（Shibli Bagarag）跨坐在鸟颈之上，挥舞利刃破土劈地，他端坐于库鲁克之上的英姿，恰似一轮皎月悬挂在子夜的夜空。

===Original===

There was no light save the light shed abroad by the flashes of the blade, and in these they beheld the air suffocated with afreets and genii in a red and brown and white heat, followers of Karaz. Strokes of the blade clove them, and their blood was fire that flowed over the feathers of Koorookh, lighting him in a conflagration; but the bird flew constantly to a fountain of earth below and extinguished it. Then the battle recommenced, and the solid earth yawned at the gashes made by the mighty blade, and its depths revealed how Karaz was flying with Shagpat from circle to circle of the under-regions, hurrying with him downward to the lowest circle, that was flaming to points, like the hair of vast heads. Presently they saw a wondrous quivering flash divide the genie, and his heels and head fell together in the abysses, leaving Shagpat prone on great brasiers of penal flame. Then the blade made another hissing sweep over Shagpat, leaving little of the wondrous growths on him save a topknot.
But now was the hour struck when Rabesqurat could be held no longer serving the ferry in Aklis; and the terrible Queen streamed in the sky, like a red disastrous comet, and dived, eagle-like, into the depths, re-ascending with Shagpat in her arms, cherishing him; and lo, there were suddenly a thousand Shagpats multiplied about, and the hand of Shibli Bagarag became exhausted with hewing at them. The scornful laugh of the Queen was heard throughout Earth as she triumphed over Shibli Bagarag with hundreds of Shagpats, Illusions; and he knew not where to strike at the Shagpat, and was losing all sleight of hand, dexterity, and cunning. Noorna shrieked, thinking him lost; but Abarak seized his bar, and leaning it in the direction of Aklis, blew a pellet from it that struck on the eye of Aklis, and this sent out a stretching finger of beams, and singled forth very Shagpat from the myriads of semblances, so that he glowed and was ruddy, the rest cowering pale, and dissolving like salt-grains in water.
Then saw Earth and its inhabitants how the genie Karaz re-ascended in the shape of a vulture with a fire beak, pecking at the eyes of him that wielded the Sword, so that he was bewildered and shook this way and that over the neck of Koorookh, striking wildly, languidly cleaving towers and palaces, and monuments of Earth underneath him.

===Chinese===

除了神刀锋芒迸射出的电光，天地间别无一丝光明，在闪烁的刀光中，他们看见整片天空被卡拉兹麾下的恶灵（afreet）与神怪（genii）堵塞得密不透风，这些魔物正散发着炽红、焦褐与惨白的狂热凶光。神刀横扫将它们劈成两半，它们的血液化为熊熊烈焰洒落在库鲁克的羽翼之上，瞬间将神鸟点燃成一团烈火；但神鸟不断俯冲飞向下方的大地泉眼，将周身烈焰扑灭。恶战随即再度爆发，坚固的大地在巨刃的劈砍下裂开道道万丈深渊，地底深处显露出卡拉兹挟持着沙格帕特在幽冥地府层层穿行遁逃的身影，正仓皇奔向最底层那如狰狞巨颅之发般尖端喷射烈焰的九幽炼狱。不一会儿，众人看见一道奇幻颤动的神光将神怪凌空劈成两段，他的头颅与脚跟双双跌入深渊，只剩沙格帕特俯卧在一盆盆刑罚烈火之中。紧接着神刀发出尖锐的呼啸，再次从沙格帕特身上一掠而过，除了头顶的一撮发结，那一身惊人的毛发几乎被剃得荡然无存。
然而就在此刻，拉贝斯库拉特（Rabesqurat）在阿克利斯（Aklis）服役摆渡的时限已满；这位可怕的女王如同一颗预示灾劫的猩红彗星划破天际，鹰隼般扎入九幽深处，怀抱着沙格帕特重返苍穹，极尽爱怜地护住他；看哪，四周骤然幻化出成千上万个沙格帕特，希布利·巴加拉格挥刀劈砍，双臂几乎精疲力竭。整座大地回荡起女王轻蔑的嘲笑，她用数百个虚妄幻象（Illusions）的沙格帕特压制了希布利·巴加拉格；他不知真正的沙格帕特身在何处，无从下手，精湛的刀法、敏捷的身手与智谋渐渐不支。努尔娜见状失声尖叫，以为他已大势已去；然而阿巴拉克猛然抓起神棒，将棒口对准阿克利斯的方向，自棒中吹出一枚弹丸，正中阿克利斯的神眼；阿克利斯顿时射出一道如手指般延伸的炽烈神光，从千千万万个化身中准确照出了真正的沙格帕特，令他通体泛红发亮，而其余虚幻的身影则面色惨白蜷缩瘫软，如水中盐粒般消融瓦解。
随后，大地与其生灵看见神怪卡拉兹化作一只长着喷火利喙的秃鹫重返战场，疯狂啄击执剑者的双眼，希布利·巴加拉格被啄得眼花缭乱，在库鲁克的脖颈上摇摇晃晃，只得胡乱劈砍，剑风无力地扫过下方的城楼、宫殿与人间巨构。

===Original===

Now, Shibli Bagarag discerned his danger, and considered, “The power of the Sword is to sever brains and thoughts. Great is Allah! I’ll seek my advantage in that.”
So he whirled Koorookh thrice in the crimson smoke of the atmosphere, and put the blade between the first and second thought in the head of Rabesqurat, whereby the sense of the combat became immediately confused in her mind, and she used her powers as the fool does, equally against all, for the sake of mischief solely﻿—no longer mistress of her own Illusions; and she began doubling and trebling Shibli Bagarag on the neck of monstrous birds, speeding in draggled flightiness from one point of the sky to another. Even in the terror of the combat, Shibli Bagarag was fair to burst into a fit of violent laughter at the sight of the Queen wagging her neck loosely, perking it like a mad raven; and he took heart, and swept the blade rapidly over Shagpat as she dandled him, leaving Shagpat but one hair remaining on him; yet was that the Identical; and it arose, and was a serpent in his head, and from its jaws issued a river of fiery serpents: these and a host of afreets besieged Shibli Bagarag; and now, to defend himself, he unloosed the twin genii, Karavejis and Veejravoosh, from the wrist of that hand which wielded the Sword of Aklis, and these alternately interwound before and about him, and were even as a glittering armour of emerald plates, warding from him the assaults of the host; and lo! he flew, and the battle followed him over blazing cities and lands on fire with the slanting hail of sparkles.
By this time every soul in the City of Shagpat, kings and people, all save Abarak and Noorna bin Noorka, were overcome and prostrate with their faces to the ground; but Noorna watched the conflict eagerly, and saw the head of Shagpat sprouting incessant fresh crops of hair, despite the pertinacious shearing of her betrothed. Then she smote her hands, and cried, “Yea! though I lose my beauty and the love of my betrothed, I must join in this, or he’ll be lost.” So, saying to Abarak, “Watch over me,” she went into the air, and, as she passed Rabesqurat, was multiplied into twenty damsels of loveliness. Then Abarak beheld a scorpion following the twenty in midair, and darting stings among them. Noorna tossed a ring, and it fell in a circle of flame round the scorpion.

===Chinese===

此时，希布利·巴加拉格察觉到了自身的凶险，心想：“这柄神剑的威力在于能斩断脑髓与思绪。安拉至大！我当以此扭转战局。”
于是他驾驭库鲁克在猩红的硝烟中盘旋了三圈，一剑插入拉贝斯库拉特的脑海，横亘在她的一念与二念之间，使得女王的心智刹那间陷入混乱，开始像蠢人那般胡乱施展法力，无差别地攻击目力所及的每一个人，纯粹为了作恶捣乱——她再也无法随心所欲驾驭自己的幻象；她甚至开始将骑在巨鸟脖颈上的希布利·巴加拉格化为一个又一个的分身，任由分身在天空各处东奔西突、狼狈飞窜。即便置身于凶险万分的恶战中，希布利·巴加拉格见女王伸长脖颈胡乱摇晃、像只疯渡鸦般顾盼作态的模样，亦忍不住险些放声大笑；他士气大振，趁女王哄抱沙格帕特之际挥刀迅捷掠过，削得沙格帕特通体上下只余下一根毛发；然而那独独剩下的一根正是独一神发（the Identical）；它猛然挺立而起，化作沙格帕特头顶的一条巨蟒，从巨口中喷涌出滔滔不绝的炽烈火蛇：这些火蛇与成群的恶灵（afreet）团团围攻希布利·巴加拉格；为了自卫，他连忙从挥舞阿克利斯神剑的手腕上解下孪生神怪卡拉韦吉斯与维吉拉武什（Karavejis and Veejravoosh），两尊神怪在他身前周遭交替盘绕，宛若一套由璀璨翡翠鳞甲构筑的护身宝甲，替他挡下敌群的连番猛攻；瞧啊！他破空疾飞，战场随之蔓延，掠过一座座燃烧的城镇与被斜落的火雨冰雹点燃的焦土。
此时此刻，沙格帕特城中的所有生灵，无论是列王抑或万民，除却阿巴拉克与努尔卡之子努尔娜之外，尽皆瘫软在地、面孔贴紧尘土；然而努尔娜依然急切注视着苍穹的恶战，眼见尽管未婚夫接连不断地奋力剃削，沙格帕特的头顶却依旧源源不绝地疯长出新的毛发。她拍手惊呼道：“罢了！即便我会失去容貌与未婚夫的爱恋，我也必须参战，否则他必死无疑。”说罢，她对阿巴拉克嘱咐道：“好生看守我的肉身，”便腾空飞向苍穹，当她掠过拉贝斯库拉特身旁时，化身成了二十位绝色佳人。阿巴拉克随即看见一只巨蝎在半空中紧随这二十道倩影身后，向她们连连施放毒刺。努尔娜抛出一枚戒指，戒指落下化作一圈烈焰，将巨蝎死死困在正中。

===Original===

So, while the scorpion was shooting in squares to escape from the circle, the fire-beaked vulture flew to it, and fluttered a dense rain which swallowed the flame, and the scorpion and vulture assailed Noorna, that was changed to a golden hawk in the midst of nineteen other golden hawks. Now, as Rabesqurat came scudding by, and saw the encounter, she made the twenty hawks a hundred. The genie Karaz howled at her, and pinioned her to a pillar below in the Desert, with Shagpat in her arms. But, as he soared aloft to renew the fight with Noorna, Shibli Bagarag loosed to her aid the Slaves of the Sword, and Abarak marked him slope to a distant corner of Earth, and reascend in a cloud, which drew swiftly over the land toward the Great Hall. Lo, Shibli Bagarag stepped from it through a casement of the hall, and with him Shagpat, a slack weight, mated out of all power of motion. Koorookh swooped low, on his back Baba Mustapha, and Shibli Bagarag flung Abarak beside him on the bird. Then Koorookh whirred off with them; and while the heavens raged, Shibli Bagarag prepared a rapid lather, and dashed it over Shagpat, and commenced shearing him with lightning sweeps of the blade. ’Twas as a racing wheel of fire to see him! Suddenly he desisted, and wiped the sweat from his face. Then calling on the name of Allah, he gave a last keen cunning sweep with the blade, and following that, the Earth awfully quaked and groaned, as if speaking in the abysmal tongue the Mastery of the Event to all men. Aklis was revealed in burning beams as of a sun, and the trouble of the air ceased, vapours slowly curling to the four quarters. Shibli Bagarag had smitten clean through the Identical! Terribly had Noorna and those that aided her been oppressed by the multitude of their enemies; but, in a moment these melted away, and Karaz, together with the scorpion that was Goorelka, vanished. Day was on the baldness of Shagpat.

===Chinese===

正当巨蝎在火圈中左冲右突试图突围之际，喷火利喙的秃鹫飞掠而至，扑打双翼洒下一场倾盆大雨扑灭了烈焰，巨蝎与秃鹫一同围攻努尔娜，此时努尔娜已化作一只金鹰，隐于另外十九只金鹰之间。拉贝斯库拉特飞掠而过目睹战局，随手一挥将二十只金鹰增殖为一百只。神怪卡拉兹对她发出愤怒的咆哮，将她连同怀里的沙格帕特一同捆缚在下方大漠的一根石柱上。然而正当他腾空而起欲重新同努尔娜决战之际，希布利·巴加拉格派出剑之奴仆（Slaves of the Sword）前去支援未婚妻，阿巴拉克看见他俯冲疾驰至大地的偏远角落，随后驾着一朵云彩重返苍穹，云彩贴着大地疾驰，直奔巍峨大殿而来。瞧，希布利·巴加拉格穿过大殿的窗扇迈步而入，手里提着沙格帕特，此时沙格帕特已浑身瘫软，彻底丧失了一切行动的气力。库鲁克低空俯冲而至，背上载着巴巴·穆斯塔法，希布利·巴加拉格将阿巴拉克一把掷到鸟背上与他同坐。库鲁克随即载着二人呼啸远去；趁着苍穹激战方酣，希布利·巴加拉格迅速打起肥皂沫泼在沙格帕特头上，挥动利刃如疾风骤雨般开始剃发。看他挥刀的身影，宛若一只飞速旋转的火轮！他猛然停下动作，擦去面颊上的汗水。随后他高呼安拉的圣名，运足神力挥出精妙绝伦的最后一击，紧接着大地骇人地震颤呻吟，仿佛用幽冥之音向世人宣告命定大事的终局（the Mastery of the Event）。阿克利斯在宛若烈日般的熊熊光芒中显现，长空中的喧嚣骚动归于沉寂，烟霞向四方徐徐散去。希布利·巴加拉格已彻底将独一神发（the Identical）齐根斩断！努尔娜与支援她的诸神此前受尽敌群的凶残围攻；但转瞬之间妖魔鬼怪尽皆消散，卡拉兹与化作巨蝎的古雷尔卡（Goorelka）亦化为乌有。曙光洒落在沙格帕特光溜溜的秃顶之上。

## The Genie Karaz / 神怪卡拉兹
===Original===

Now, when the voice of the Vizier had ceased, Shibli Bagarag exclaimed, “O Vizier, this night, no later, I’ll surprise Shagpat, and shave him while he sleepeth: and he shall wake shorn beside his spouse. *Wullahy!* I’ll delay no longer, I, Shibli Bagarag.”
Said the Vizier, “Thou?”
And he replied, “Surely, O Vizier! thou knowest little of my dexterity.”
So the Vizier laughed, and Noorna bin Noorka laughed, and he was at a loss to interpret the cause of their laughter. Then said Noorna, “O my betrothed, there’s not a doubt among us of thy dexterity, nor question of thy willingness; but this shaving of Shagpat, *wullahy!* ’tis longer work than what thou makest of it.”
And he cried, “How? because of the Chief of Identicals planted by thee in his head?”
She answered, “Because of that; but ’tis the smallest opposer, that.”
Then the Vizier said, “Let us consult.”
So Shibli Bagarag gave ear, and the Vizier continued, “There’s first, the Chief of Identicals planted by thee in the head of that presumptuous fellow, O my daughter! By what means shall that be overcome?”
She said, “I rank not that first, O Feshnavat, my father; surely I rank first the illusions with which Rabesqurat hath surrounded him, and made it difficult to know him from his semblances, whenever real danger threateneth him.”
The Vizier assented, saying, “Second, then, the Chief of Identicals?”
She answered, “Nay, O my father; second, the weakness that’s in man, and the little probability of his finishing with Shagpat at one effort; and there is but a sole chance for whoso attempteth, and if he faileth, ’tis forever he faileth.”
So the Vizier said, “Even I knew not ’twas so grave! Third, then, the Chief of Identicals?”
She replied, “Third! which showeth the difficulty of the task. Read ye not, first, how the barber must come upon Shagpat and fix him for his operation; second, how the barber must be possessed of more than mortal strength to master him in so many strokes; third, how the barber must have a blade like no other blade in this world in sharpness, in temper, in velocity of sweep, that he may reap this crop which flourisheth on Shagpat, and with it the magic hair which defieth edge of mortal blades?”
Now, the Vizier sighed at the words, saying, “Powerful is Shagpat. I knew not the thing I undertook. I fear his mastery of us, and we shall be contemned﻿—objects for the red finger of scorn.”

===Chinese===

宰相话音刚落，希布利·巴加拉格（Shibli Bagarag）便高呼道：“宰相大人，就在今夜，绝不再拖延，我定要打沙格帕特（Shagpat）一个措手不及，趁他沉睡之际将他剃光；待他醒来，必已光溜溜地躺在妻子身旁。凭安拉起誓（Wullahy）！我希布利·巴加拉格绝不再拖延！”
宰相问：“凭你？”
他答道：“千真万确，宰相大人！您对我手艺的敏捷利落实在知之甚少。”
宰相闻言大笑起来，努尔卡之子努尔娜（Noorna bin Noorka）也忍俊不禁，他一时摸不着头脑，不知二人为何发笑。接着努尔娜开口道：“我的未婚夫啊，我们丝毫不怀疑你的精湛敏捷，更不怀疑你的满腔热忱；但剃度沙格帕特（the shaving of Shagpat）这桩大事，凭安拉起誓（Wullahy）！绝非你想象得那般简单轻巧。”
他急忙追问：“这是为何？难道是因为你植入他头顶的众神发之魁（the Chief of Identicals）？”
她答道：“这确是一由，但那不过是其中最小的阻碍罢了。”
宰相道：“让我们从长计议。”
于是希布利·巴加拉格洗耳恭听，宰相接着道：“我的女儿啊，首先便是你植入那个狂妄之徒头顶的众神发之魁！究竟该如何克制它？”
她道：“我并未将此列为首要之难，我的父亲费什纳瓦特（Feshnavat）；在我看来，首要之难乃是拉贝斯库拉特（Rabesqurat）围绕在他周遭布下的重重幻象，每当真切的凶险逼近之际，教人根本分不清哪个是他、哪个是他的替身。”
宰相点头称是，问道：“那么排在第二位的，可是众神发之魁？”
她答道：“非也，父亲；排在第二位的，是凡人与生俱来的软弱，以及极难一鼓作气将沙格帕特彻底剃尽；凡尝试之人唯有一次机会，一旦失手，便永无翻盘之日。”
宰相叹道：“连我都未曾料到事情竟凶险至此！那么排在第三位的，才是众神发之魁？”
她答道：“正是第三！由此可见这桩差事何其艰难。难道你们不明白：第一，理发师必须设法近身制住沙格帕特，使其无法动弹；第二，理发师必须拥有超越凡人的神力，方能在数刀之内彻底将他降伏；第三，理发师必须拥有一柄世间罕匹的神刃，其锋利、韧性与挥斩之迅猛举世无双，方能收割沙格帕特身上疯长的须发，并斩断那凡间利刃绝难伤其分毫的魔发？”
听到这里，宰相不禁长叹一声，道：“沙格帕特法力滔天。我此前竟不知自己挑起的是何等重担。只怕到头来反被他制服，落得受人唾弃，沦为千夫所指的耻辱笑柄。”

===Original===

Noorna turned to Shibli Bagarag and asked, “Do the three bonds of enterprise﻿—vengeance, ambition, and love﻿—shrink in thee from this great contest?”
Shibli Bagarag said, “ ’Tis terrible! on my head be it!”
She gazed at him a moment tenderly, and said, “Thou art worthy of what is in store for thee, O my betrothed! and I think little of the dangers, in contemplation of the courage in thee. Lo, if vengeance and ambition spur thee so, how will not love when added to the two?”
Then said she, “As to the enchantments and spells that shall overreach him, and as to the blade wherewith to shear him?”
Feshnavat exclaimed, “Yonder’s indeed where we stumble and are tripped at starting.”
But she cried, “What if I know of a sword that nought on Earth or under resisteth, and before the keen edge of which all Illusions and Identicals are as summer grass to the scythe?”
They both shouted, “The whereabout of that sword, O Noorna!”
So she said, “ ’Tis in Aklis, in the mountains of the Kush; and the seven sons of Aklis sharpen it day and night till the adventurer cometh to claim it for his occasion. Whoso succeedeth in coming to them they know to have power over the sword, and ’tis then holiday for them. Many are the impediments, and they are as holes where the fox haunteth. So they deliver to his hand the sword till his object is attained, his Event mastered, smitten through with it; and ’tis called the Sword of Events. Surely, with it the father of the Seven vanquished the mighty Roc, Kroojis, that threatened mankind with ruin, and a stain of the Roc’s blood is yet on the hilt of the sword. How sayest thou, O Feshnavat﻿—shall we devote ourselves to get possession of that Sword?”
So the Vizier brightened at her words, and said, “O excellent in wisdom and star of counsel! speak further, and as to the means.”
Noorna bin Noorka continued, “Thou knowest, O my father, I am proficient in the arts of magic, and I am what I am, and what I shall be, by its uses. ’Tis known to thee also that I hold a genie in bondage, and can utter ten spells and one spell in a breath. Surely my services to the youth in his attainment of the Sword will be beyond price!

===Chinese===

努尔娜转向希布利·巴加拉格，问道：“驱使凡人建功立业的三大纽带——复仇、抱负与情爱——是否会令你在如此严峻的较量面前退缩？”
希布利·巴加拉格道：“纵使凶险万状，我也甘愿一力承担！”
她含情脉脉地注视了他片刻，说道：“我的未婚夫啊，你配得上等待着你的崇高宿命！看到你这般英勇无畏，那些艰难险阻在我眼中便算不得什么了。试想，若复仇与抱负已然教你如此奋勇，待情爱融入其中，又该激发出何等惊人的力量？”
随后她又道：“至于克制其法力的符咒，以及用于剃度的利刃，又该从何处着手？”
费什纳瓦特感叹道：“这正是我们起步维艰、寸步难行之处。”
她却高呼道：“若我知晓有一柄神剑，无论天上地下皆无物可当，在其锋芒之下，世间一切幻象（Illusions）与神发（Identicals）皆如夏草遭遇镰刀般不堪一击，又当如何？”
二人异口同声喊道：“那柄神剑在何方，努尔娜？”
她答道：“此剑藏于库什（Kush）山脉的阿克利斯（Aklis）；阿克利斯的七个儿子日夜不停地磨砺此剑，专候勇者前来因时取剑。凡能历经万难来到他们面前之人，他们便知其拥有驾驭此剑的资格，届时他们便可功德圆满、得享安歇。前路关卡重重，险恶如狡狐出没的洞穴。他们会将神剑交付勇者之手，直至大功告成，凭此神剑将命定大事（the Event）彻底劈开、降伏于掌中；此剑因此得名‘胜事之剑’（the Sword of Events）。昔日，那七子的生父正是凭借此剑诛灭了企图毁灭苍生的大鹏鸟克鲁吉斯（Kroojis），剑柄上至今仍残留着大鹏鸟的血迹。意下如何，我的父亲费什纳瓦特——我们是否全力以赴夺取此剑？”
宰相闻言神色大振，喜道：“智慧卓绝之女、指路明灯啊！快细细道来，我们该当如何行事。”
努尔卡之子努尔娜继续说道：“父亲，您深知我精通各门巫术，我之所以能有今日与未来，全赖巫术之功。您亦知晓我手中奴役着一尊神怪，一口气便能连施十一道咒语。在这少年夺取神剑的征途中，我能助他的一臂之力必是无价之宝！

===Original===

Now to reach Aklis and the Sword there are three things needed﻿—charms: and one is a phial full of the waters of Paravid from the wells in the mountain yonside the desert; and one, certain hairs that grow in the tail of the horse Garraveen, he that roameth wild in the meadows of Melistan; and one, that the youth gather and bear to Aklis, for the white antelope Gulrevaz, the Lily of the Lovely Light that groweth in the hollow of the crags over the Enchanted Sea: with these spells he will command the Sword of Aklis, and nothing can bar him passage. Moreover I will expend in his aid all my subtleties, my transformations, the stores of my wisdom. Many seek this Sword, and people the realms of Rabesqurat, or are beasts in Aklis, or crowned Apes, or go to feed the Roc, Kroojis, in the abyss beneath the Roc’s-egg bridge; but there’s virtue in Shibli Bagarag: *wullahy!* I am wistful in him of the hand of Destiny, and he will succeed in this undertaking if he dareth it.”
Shibli Bagarag cried, “At thy bidding, O Noorna! Care I for dangers? I’m on fire to wield the Sword, and master the Event.”
Thereupon, Noorna bin Noorka arose instantly, and took him by the cheeks a tender pinch, and praised him. Then drew she round him a circle with her forefinger that left a mark like the shimmering of evanescent green flame, saying, “White was the day I set eyes on thee!” Round the Vizier, her father, she drew a like circle; and she took an unguent, and traced with it characters on the two circles, and letters of strange form, arrowy, lance-like, like leaning sheaves, and crouching baboons, and kicking jackasses, and cocks a-crow, and lutes slack-strung; and she knelt and mumbled over and over words of magic, like the drone of a bee to hear, and as a roll of water, nothing distinguishable. After that she sought for an unguent of a red colour, and smeared it on a part of the floor by the corner of the room, and wrote on it in silver fluid a word that was the word “Eblis,” and over that likewise she droned awhile. Presently she arose with a white-heated face, the sweat on her brow, and said to Shibli Bagarag and Feshnavat hurriedly and in a harsh tone, “How? have ye fear?”
They answered, “Our faith is in Allah, our confidence in thee.”
Said she then, “I summon the genie I hold in bondage. He will be wrathful; but ye are secure from him.

===Chinese===

如今若要抵达阿克利斯并取得神剑，需备齐三样护身法宝：其一，是装满大漠彼方深山古井中帕拉维德之水（the waters of Paravid）的小药瓶；其二，是游荡在梅利斯坦（Melistan）草场上的野马加拉文（Garraveen）尾巴上的若干缕鬃毛；其三，是少年必须采撷生长在迷境之海（the Enchanted Sea）峭壁石缝中的妙光百合（the Lily of the Lovely Light），带往阿克利斯献给那头白羚羊古尔雷瓦兹（Gulrevaz）：备齐这三桩法宝，他便能号令阿克利斯神剑，天地间再无阻碍能拦住他的去路。不仅如此，我更会倾尽平生所学，以一切机巧、变形术与满腹韬略全力相助。世间求取此剑者数不胜数，却大多沦为拉贝斯库拉特领地的亡魂，或变成阿克利斯的走兽、戴王冠的猿猴，或跌落大鹏蛋石桥底下的万丈深渊，沦为大鹏鸟克鲁吉斯腹中的口粮；然而希布利·巴加拉格身负异禀：凭安拉起誓（wullahy）！我在他身上真切感受到了命运之手的主宰，只要他敢作敢当，此行必克大功。”
希布利·巴加拉格高呼：“悉听你的差遣，努尔娜！我何惧凶险？我早已满腔热血，誓要挥舞神剑，成就命定大事！”
努尔卡之子努尔娜闻言当即起身，温存地轻捏他的脸颊，对他赞赏有加。接着她用食指在他周身画了一道圆圈，指尖留下一道如幽幽青焰般闪烁跳动的痕迹，口中说道：“初遇你之日乃吉祥之日！”随后她在父亲宰相身周亦画下了同样的圆圈；她取出一盒油膏，在两个圆圈周围勾勒出神秘的符文与形状怪异的字母，有的形似箭矢，有的利如长矛，有的状如斜倚的麦捆、蜷伏的狒狒、尥蹶子的野驴、啼鸣的雄鸡以及松了弦的鲁特琴；她双膝跪地，翻来覆去喃喃念诵咒语，听来宛如群蜂嗡鸣，又似流水翻滚，教人浑然莫辨。随后她又寻出一盒红色的药膏，涂抹在房间一角的地板上，以银色药液在上面书写了一个名讳，赫然便是“易卜劣斯”（Eblis），她俯在上面同样低吟了良久。不多时，她面带苍白狂热之色缓缓站起，额头上沁满了汗珠，急促而厉声对希布利·巴加拉格与费什纳瓦特道：“怎么？你们心中可有畏惧？”
二人齐声答道：“我们虔信安拉，对你深信不疑。”
她这才说道：“我即将召唤那尊被我奴役的神怪。他必会怒火滔天；但在这圆圈之中，你们安然无恙。

===Original===

He’s this moment in the farthest region of Earth, doing ill, as is his wont, and the wont of the stock of Eblis.”
So the Vizier said, “He’ll be no true helper, this genie, and I care not for his company.”
She answered, “O my father! leave thou that to me. What says the poet?﻿—

> “ ‘It is the sapiency of fools,

To shrink from handling evil tools.’ ”

===Chinese===

“他此时此刻正身处大地最偏远的角落作恶多端，这正是他的本性，亦是易卜劣斯一脉子孙的宿习。”
宰相闻言说道：“这神怪绝不会是真心相助的盟友，我可不愿与他为伍。”
她答道：“父亲！一切交由我来处置便是。正如诗人所言：

> ‘凡遇恶物便畏缩不敢御用者，
> 乃是愚人自命的聪明。’

===Original===

Now, while she was speaking, she suddenly inclined her ear as to a distant noise; but they heard nothing. Then, after again listening, she cried in a sharp voice, “Ho! muffle your mouths with both hands, and stir not from the ring of the circles, as ye value life and its blessings.”
So they did as she bade them, and watched her curiously. Lo! she swathed the upper and lower part of her face in linen, leaving the lips and eyes exposed; and she took water from an ewer, and sprinkled it on her head, and on her arms and her feet, muttering incantations. Then she listened a third time, and stooped to the floor, and put her lips to it, and called the name, “Karaz!” And she called this name seven times loudly, sneezing between whiles. Then, as it were in answer to her summons, there was a deep growl of thunder, and the palace rocked, tottering; and the air became smoky and full of curling vapours. Presently they were aware of the cry of a cat, and its miaulings; and the patch of red unguent on the floor parted and they beheld a tawny cat with an arched back. So Noorna bin Noorka frowned fiercely at the cat, and cried, “This is thy shape, O Karaz; change! for it serves not the purpose.”
The cat changed, and was a leopard with glowing yellow eyes, crouched for the spring. So Noorna bin Noorka stamped, and cried again, “This is thy shape, O Karaz; change! for it serves not the purpose.”
And the leopard changed, and was a serpent with many folds, sleek, curled, venomous, hissing.
Noorna bin Noorka cried in wrath, “This is thy shape, O Karaz; change! or thou’lt be no other till Eblis is accepted in Paradise.”
And the serpent vanished. Lo! in its place a genie of terrible aspect, black as a solitary tree seared by lightning; his forehead ridged and cloven with red streaks; his hair and ears reddened; his eyes like two hollow pits dug by the shepherd for the wolf, and the wolf in them. He shouted, “What work is it now, thou accursed traitress?”
Noorna replied, “I’ve need of thee!”
He said, “What shape?”
She answered, “The shape of an ass that will carry two on its back, thou Perversity!”
Upon that, he cried, “O faithless woman, how long shall I be the slave of thy plotting? Now, but for that hair of my head, plucked by thy hand while I slept, I were free, no doer of thy tasks. Say, who be these that mark us?”

===Chinese===

她话音刚落，便抬头凝神侧耳倾听；她用亚麻布紧紧缠绕住自己脸部的上下两端，只露出双唇与双目；随后自水壶中取水，洒在自己的头顶、双臂与双足之上，口中念念有词。接着她第三次侧耳倾听，弯腰俯伏在地，以唇贴地，呼唤那个名字：“卡拉兹（Karaz）！”她接连高声呼唤了七次这个名号，其间时不时打着喷嚏。紧接着，仿佛是在应答她的召请，平地响起一阵低沉可怖的滚雷，整座宫殿剧烈摇晃、几近坍塌；殿内空气弥漫着浓烟，缭绕着滚滚烟雾。不多时，众人听见一阵阵猫叫声；地板上那块涂着红色药膏的地方骤然裂开，只见一只拱起脊背的黄褐皮毛野猫显现出来。努尔卡之子努尔娜见状厉声皱眉，对着野猫喝道：“这便是你的化身吗，卡拉兹；速速变换！这般模样派不上用场。”
野猫应声而变，化作一头闪烁着黄色凶光的金钱豹，伏地蓄势待扑。努尔卡之子努尔娜用力顿足，再度喝道：“这便是你的化身吗，卡拉兹；速速变换！这般模样派不上用场。”
金钱豹随即变换，化作一条盘绕层叠的长蛇，鳞片光滑，身躯蜿蜒盘曲，口吐毒信，嘶嘶作响。
努尔卡之子努尔娜勃然大怒道：“这便是你的化身吗，卡拉兹；速速变换！否则在易卜劣斯重获乐园恩准之前，你便永生永世以此模样苟活！”
长蛇瞬间消逝无踪。看哪！取而代之的是一尊相貌可怖的神怪，浑身漆黑如被雷殛焦枯的孤树；额头布满纵横交错的血红裂痕；须发与双耳赤红如火；一双怪眼犹如牧羊人用来捕狼的幽深陷坑，而坑底正卧着恶狼。他狂暴咆哮道：“你这该死的女叛徒，如今又要派我做什么苦差？”
努尔娜答道：“我需用你！”
他问：“要何模样？”
她答道：“化作一头足以承载二人骑乘的毛驴，你这冥顽不灵的顽孽！”
神怪闻言大吼道：“无信无义的妇人，我还要被你的阴谋诡计奴役到几时？若非你趁我熟睡之际拔下了我头顶的那根发丝，我早已是自由之身，何须为你效犬马之劳。快说，那两个打量着我们的厮鸟是何人？”

===Original===

She answered, “One, the Vizier Feshnavat; and one, Shibli Bagarag of Shiraz, he that’s destined to shave Shagpat, the son of Shimpoor, the son of Shoolpi, the son of Shullum; and the youth is my betrothed.”
Now, at her words the whole genie became as live coal with anger, and he panted black and bright, and made a stride toward Shibli Bagarag, and stretched his arm out to seize him; but Noorna blew quickly on the circles she had drawn, and the circles rose up in a white flame high as the heads of those present, and the genie shrank hastily back from the flame, and was seized with fits of sneezing. Then she said in scorn, “Easily, O Karaz, is a woman outwitted! Surely I could not guess what would be thy action! and I was wanting in foresight and insight! and I am a woman bearing the weight of my power as a woodman staggereth under the logs he hath felled!”
Now, at her words the whole genie became as live coal with anger, and he panted black and bright, and made a stride toward Shibli Bagarag, and stretched his arm out to seize him; but Noorna blew quickly on the circles she had drawn, and the circles rose up in a white flame high as the heads of those present, and the genie shrank hastily back from the flame, and was seized with fits of sneezing. Then she said in scorn, “Easily, O Karaz, is a woman outwitted! Surely I could not guess what would be thy action! and I was wanting in foresight and insight! and I am a woman bearing the weight of my power as a woodman staggereth under the logs he hath felled!”
So she taunted him, and he still sneezing and bent double with the might of the sneeze. Then said Noorna in a stern voice, “No more altercation between us! Wait thou here till I reappear, Karaz!”
Thereupon, she went from them; and the two, Feshnavat and Shibli Bagarag, feared greatly being left with the genie, for he became all colours, and loured on them each time that he ceased sneezing. He was clearly menacing them when Noorna returned, and in her hand a saddle made of hide, traced over with mystic characters and gold stripes.
So she cried, “Take this!” Then, seeing he hesitated, she unclosed from her left palm a powder, and scattered it over him; and he grew meek, and the bending knee of obedience was his, and he took the saddle. So she said, “ ’Tis well! Go now, and wait outside the city in the shape of an ass, with this saddle on thy back.”

===Chinese===

她答道：“一位是宰相费什纳瓦特；另一位是设拉子的希布利·巴加拉格，正是命中注定要剃光舒伦之子舒尔皮之子欣普尔之子沙格帕特之人；这少年乃是我的未婚夫。”
神怪听闻此言，浑身因狂怒而烧得如同一块滚烫的活炭，喘息间黑亮凶光闪烁，跨步上前直扑希布利·巴加拉格，伸出巨臂欲将他生擒；但努尔娜迅速吹了一口气拂过她画下的圆圈，圈子顿时升腾起一人多高的炽白烈焰，神怪见状慌忙倒退躲避烈火，紧接着连打喷嚏止不住。于是她轻蔑讥讽道：“女人当真容易被瞒过哄骗啊，卡拉兹！我怎会料不到你的一举一动？莫非我还欠缺远见与洞察？莫非我这弱女子扛不起自身的法力，恰似樵夫被自己砍倒的原木压得步履蹒跚不成？”
神怪听闻此言，浑身因狂怒而烧得如同一块滚烫的活炭，喘息间黑亮凶光闪烁，跨步上前直扑希布利·巴加拉格，伸出巨臂欲将他生擒；但努尔娜迅速吹了一口气拂过她画下的圆圈，圈子顿时升腾起一人多高的炽白烈焰，神怪见状慌忙倒退躲避烈火，紧接着连打喷嚏止不住。于是她轻蔑讥讽道：“女人当真容易被瞒过哄骗啊，卡拉兹！我怎会料不到你的一举一动？莫非我还欠缺远见与洞察？莫非我这弱女子扛不起自身的法力，恰似樵夫被自己砍倒的原木压得步履蹒跚不成？”
她这般连连出言讥诮，神怪仍在不住打喷嚏，整个人被剧烈的喷嚏折腾得弯腰躬身。接着努尔娜严厉呵斥道：“休要再多费唇舌！在此静候，待我回来，卡拉兹！”
说罢，她转身离去；费什纳瓦特与希布利·巴加拉格同这尊神怪独处，心中惊惧万分，只见他通体变幻出万道凶光，每打完一个喷嚏便狠狠怒视二人。正当他明显目露杀机、虎视眈眈之际，努尔娜回到了殿中，手里托着一具由兽皮制成的鞍辔，上面描绘着神秘的符文与道道金纹。
她喝道：“接下此物！”见他犹豫不决，她摊开左掌，将掌心中的一撮药粉洒在他身上；神怪顿时变得驯服温顺，曲膝下跪遵从法旨，伸手接过了鞍辔。于是她道：“甚好！如今便速去城外等候，化作毛驴模样，背上披挂此鞍。”

===Original===

The genie groaned, and said, “To hear is to obey!” And he departed with those words, for she held him in bondage. Then she calmed down the white flames of the circles that enclosed Shibli Bagarag and the Vizier Feshnavat, and they stepped forth, marvelling at the greatness of her sorceries that held such a genie in bondage.

===Chinese===

神怪哀叹一声，道：“谨遵法旨！”说完便奉命离去，盖因他受制于她的法力羁绊。随后努尔娜平息了笼罩在希布利·巴加拉格与宰相费什纳瓦特身周的白色火圈，二人迈步走出，无不惊叹于她通天彻地的巫术，竟能将这等凶顽的神怪死死禁锢掌控。

## The Horse Garraveen / 神驹加拉文
===Original===

Now, they descended leisurely the slopes of the mountain, and when they were again in the green of its base, Noorna called to the ass, “Ho! Karaz! Sniff now the breezes, for the end of our journey by night is the meadows of Melistan. Forward in thy might, and bray not when we are in them, for thy comfort’s sake!”
The ass sniffed, turning to the four quarters, and chose a certain direction, and bore them swiftly over hills and streams eddying in silver; over huge mounds of sand, where the tents of Bedouins stood in white clusters; over lakes smooth as the cheeks of sleeping loveliness; by walls of cities, mosques, and palaces; under towers that rose as an armed man with the steel on his brows and the frown of battle; by the shores of the pale foaming sea it bore them, going at a pace that the Arab on his steed outstrippeth not. So when the sun was red and the dews were blushing with new light, they struggled from a wilderness of barren broken ground, and saw beneath them, in the warm beams, green, peaceful, deep, the meadows of Melistan. They were meadows dancing with flowers, as it had been fresh damsels of the mountain, fair with variety of colours that were so many gleams of changing light as the breezes of the morn swept over them; lavish of hues, of sweetness, of pleasantness, fir for the souls of the blest.
Then, after they had gazed awhile, Noorna bin Noorka said, “In these meadows the horse Garraveen roameth at will. Heroes of bliss bestride him on great days. He is black to look on; speed quivers in his flanks like the lightning; his nostrils are wide with flame; there is that in his eye which is settled fire, and that in his hoofs which is ready thunder; when he paws the Earth kingdoms quake: no animal liveth with blood like the horse Garraveen. He is under a curse, for that he bore on his back one who defied the Prophet. Now, to make him come to thee thou must blow the call of battle, and to catch him thou must contrive to strike him on the fetlock as he runs with this musk-ball which I give thee; and to tame him thou must trace between his eyes a figure or the crescent with thy forenail. When that is done, bring him to me here, where I await thee, and I will advise thee further.”
So she said, “Go!”

===Chinese===

他们从容不迫地下了山坡，当再次踏上山脚的青葱草木时，努尔娜呼唤毛驴道：“喂！卡拉兹！快嗅嗅风向，我们今夜行程的终点便是梅利斯坦（Melistan）草场。使出你的全部气力前行，到了草场切勿乱叫，这可是为了你自己的安危着想！”
毛驴四面嗅了嗅，选定了一个方向，驮着他们迅疾飞奔，穿过重重山峦与银波荡漾的溪流；掠过浩瀚的沙丘，那里的贝都因人帐篷宛如一簇簇白云；越过平滑如沉睡佳人脸颊的湖泊；掠过城墙、清真寺与巍峨宫殿；穿过那宛如顶盔贯甲、怒容满面的披甲武士般高耸的塔楼；沿着翻卷着惨白浪花的海岸疾驰，速度之快，纵是策马疾驰的阿拉伯骑士也难以企及。当日轮泛红、晨露在初升曙光中泛起羞红之色时，他们终于穿过了一片崎岖荒凉的荒野，只见在他们脚下的温暖晨曦中，苍翠、静谧、深邃的梅利斯坦草场展现在眼前。那片草场上百花摇曳起舞，宛如山野间亭亭玉立的娇俏少女，晨风轻拂而过，万紫千红变幻出道道流光；那里芬芳馥郁、令人心旷神怡，端的是供蒙福之魂憩息的福地。
凝神观望片刻后，努尔卡之子努尔娜（Noorna bin Noorka）说道：“神驹加拉文（Garraveen）便在这片草场上自由游荡。唯有得享极乐的盖世豪杰方能在重大吉日跨上它的马背。它通体漆黑如墨；疾风般的速度宛如雷电在它两侧肋部颤动；它的鼻孔喷吐着熊熊烈焰；它的眸中燃烧着永不熄灭的炽火，蹄下蕴藏着蓄势待发的滚滚天雷；当它蹄踏大地之时，列国皆为之震颤：世间万兽绝无任何生灵能拥有神驹加拉文这般高贵的血统。它身受诅咒，盖因它曾驮负过一位胆敢违抗先知之人。如今，若要引它来到你身旁，你必须吹响出征的战阵号令；若要将它擒获，你必须在它飞奔之际设法用我赐予你的这枚麝香丸击中它的后蹄髢；若要将它驯服，你必须用食指指甲在它的双眼之间勾勒出一弯新月的印记。待功成之后，将它牵至我在此守候之处，我自会进一步指点于你。”
说罢，她道：“去吧！”

===Original===

and Shibli Bagarag showed her the breadth of his shoulders, and stepped briskly toward the meadows, and was soon brushing among the flowers and soft mosses of the meadows, lifting his nostrils to the joyful smells, looking about him with the broad eye of one that hungereth for a coming thing. The birds went up above him, and the trees shook and sparkled, and the waters of brooks and broad rivers flashed like waving mirrors waved by the slave-girls in sport when the beauties of the harem riot and dip their gleaming shoulders in the bath. He wandered on, lost in the gladness that lived, till the loud neigh of a steed startled him, and by the banks of a river before him he beheld the horse Garraveen stooping to drink of the river; glorious was the look of the creature﻿—silver-hoofed, fashioned in the curves of beauty and swiftness. So Shibli Bagarag put up his two hands and blew the call of battle, and the horse Garraveen arched his neck at the call, and swung upon his haunches, and sought the call, answering it, and tossing his mane as he advanced swiftly. Then, as he neared, Shibli Bagarag held the musk-ball in his fingers, and aimed at the fetlock of the horse Garraveen, and flung it, and struck him so that he stumbled and fell. He snorted fiercely as he bent to the grass, but Shibli Bagarag ran to him, and grasped strongly the tuft of hair hanging forward between his ears, and traced between his fine eyes a figure of the crescent with his forenail, and the horse ceased plunging, and was gentle as a colt by its mother’s side, and suffered Shibli Bagarag to bestride him, and spurn him with his heel to speed, and bore him fleetly across the fair length of the golden meadows to where Noorna bin Noorka sat awaiting him. She uttered a cry of welcome, saying, “This is achieved with diligence and skill, O my betrothed! and on thy right wrist I mark strength like a sleeping leopard, and the children of Aklis will not resist thee.”
So she bade him alight from the horse, but he said, “Nay.” And she called to him again to alight, but he cried, “I will not alight from him! By Allah! such a bounding wave of bliss have I never yet had beneath me, and I will give him rein once again; as the poet says:

> “ ‘Divinely rings the rushing air

When I am on my mettled mare:

When fast along the plains we fly,

A creature of the heavens am I.’ ”

===Chinese===

希布利·巴加拉格（Shibli Bagarag）向她展现了宽阔健硕的背影，迈着矫健的步伐朝草场走去，不多时便在草场的繁花与柔软苔藓间穿行，他仰起鼻息深吸着令人愉悦的芳香，放眼四顾，目光宛若一位渴盼着宿命降临的勇士。群鸟在他头顶盘旋，树木婆娑闪烁，溪流与宽阔江河的水面波光粼粼，恰若后宫佳丽嬉戏沐浴、浸润晶莹香肩时女奴手中挥舞翻动的明镜。他一路漫步前行，沉醉在蓬勃生机之中，忽听一声高亢的战马嘶鸣令他心头一惊；在前方的一条河畔，他看见神驹加拉文正俯身在河边饮水；那生灵的英姿端的是神武非凡——生就银蹄，身形勾勒出集优美与迅疾于一身的绝妙线条。于是希布利·巴加拉格合拢双掌吹响了出征的战阵号令，神驹加拉文闻声昂起马颈，后蹄发力调转马身，循着号令长嘶应答，甩动鬃毛疾驰而来。待它靠近之际，希布利·巴加拉格指尖捏紧麝香丸，瞄准神驹加拉文的蹄髢猛掷过去，正中目标，打得神驹踉跄摔倒在地。它扑倒在草丛中狂暴地打着响鼻，但希布利·巴加拉格飞奔上前，一把死死抓住它双耳间向前垂落的马鬃，用食指指甲在它俊美的双眼之间划出一道新月印记；神驹顿时不再挣扎暴跳，变得宛如依偎在母马身旁的小马驹般温顺驯服，任由希布利·巴加拉格跨上马背，以脚后跟策马加速，载着他轻快飞驰过广袤辽阔的金黄草场，直奔努尔卡之子努尔娜端坐守候之处。她发出一声欢呼相迎，道：“我的未婚夫啊，此事办得何等敏捷利落！我在你的右手腕上看到了一股宛若沉睡金钱豹般的磅礴神力，阿克利斯（Aklis）的子嗣定无法抗拒于你。”
于是她命他下马，他却道：“不。”她再次呼唤他下马，他却高喊道：“我绝不下马！凭安拉起誓！我还从未体会过胯下这般如狂涛奔涌般的极乐，我定要再纵马狂奔一回；正如诗人所言：

> ‘当我跨上烈性的雌马，
> 扑面的疾风奏响神圣的乐章；
> 当我们在平原上风驰电掣，
> 我便化作了九天之上的神明。’

===Original===

Then she levelled her brows at him, and said gravely, “This is the temptation thou art falling into, as have thousands before thy time. Give him the rein a second time, and he will bear thee to the red pit, and halt upon the brink, and pitch thee into it among bleeding masses and skeletons of thy kind, where they lie who were men like to thee, and were borne away by the horse Garraveen.”
He gave no heed to her words, taunting her, and making the animal prance up and prove its spirit.
And she cried reproachfully, “O fool! is it thus our great aim will be defeated by thy silly conceit? Lo, now, the greatness and the happiness thou art losing for this idle vanity is to be as a dunghill cock matched with an ostrich; and think not to escape the calamities thou bringest on thyself, for as is said,

> “No runner can outstrip his fate;

===Chinese===

她登时双眉紧蹙，严肃地告诫他道：“这正是你深陷其中的诱惑，在你之前已有千万人重蹈覆辙。若你再次放缰任它狂奔，它便会将你驮往那口猩红魔坑，骤停在悬崖之巅，将你狠狠抛入坑底的血肉残骸与累累白骨之中，那些人曾与你一般是血肉之躯，皆因被神驹加拉文裹挟狂奔而葬身于此。”
他却对她的劝诫充耳不闻，出言戏谑，甚至故意催动坐骑昂首腾跃以炫耀神骏的烈性。
她痛心斥责道：“愚蠢之徒！难道我们的大业竟要因你这愚妄的自大而功亏一篑吗？看哪，为了这等虚幻的虚荣，你所抛弃的伟业与福祉，恰似将草堆上的笨雄鸡去同鸵鸟相比；休想逃脱你自招的灾祸，正如古语所云：

> ‘凡奔者皆难逃命运的追赶；

===Original===

“and it will overtake thee, though thou part like an arrow from the bow.”
He still made a jest of her remonstrance, trying the temper of the animal, and rejoicing in its dark flushes of ireful vigour.
And she cried out furiously, “How! art thou past counsel? then will we match strength with strength ere ’tis too late, though it weaken both.”
Upon that, she turned quickly to the ass and stroked it from one extremity to the other, crying, “Karaz! Karaz!” shouting, “Come forth in thy power!” And the ass vanished, and the genie stood in his place, tall, dark, terrible as a pillar of storm to travellers ranging the desert. He exclaimed, “What is it, O woman? Charge me with thy command!”
And she said, “Wrestle with him thou seest on the horse Garraveen, and fling him from his seat.”
Then he yelled a glad yell, and stooped to Shibli Bagarag on the horse and enveloped him, and seized him, and plucked him from the horse, and whirled him round, and flung him off. The youth went circling in the air, high in it, and descended, circling, at a distance in the deep meadow-waters. When he crept up the banks he saw the genie astride the horse Garraveen, with a black flame round his head; and the genie urged him to speed and put him to the gallop, and was soon lost to sight, as he had been a thunderbeam passing over a still lake at midnight. And Shibli Bagarag was smitten with the wrong and the folly of his act, and sought to hide his sight from Noorna; but she called to him, “Look up, O youth! and face the calamity. Lo, we have now lost the service of Karaz! for though I utter ten spells and one spell in a breath, the horse Garraveen will ere that have stretched beyond the circle of my magic, and the genie will be free to do his ill deeds and plot against us. Sad is it! but profit thou by a knowledge of thy weakness.”
Then said she, “See, I have not failed to possess myself of the three hairs of Garraveen, and there is that to rejoice in.”
She displayed them, and they were sapphire hairs, and had a flickering light; and they seemed to live, wriggling their lengths, and were as snakes with sapphire skins. Then she said, “Thy right wrist, O my betrothed!”
He gave her his right wrist, and she tied round it the three hairs of Garraveen, exclaiming, “Thus do skilful carpenters make stronger what has broken and indicated disaster. Surely, I confide in thy star?

===Chinese===

“纵如离弦之箭疾射，它亦必将你追上。”
他依旧拿她的苦劝当儿戏，不断试探马匹的烈性，为它身上散发出的漆黑凶悍神威欢欣不已。
她勃然大怒斥道：“怎么！你竟已不可救药？那我们便唯有趁一切未晚之时以力相搏，哪怕此举会两败俱伤！”
说罢，她猛然转向那头毛驴，从头到尾抚摸了一遍，高呼：“卡拉兹！卡拉兹！”厉声喝道：“显现你的真身威能！”毛驴瞬间消逝，神怪赫然挺立原地，高大、阴森、可怖，宛如穿行在大漠中的旅人所遭遇的暴风沙柱。他厉声问道：“妇人，有何差遣？速发号施令！”
她道：“去同那跨在神驹加拉文背上的人角力，将他从马背上掀翻下来！”
神怪发出一声快意的狂呼，扑向马背上的希布利·巴加拉格将他死死笼罩，一把抓住他，将他从马鞍上生生拔起，在半空猛烈旋转，狠狠甩了出去。少年在半空高高打着旋飞出，旋转着跌落到远处草场深邃的河水之中。待他爬上岸边时，只见神怪已跨骑在神驹加拉文背上，头顶环绕着一圈黑色的烈焰；神怪不断策马疾驰，催动战马狂奔，转瞬便消失在视野之中，恰若子夜时分掠过沉寂湖面的一道霹雳雷光。希布利·巴加拉格深知自己的狂妄过错与愚行，羞愧得不敢直视努尔娜；她却呼唤他道：“抬起头来，少年！勇于直面这场灾祸。看哪，我们如今失去了卡拉兹的效力！即便我一口气能念诵十一道咒语，神驹加拉文此刻也早已飞奔出了我的法力结界，神怪将重获自由为非作歹，暗算谋害我们。委实可悲！但愿你能认清自身的软弱并引以为戒。”
随后她道：“瞧，我并未失手，已取下了加拉文的三根鬃毛，这倒是一件值得庆幸之事。”
她展示出那三根鬃毛，它们通体呈现出蓝宝石般的光泽，闪烁着微弱的幽光；它们仿佛具有生命一般蠕动伸缩，宛如披着蓝宝石鳞皮的细蛇。接着她道：“伸出你的右手腕，我的未婚夫！”
他递过右手腕，她将加拉文的三根鬃毛紧紧系在他的手腕上，高呼道：“能工巧匠正是如此加固断裂之处、化解凶兆。莫非我当信奉你的吉星，

===Original===

I have faith in my foresight?”
And she cried, “Eyes of mine, what sayest thou to me? Lo, we must part awhile: it is written.”
Said he, “Leave me not, my betrothed: what am I without thy counsel? And go not from me, or this adventure will come to miserable issue.”
So she said, “Thou beginnest to feel my worth?”
He answered, “O Noorna! was woman like thee before in this world? Surely ’tis a mask I mark thee under; yet art thou perforce of sheer wisdom and sweet manners lovely in my sight; and I have a thirst to hear thee and look on thee.”
While he spake, a beam of struggling splendour burst from her, and she said, “O thou dear youth, yes! I must even go. But I go glad of heart, knowing thee prepared to love me. I must go to counteract the machinations of Karaz, for he’s at once busy, vindictive, and cunning, and there’s no time for us to lose; so farewell, my betrothed, and make thy wits keen to know me when we next meet.”
So he said, “And I﻿—whither go I?”
She answered, “To the City of Oolb straightway.”
Then he, “But I know not its bearing from this spot: how reach it?”
She answered, “What! thou with the phial of Paravid in thy vest, that endoweth, a single drop of it, the flowers, the herbage, the very stones and desert sands, with a tongue to articulate intelligible talk?”
Said he, “Is it so?”
She answered, “Even so.”
Ere Shibli Bagarag could question her further she embraced him, and blew upon his eyes, and he was blinded by her breath, and saw not her departure, groping for a seat on the rocks, and thinking her still by him. Sight returned not to him till long after weariness had brought the balm of sleep upon his eyelids.

===Chinese===

而对自己的先见深信不疑？”
她又喊道：“我的心上人啊，你意下如何？看哪，我们必须暂别片刻：宿命如此注定。”
他道：“休要离开我，我的未婚妻：若无你的指点，我算得了什么？切莫弃我而去，否则这场远征必将落得凄惨收场。”
她问道：“你终于开始体会到我的宝贵之处了？”
他答道：“努尔娜！世间可曾有过你这般的奇女子？我虽看出你戴着假面，但你通天的睿智与温婉的举止在我眼中无比可爱动人；我渴望聆听你的声音，渴望凝望你的容颜。”
正当他说话之际，一道挣扎迸射的璀璨光华自她周身绽放开来，她说道：“亲爱的少年，正是如此！但我必须离去了。不过我离去时满心欢喜，因我知道你已做好了爱我的准备。我必须前去粉碎卡拉兹的阴谋诡计，皆因他既手脚勤快、又睚眦必报且狡黠过人，我们刻不容缓；别了，我的未婚夫，下一次相见之时，务必擦亮你的双眼认出我来。”
他问道：“那么我呢——我该往何处去？”
她答道：“径直前往乌尔布（Oolb）城。”
他又问：“可我不知它位于何方：又该如何抵达？”
她答道：“怎么！你怀中揣着帕拉维德之水（Paravid）的小药瓶，只需滴下一滴，便能赋予百花、草木、乃至顽石与荒漠黄沙以人言，口吐清晰通晓的话语，你难道忘了？”
他问：“当真如此神奇？”
她答道：“千真万确。”
未等希布利·巴加拉格再多加询问，她便上前拥抱了他，向着他的双眼吹了一口气；他被她的鼻息迷住了双眼，并未看清她是如何离去的，只得在礁石上摸索着坐下，以为她仍依偎在身旁。直到长久的疲惫为他的眼睑带来甜美安眠的抚慰许久之后，他的视力才重新恢复。

## The Lily of the Enchanted Sea / 迷境之海的百合
===Original===

Now, after the cockleshell had skimmed calmly awhile, it began to pitch and grew unquiet, and came upon a surging foam, pale, and with scintillating bubbles. The surges increased in volume, and boiled, hissing as with anger, like savage animals. Presently, the cockleshell rose upon one very lofty swell, and Shibli Bagarag lost hold of it, and lo! it was overturned and engulfed in the descent of the great mountain of water, and the Princess Goorelka was immersed in the depths. She would have sunk, but Shibli Bagarag caught hold of her, and supported her to the shore by the strength of his right arm. The shore was one of sand and shells, their wet cheeks sparkling in the moonlight; over it hung a promontory, a huge jut of black rock. Now, the Princess when she landed, seeing not him that supported her, delayed not to run beneath the rock, and ascended by steps cut from the base of the rock. And Shibli Bagarag followed her by winding paths round the rock, till she came to the highest peak commanding the circle of the Enchanted Sea, and glimpses of enthralled vessels, and mariners bewitched on board; long paths of starlight rippled into the distant gloom, and the reflection of the moon opposite was as a wide nuptial sheet of silver on the waters: islands, green and white, and with soft music floating from their foliage, sailed slowly to and fro. Surely, to dwell reclining among the slopes of those islands a man would forfeit Paradise! Now, the Princess, as she stood upon the peak, knew that she was not alone, and pretended to slip from her footing, and Shibli Bagarag called out and ran to her; but she turned in the direction of his voice and laughed, and he knew he was outwitted. Then, to deceive her, he dropped from the phial twenty drops round her on the rock, and those twenty drops became twenty voices, so that she was bewildered with their calls, and stopped her ears, and ran from them, and descended from the eminence nimbly, slipping over ledges and leaping the abysses. And Shibli Bagarag followed her, clutching at the trailers and tearing them with him, letting loose a torrent of stones and earth, till on a sudden they stood together above a greenswarded basin of the rock opening to the sea; and in the middle of the basin, lo!

===Chinese===

贝壳舟平稳滑行了片刻之后，便开始上下颠簸、剧烈摇晃起来，迎面遇上了一片泛着惨白泡沫、翻滚着闪烁水泡的汹涌浪涛。狂涛越聚越高，如野兽般暴怒咆哮、嘶嘶沸腾。不多时，贝壳舟被一道万丈巨浪高高托起，希布利·巴加拉格（Shibli Bagarag）手上一滑失了抓握，看哪！贝壳舟在那座水山崩塌之际被彻底掀翻吞没，古雷尔卡公主（Princess Goorelka）跌入了深渊。她本会沉入海底，但希布利·巴加拉格一把抓住了她，凭着右臂的神力护送她游上了岸。岸边铺满了细沙与贝壳，湿漉漉的贝壳在月光下闪闪发亮；海岸上方耸立着一处海角，乃是一块巨大的黑色突岩。公主登岸之后，并未看清托举她的人是谁，急忙奔到岩石下方，顺着从岩基上凿出的石阶快步攀登。希布利·巴加拉格循着环绕岩石的蜿蜒小径紧随其后，直至她登上最高峰，俯瞰整座迷境之海（the Enchanted Sea）的万顷波涛，隐约可见被施了法术的船只，以及甲板上中邪着魔的水手；长长的星光轨迹在远方的昏暗中泛起涟漪，对岸倒映的明月犹如铺展在水面上的宽大银色婚毯；青翠与皎白的岛屿在水面上缓缓漂浮巡游，枝叶间飘荡着悠扬轻柔的仙乐。凡人若能斜倚在那岛屿的山坡上安居，哪怕放弃乐园也在所不惜！此时，站在峰顶的公主察觉到自己身旁并非空无一人，便假装脚下一滑，希布利·巴加拉格忍不住惊呼出声奔上前去；她却循着声音的方向转过身来放声大笑，他这才明白自己中了圈套。为了迷惑她，他从小药瓶中倒出二十滴药水洒在她周遭的岩石上，那二十滴药水顿时化作二十道呼唤的声音，将她搅得心烦意乱，她连忙捂住双耳仓皇逃窜，敏捷地从高处奔下，滑过一道道岩壁，跃过深渊沟壑。希布利·巴加拉格一路紧随，抓扯着藤蔓顺势下滑，带落下一片滚滚碎石与泥土，直至二人猛然伫立在面朝大海的一处生满绿草的岩石洼地之上；在那洼地的中央，瞧啊！

===Original===

in stature like a maiden of the mountains, and one that droopeth her head pensively thinking of her absent lover, the Enchanted Lily. Wonder knocked at the breast of Shibli Bagarag when he saw that queenly flower waving its illumined head to the breeze: he could not retain a cry of rapture. As he did this the Princess stretched her hand to where he was and groped a moment, and caught him by the silken dress and tore in it a great rent, and by the rent he stood revealed to her. Then said she, “O youth, thou halt done ill to follow me here, and the danger of it is past computing; surely, the motive was a deep one, nought other than the love of me.”
She spoke winningly, sweet words to a luted voice, and the youth fell upon his knees before her, smitten by her beauty; and he said, “I followed thee here as I would follow such loveliness to the gates of doom, O Princess of Oolb.”
She smiled and said playfully, “I will read by thy hand whether thou be one faithful in love.”
She took his hand and sprinkled on it earth and gravel, and commenced scanning it curiously. As she scanned it her forehead wrinkled up, and a shot like black lightning travelled across her countenance, withering its beauty: she cried in a forced voice, “Aha! it is well, O youth, for thee and for me that thou lovest me, and art faithful in love.”
The look of the Princess of Oolb and her voice affrighted the soul of Shibli Bagarag, and he would have turned from her; but she held him, and went to the Lily, and emptied into the palm of her hand the dew that was in the Lily, and raised it to the lips of Shibli Bagarag, bidding him drink as a pledge for her sake and her love, and to appease his thirst. As he was about to drink, there fell into the palm of the Princess from above what seemed a bolt of storm scattering the dew; and after he had blinked with the suddenness of the action he looked and beheld the hawk, its red eyes inflamed with wrath. And the hawk screamed into the ear of Shibli Bagarag, “Pluck up the Lily ere it is too late, O fool!﻿—the dew was poison! Pluck it by the root with thy right hand!”
So thereat he strode to the Lily, and grasped it, and pulled with his strength; and the Lily was loosened, and yielded, and came forth streaming with blood from the bulb of the root; surely the bulb of the root was a palpitating heart, yet warm, even as that we have within our bosoms.

===Chinese===

宛若山野少女般婷婷玉立、又似因思念远方情郎而含羞垂首的，正是迷境百合（the Enchanted Lily）。希布利·巴加拉格目睹那朵华贵如女王般的仙花在微风中摇曳生姿、散发着幽幽莹光，心中惊叹莫名：他忍不住发出一声狂喜的赞叹。就在这一瞬，公主向他所在之处探出手来摸索了片刻，一把揪住了他的丝袍，撕开了一道大口子，顺着那道裂口，他整个人赫然暴露在她眼前。于是她道：“少年啊，你一路尾随我至此委实不智，此地的凶险难以估量；想来你的动机必深沉至极，除却对我的倾心爱慕，绝无他由。”
她语调轻柔妩媚，宛如鲁特琴伴奏下的甜言蜜语，少年被她的绝色所倾倒，不由自主双膝跪倒在她面前；他道：“我尾随你至此，正如我甘愿追随这般倾城绝色直至毁灭之门，乌尔布公主（Princess of Oolb）啊。”
她嫣然一笑，娇嗔道：“且让我看你的手相，瞧瞧你是否是个对情爱坚贞不渝之人。”
她执起他的手，在上面洒下些许泥土与碎石，开始好奇地细细端详。端详之际，她的额头渐渐聚起褶皱，一道宛若漆黑霹雳般的戾气自她面庞上一闪而过，刹那间令她的美貌凋零：她勉强挤出一丝声音尖叫道：“啊哈！少年啊，你既然爱我且坚贞不渝，这对你我皆是天大的幸事。”
乌尔布公主的神色与嗓音令希布利·巴加拉格毛骨悚然，他本欲转身离她而去；她却死死拉住他，走向那朵百合，将花蕊中的露珠倾倒在自己的掌心之中，举到希布利·巴加拉格唇边，命他满饮此露，作为见证她情意的信物，并借以解渴。正当他欲饮之际，空中猛然坠落下一团如暴风霹雳般的事物，正中公主的掌心，将露水震得四散飞溅；待他因这突如其来的变故眨了眨眼回过神来，定睛一看，只见一只苍鹰赫然在前，一双赤红的鹰眼燃烧着滔天怒火。苍鹰向着希布利·巴加拉格耳畔厉声尖叫：“趁一切未晚速速拔起百合，蠢材！——那露水乃是剧毒！用你的右手抓住根部将它拔起！”
闻听此言，他跨步上前抓住百合，运足全身神力猛力一拔；百合顿时松动脱落，自根部的球茎中汩汩流淌出鲜血；那根部的球茎分明是一颗仍在跳动、尚带余温的心脏，恰如我们胸膛中跳动的心脏毫无二致。

===Original===

Now, from the terror of that sight the Princess hid her eyes, and shrank away. And the lines of malice, avarice, and envy seemed ageing her at every breath. Then the hawk pecked at her three pecks, and perched on a corner of rock, and called shrilly the name “Karaz!” And the genie Karaz came slanting down the night air, like a preying bird, and stood among them. So the hawk cried, “See, O Karaz, the freshness of thy Princess of Oolb”; and the genie regarded her till loathing curled his lip, for she grew in ghastliness to the colour of a frog, and a frog’s face was hers, a camel’s back, a pelican’s throat, the legs of a peacock.
Then the hawk cried, “Is this how ye meet, ye lovers﻿—ye that will be wedded?” And the hawk made his tongue as a thorn to them. At the last it exclaimed, “Now let us fight our battle, Karaz!”
But the genie said, “Nay, there will come a time for that, traitress!”
The hawk cried, “Thou delayest, till the phial of Paravid, the hairs of Garraveen, and this Lily, my three helps, are expended, thinking Aklis, for which we barter them, striketh but a single blow? That is well! Go, then, and take thy Princess, and obtain permission of the King of Oolb, her father, to wed her, O Karaz!”
The hawk whistled with laughter, and the genie was stung with its mockeries, and clutched the Princess of Oolb in a bunch, and arose from the ground with her, slanting up the night-air like fire, till he was seen high up even as an angry star reddening the seas beneath.
When he was lost to the eye, Shibli Bagarag drew a long breath and cried aloud, “The likeness of that Princess of Oolb in her ugliness to Noorna, my betrothed, is a thing marvellous, if it be not she herself.” And he reflected, “Yet she seemed not to recognize and claim me”; and thought, “I am bound to her by gratitude, and I should have rescued her from Karaz, but I know not if it be she. *Wullahy!* I am bewildered; I will ask counsel of the hawk.” He looked to the corner of the rock where the hawk had perched, but the hawk was gone; as he searched for it, his eyes fell upon the bed of earth where the Lily stood ere he plucked it, and lo!

===Chinese===

目睹这惊悚一幕，公主吓得捂住双眼，连连退缩。恶毒、贪婪与嫉妒的道道沟壑仿佛随着每一次呼吸令她急剧衰老。此时苍鹰朝她狠狠啄了三下，随后栖息在岩石一角，厉声尖啸呼唤那个名字：“卡拉兹（Karaz）！”神怪卡拉兹如猛禽俯冲般穿透夜色斜掠而下，落在大伙儿身旁。苍鹰喊道：“瞧啊，卡拉兹，这就是你那位娇嫩欲滴的乌尔布公主；”神怪端详着她，厌恶得撇起了嘴唇，因她已变得面目可怖如青蛙的皮色，生就一张青蛙面孔、一副骆驼驼背、一条鹈鹕喉囊，以及一双孔雀般的细腿。
苍鹰讥笑道：“莫非这就是你们相会之态，两位情郎佳人——即将拜堂成亲的二位？”苍鹰句句话如针尖般刺痛着他们。最后它厉声道：“如今便让我们决一死战吧，卡拉兹！”
神怪却道：“不，来日方长，你这女叛徒！”
苍鹰高呼：“你一再拖延，莫不是以为等耗尽了帕拉维德（Paravid）药瓶、加拉文（Garraveen）的鬃毛与这朵百合这三样法宝，我们用以换取神剑的阿克利斯（Aklis）便只能挥出一击？甚好！那么便去吧，带上你的公主，去求得她父王乌尔布国王的恩准同她完婚吧，卡拉兹！”
苍鹰发出阵阵刺耳的嘲弄笑声，神怪被嘲讽得羞怒交加，一把将乌尔布公主抓成一团，带着她腾空而起，如一团烈火般斜刺入夜空，直至高高升上天际，宛若一颗愤怒的赤星映红了下方的海面。
待他消失在视野之外，希布利·巴加拉格长舒了一口气，失声喊道：“那乌尔布公主丑陋的模样与我的未婚妻努尔娜何其相似，委实令人称奇，若不是她本人又会是谁？”他又思忖：“可她似乎并未认出我、同我相认；”心想：“我受她的大恩，理当将她自卡拉兹手中救出，可我终究拿不准那是否就是她。凭安拉起誓（Wullahy）！我心乱如麻；我且去向苍鹰请教。”他望向苍鹰先前栖息的岩石角落，却见苍鹰早已不知所踪；四下搜寻之际，他的目光落在了拔起百合前的那片泥土上，看哪！

===Original===

in the place of the Lily, there was a damsel dressed in white shining silks, fairer than the enchanted flower, straighter than the stalk of it; her head slightly drooping, like the moon on a border of the night; her bosom like the swell of the sea in moonlight; her eyes dark, under a low arch of darker lashes, like stars on the skirts of storm; and she was the very dream of loveliness, formed to freeze with awe, and to inflame with passion. So Shibli Bagarag gazed at her with adoration, his hands stretched halfway to her as if to clasp her, fearing she was a vision and would fade; and the damsel smiled a sweet smile, and lifted her antelope eyes, and said, “Who am I, and to whom might I be likened, O youth?”
And he answered, “Who thou art, O young perfection, I know not, if not a Houri of Paradise; but thou art like the Princess of Oolb, yet lovelier, oh lovelier! And thy voice is the voice of Noorna, my betrothed; yet purer, sweeter, younger.”
So the damsel laughed a laugh like a sudden sweeping of wild chords of music, and said, “O youth, saw’st thou not the ascent of Noorna, thy betrothed, gathered in a bunch by Karaz?”
And he answered, “I saw her; but I knew not, O damsel of beauty; surely I was bewildered, amazed, without power to contend with the genie.”
Then she said, “Wouldst thou release her? So kiss me on the lips, on the eyes, and on the forehead, three kisses each time; and with the first say, ‘By the well of Paravid’; and with the second, ‘By the strength of Garraveen!’ and with the third, ‘By the Lily of the Sea!’ ”
Now, the heart of the youth bounded at her words, and he went to her, and trembling kissed her all bashfully on the lips, on the eyes, and on the forehead, saying each time as she directed. Then she took him by the hand, and stepped from the bed of earth, crying joyfully, “Thanks be to Allah and the Prophet! Noorna, is released from the sorceries that held her, and powerful.”
So, while he was wondering, she said, “Knowest thou not the woman, thy betrothed?”
He answered, “O damsel of beauty, I am charged with many feelings; doubts and hopes are mixed in me. Say first who thou art, and fill my two ears with bliss.”

===Chinese===

在原本生长百合之处，赫然伫立着一位身披洁白莹亮丝绸的少女，比那迷境仙花更娇艳动人，身段比那花茎更挺拔婀娜；她的螓首微垂，宛若斜倚在夜空边际的明月；她的酥胸如月光下起伏的海波；深邃的明眸隐于浓密睫毛构筑的纤细眼睑之下，恰似暴风雨边缘闪烁的星辰；她美得宛若梦幻中的造物，既教人敬畏屏息，又教人情焰炽燃。希布利·巴加拉格满怀倾慕地凝视着她，双手半伸欲去拥抱，却又唯恐这是一场转瞬即逝的虚妄幻影；少女嫣然一笑，抬起她那宛若羚羊般清澈的双眸，说道：“我是何人，我又与何人相仿，少年啊？”
他答道：“若非乐园中的仙女（Houri），我委实不知你是何方神圣，完美无瑕的少女啊；但你神似乌尔布公主，却比她更美，噢，更美千百倍！而你的嗓音分明是我未婚妻努尔娜的嗓音，却比她更纯澈、更甜美、更青春韶华。”
少女闻言发出一阵宛若骤然扫过旷野琴弦般的悦耳笑声，说道：“少年啊，难道你方才未曾见到你的未婚妻努尔娜被卡拉兹抓成一团掠上苍穹吗？”
他答道：“我见到了；可我并不知道真相，绝美动人的少女啊；我方才着实心神大乱、惊愕万分，根本无力同那神怪抗衡。”
接着她道：“你可愿将她解救出来？那就请亲吻我的双唇、双眸与额头，每一处皆吻三记；第一吻念诵：‘凭帕拉维德之井起誓’；第二吻念诵：‘凭加拉文之神力起誓！’第三吻念诵：‘凭迷境之海的百合起誓！’”
少年闻言心跳如擂鼓，他走向她，羞怯颤抖着依次亲吻了她的双唇、双眸与额头，每一次皆依照她的吩咐念诵誓言。随后她牵起他的手，迈步走下泥土花坛，欣喜高呼道：“感谢安拉与先知！努尔娜已从束缚她的巫术中彻底挣脱，重获通天神力！”
正当他惊疑不定之际，她问道：“你难道认不出你的未婚妻了吗？”
他答道：“绝色佳人啊，我胸中百感交集；疑虑与希冀交织难分。请先告诉我你究竟是谁，让无上幸福充盈我的双耳吧。”

===Original===

And she said, “I will leave my name to other lips; surely I am the daughter of the Vizier Feshnavat, betrothed to a wandering youth﻿—a barber, who sickened at the betrothal, and consoled himself with a proverb when he gave me the kiss of contract, and knew not how with truth to pay me a compliment.”
Now, Shibli Bagarag saw this was indeed Noorna bin Noorka, his betrothed, and he fell before her in love and astonishment; but she lifted him to her neck, and embraced him, saying, “Said I not truly when I said ‘I am that I shall be’? My youth is not as that of Bhanavar the Beautiful, gained at another’s cost, but my own, and stolen from me by wicked sorceries.”
And he cried, “Tell me, O Noorna, my betrothed, how this matter came to pass?”
She said, “On our way to Aklis.”
She bade him grasp the Lily, and follow her; and he followed her down the rock and over the bright shells upon the sand, admiring her stateliness, her willowy lightness, her slimness as of the palm-tree. Then she waded in the water, and began to strike out with her arms, and swim boldly﻿—he likewise; and presently they came to a current that hurried them off in its course, and carried them as weeds, streaming rapidly. He was bearing witness to his faith as a man that has lost hope of life, when a strong eddy stayed him, and whirled him from the current into the calm water. So he looked for Noorna, and saw her safe beside him flinging back the wet tresses from her face, that was like the full moon growing radiant behind a dispersing cloud. And she said, “Ask not for the interpretation of wonders in this sea, for they cluster like dates on a date branch. Surely, to be with me is enough?”
And she bewitched him in the midst of the waters, making him oblivious of all save her, so that he hugged the golden net of her smiles and fair flatteries, and swam with an exulting stroke, giving his breast broadly to the low billows, and shouting verses of love and delight to her. And while they swam sweetly, behold, there was seen a pearly shell of flashing crimson, amethyst, and emerald, that came scudding over the waves toward them, raised to the wind, fan-shaped, and in its front two silver seats. When she saw it, Noorna cried, “She has sent me this, Rabesqurat! Perchance is she favourable to my wishes, and this were well!”

===Chinese===

她答道：“我的芳名且留待他人之口；我分明是宰相费什纳瓦特（Feshnavat）之女，许配给了一位流浪的少年——一位在订婚时面露恶心之色、在献上定亲之吻时只能拿谚语自我安慰、甚至连一句真诚赞词都说不出口的理发师。”
希布利·巴加拉格这才恍然大悟，眼前之人千真万确就是他的未婚妻努尔卡之子努尔娜（Noorna bin Noorka），他满怀情爱与震惊拜倒在她身前；但她将他拉起紧紧搂住他的脖颈，拥抱他道：“我昔日所言‘我乃我未来之模样’，岂非句句属实？我的青春韶华并非如绝代佳人巴纳瓦尔（Bhanavar the Beautiful）那般靠牺牲他人换取，而是属于我自己的容颜，此前不过是被邪恶的巫术生生夺走罢了。”
他急切喊道：“快告诉我，我的未婚妻努尔娜，这一切究竟是如何发生的？”
她答道：“待我们前往阿克利斯（Aklis）的途中再细说吧。”
她命他紧紧握住百合随她前行；他跟随着她走下岩石，踏过沙滩上晶莹的贝壳，暗暗赞叹她雍容的气度、如杨柳般轻盈的体态，以及如棕榈树般修长的身姿。随后她涉水入海，舒展双臂矫健畅游——他也紧随其后；不多时，他们遇上了一股湍急的激流，激流裹挟着他们一路飞速前行，宛如顺流疾漂的水草。正当他自忖生还无望、开始诵念信仰誓词之际，一个强劲的漩涡将他稳稳托住，把他从湍流中甩入了平静的水域。他急忙寻找努尔娜，只见她安然无恙地伴在身旁，轻轻甩开额前湿漉漉的秀发，那张娇颜恰似自散去的云层后绽放出万道华光的圆月。她道：“切莫追问这片海域中的种种奇迹，皆因它们密集如椰枣枝头累累垂挂的果实。有我相伴，难道还不够吗？”
在茫茫波涛之中，她将他彻底迷醉，令他忘却了除她之外的尘世万物，甘愿沉溺在她甜美微笑与柔情软语织就的金网之中；他豪情万丈地劈波斩浪，挺起宽阔的胸膛迎击层层低浪，向着她放声高唱表达情爱与欢愉的诗句。正当二人甜美畅游之际，看哪，海面上浮现出一只闪烁着绯红、紫晶与翡翠光泽的珍珠贝壳舟，扇形迎风破浪朝他们疾驰而来，前方并排设着两张银质座椅。努尔娜见状惊呼道：“这是拉贝斯库拉特（Rabesqurat）赠予我的！看来她对我的愿望颇为顺从，此乃大吉之兆！”

===Original===

Then she swayed in the water sideways, and drew the shell to her, and the twain climbed into it, and sat each on one of the silver seats, folded together. In its lightness it was as a foam-bubble before the wind on the blue water, and bore them onward airily. At his feet Shibli Bagarag beheld a stool of carved topaz, and above his head the arch of the shell was inlaid with wreaths of gems: never was vessel fairer than that.
Now, while they were speeding over the water, Noorna said, “The end of this fair sea is Aklis, and beyond it is the Kush. So while the wind is our helmsman, and we go circled by the quiet of this sea, I’ll tell thee of myself, if thou carest to hear.”
And he cried with the ardour of love, “Surely, I would hear of nought save thyself, Noorna, and the music of the happy garden compareth not in sweetness with it. I long for the freshness of thy voice, as the desert camel for the green spring, O my betrothed!”
So she said, “And now give ear to the following”:﻿—

===Chinese===

于是她在水中侧身游动，将贝壳舟拉到身前，二人相继攀入舟中，紧紧依偎着各自坐在一张银座之上。那贝壳舟轻盈若碧波之上迎风飘曳的泡沫，载着他们凌波御风飞驰。在脚下，希布利·巴加拉格看到了一张精雕细琢的黄玉脚凳，头顶上方的贝壳穹顶镶嵌着一圈圈璀璨的花环宝石：世间绝无任何舟楫能比这更为华美绝伦。
正当他们风驰电掣掠过水面之际，努尔娜说道：“这片仙海的尽头便是阿克利斯（Aklis），越过它便是库什（Kush）。趁着清风为我们掌舵，趁着宁谧的万顷波涛将我们环抱，若你愿意倾听，我便将我自身的身世尽数告知于你。”
他满怀炽热的爱意高呼道：“千真万确，除却你自身，我什么也不愿多听，努尔娜，纵使极乐花园的仙乐也难以媲美这等甘美。我渴望聆听你清澈甜美的嗓音，正如大漠中的骆驼渴盼着青翠的清泉，我的未婚妻啊！”
于是她道：“那么，且洗耳恭听下文：”——

## The Palace of Aklis / 阿克利斯王宫
===Original===

Now, Shibli Bagarag assured himself of his three spells, and made his heart resolute, and hastened up the reddened marble steps of the Palace; and when he was on the topmost step, lo! one with a man’s body and the head of a buffalo, that prostrated himself, and prayed the youth obsequiously to enter the palace with the title of King. So Shibli Bagarag held his head erect, and followed him with the footing of a Sultan, and passed into a great hall, with fountains in it that were fountains of gems, pearls, chrysolites, thousand-hued jewels, and by the margin of the fountains were shapes of men with the heads of beasts-wolves, foxes, lions, bears, oxen, sheep, serpents, asses, that stretched their hands to the falls, and loaded their vestments with brilliants, loading them without cessation, so that from the vestments of each there was another pouring of the liquid lights. Then he with the buffalo’s head bade Shibli Bagarag help himself from the falls; but Shibli Bagarag refused, for his soul was with Noorna, his betrothed; and he saw her pale on that solitary pillar in the tumult of the sea, and knew her safety depended on his faithfulness.
He cried, “The Sword of Aklis! nought save the Sword!”
Now, at these words the fox-heads and the sheep-heads and the ass-heads and the other heads of beasts were lifted up, and lo! they put their hands to their ears, and tapped their foreheads with the finger of reflection, as creatures seeking to bring to mind a serious matter. Then the fountains rose higher, and flung jets of radiant jewels, and a drenching spray of gems upon them, and new thirst aroused them to renew their gulping of the falls, and a look of eagerness was even in the eyes of the ass-heads and the silly sheep-heads; surely, Shibli Bagarag laughed to see them! Now, when he had pressed his lips to recover his sight from the dazzling of those wondrous fountains, he heard himself again addressed by the title of King, and there was before him a lofty cock with a man’s head. So he resumed the majesty of his march, and followed the fine-stepping cock into another hall, spacious, and clouded with heavy scents and perfumes burning in censers and urns, musk, myrrh, ambergris, and livelier odours, gladdening the nostril like wine, making the soul reel as with a draught of the forbidden drink.

===Chinese===

希布利·巴加拉格（Shibli Bagarag）反复确认了随身携带的三件法宝，坚定了心志，快步踏上通往王宫的赤红大理石台阶；当他踏上最高一级台阶时，看哪！一个长着人身水牛头之怪匍匐在地，谄媚恭敬地叩请这位少年以国王的尊号步入宫殿。希布利·巴加拉格昂首挺胸，迈着苏丹般威严的步伐随他前行，步入了一座宏伟的大殿；殿中喷涌着座座喷泉，泉中流淌的尽是宝珠、珍珠、金绿宝石与千姿百态的珍宝，泉池边站立着一具具生着狼、狐狸、雄狮、黑熊、野牛、绵羊、长蛇与野驴兽头的人身怪物，它们争相将双手伸向飞瀑泉流，源源不绝地将宝石填满衣袍，直装得每具衣袍上都倾泻下另一道流光溢彩的珠光瀑布。那水牛头怪请希布利·巴加拉格自取喷泉中的珍宝；但希布利·巴加拉格断然拒绝，因他的心神全系在未婚妻努尔娜（Noorna）身上；他看见她在狂暴波涛中的孤绝石柱上面色惨白，深知她的安危全凭自己的忠贞。
他高呼道：“阿克利斯神剑（The Sword of Aklis）！除却神剑，我一无所求！”
闻听此言，那些狐狸头、绵羊头、野驴头以及诸般兽头怪物纷纷抬起头来，看哪！它们将手搭在耳旁，以沉思的手指轻轻敲击额头，恰若竭力思索重大要事的生灵。紧接着泉水骤然喷涌得更高，向它们倾泻下一道道璀璨宝珠的射流与滂沱的宝石雨，贪婪的渴念驱使它们重新狂饮飞泉，甚至连野驴头与蠢羊头的眼中都流露出急切之色；希布利·巴加拉格见状忍不住失声大笑！待他抿了抿嘴唇、从奇异喷泉的炫目强光中缓过神来之际，他又听到有人以国王的尊号称呼他，眼前赫然出现了一只高大威武、生着人头的大公鸡。于是他重新端起威严的步伐，跟随着昂首阔步的人头公鸡步入另一座大殿；殿内极其宽敞，香炉与大瓮中焚烧的浓烈香料蒸腾起阵阵青烟，麝香、没药、龙涎香以及各种更辛烈的气息扑鼻而来，宛若美酒般沁人心脾，教人神魂颠倒，如饮禁酒般脚步踉跄。

===Original===

Here, before a feast that would prick the dead with appetite, were shapes of beasts with heads of men, asses, elephants, bulls, horses, swine, foxes, river-horses, dromedaries; and they ate and drank as do the famished with munch and gurgle, clacking their lips joyfully. Shibli Bagarag remembered the condition of his frame when first he looked upon the City of Shagpat, and was incited to eat and accede to the invitation of the cock with the man’s head, and sit among these merry feeders and pickers of mouth-watering morsels, when, with the City of Shagpat, lo! he had a vision of Shagpat, hairier than at their interview, arrogant in hairiness; his head remote in contemptuous waves and curls and frizzes, and bushy protuberances of hair, lost in it, like an idolatrous temple in impenetrable thickets. Then the yearning of the Barber seized Shibli Bagarag, and desire to shear Shagpat was as a mighty overwhelming wave in his bosom, and he shouted, “The Sword of Aklis! nought save the Sword!”
Now, at these words the beasts with men’s heads wagged their tails, all of them, from right to left, and kept their jaws from motion, staring stupidly at the dishes; but the dishes began to send forth stealthy steams, insidious whispers to the nose, silver intimations of savouriness, so that they on a sudden set up a howl, and Shibli Bagarag puckered his garments from them as from devouring dogs, and hastened from that hall to a third, where at the entrance a damsel stood that smiled to him, and led him into a vast marbled chamber, forty cubits high, hung with draperies, and in it a hundred doors; and he was in the midst of a very rose-garden of young beauties, such as the Blest behold in Paradise, robed in the colours of the rising and setting sun; plump, with long, black, languishing, almond-shaped eyes, and undulating figures. So they cried to him, “What greeting, O our King?”
Now, he counted twenty and seven of them, and, fitting his gallantry to verse, answered:

> “Poor are the heavens that have not ye

To swell their glowing plenty;

Up there but one bright moon I see,

Here mark I seven-and-twenty.”

===Chinese===

在这座大殿中，面对一桌足以令死者食指大动的饕餮盛宴，伫立着一具具长着人头的走兽：野驴、巨象、公牛、骏马、肥猪、狐狸、河马与单峰驼；它们宛若饥殍般狼吞虎咽，大嚼大咽，咕咚狂饮，惬意地咂嘴作响。希布利·巴加拉格忆起自己初见沙格帕特城时的饥肠辘辘，不禁食欲大动，欲欣然接受人头公鸡的盛情相邀，坐到这群欢快吞咽垂涎美食的饕客之中；然而就在沙格帕特城浮现的同时，看哪！他脑海中浮现出了沙格帕特的虚影，比相见之时须发更为浓密，因多毛而傲慢不可一世；他的面孔深陷在傲慢的卷发、波浪与蓬乱膨胀的毛发丛林深处，宛如隐没在荆棘密林中的异教神庙。理发师的本能渴望刹那间攫住了希布利·巴加拉格，要将沙格帕特剃个精光的欲望如狂涛般在他胸中奔涌激荡，他厉声高呼：“阿克利斯神剑！除却神剑，我一无所求！”
听到这声呐喊，那些长着人头的走兽无不左右摇晃起尾巴，僵住下颌不再咀嚼，呆呆凝视着盘中佳肴；然而一道道盘中珍馐开始蒸腾起缕缕幽香，如窃窃私语般撩拨着鼻息，传递着令人垂涎欲滴的香醇诱惑，引得群兽突然齐声狂嚎起来；希布利·巴加拉格如躲避疯狗般紧紧收拢衣袍，快步逃出大殿，奔入第三座殿堂。在殿堂入口处，伫立着一位向他嫣然巧笑的少女，引他走入一座宽广的大理石殿室，大殿高四十肘（forty cubits），悬垂着层层华幔，其间排列着一百扇门（a hundred doors）；他赫然置身于一片宛若蒙福之人于乐园中所见的年轻佳丽的玫瑰花丛中，她们身披朝霞与落日色泽的罗绮；体态丰腴，生就一双深邃、多情、修长的杏眼，身段婀娜如波浪起伏。她们向他齐声高呼：“尊贵的国王，有何赐教？”
他细细一数，共有二十七位（twenty and seven）佳人，于是他以风雅的诗句回应道：

> “苍穹若无诸位佳丽，
> 亦算不得富丽堂皇；
> 仰首唯见一轮孤月，
> 此地却有明月二十七双。”

===Original===

The damsels laughed and flung back their locks at his flattery, sporting with him; and he thought, “These be sweet maidens! I will know if they be illusions like Rabesqurat”; so, as they were romping, he slung his right arm round one, and held the Lily to her, but there was no change in her save that she winked somewhat and her eyes watered; and it was so with the others, for when they saw him hold the Lily to one they made him do so to them likewise. Then he took the phial, and touched their lips with the waters, and lo! they commenced luting and laughing, and singing verses, and prattling, laughing betweenwhiles at each other; and one, a noisy one, with long, black, unquiet tresses, and a curved foot and roguish ankle, sang as she twirled:

> “My heart is another’s, I cannot be tender;

Yet if thou storm it, I fain must surrender.”

And another, a fresh-cheeked, fair-haired, full-eyed damsel, strong upon her instep and stately in the bearing of her shoulders, sang shrilly:

> “I’m of the mountains, and he that comes to me

Like eagle must win, and like hurricane woo me.”

And another, reclining on a couch buried in dusky silks, like a butterfly under the leaves, a soft ball of beauty, sang moaningly:

> “Here like a fruit on the branch am I swaying;

Snatch ere I fall, love! there’s death in delaying.”

And another, light as an antelope on the hills, with antelope eyes edged with kohl, and timid, graceful movements, and small, white, rounded ears, sang clearly:

> “Swiftness is mine, and I fly from the sordid;

Follow me, follow! and you’ll be rewarded.”

And another, with large limbs and massive mould, that stepped like a cow leisurely cropping the pasture, and shook with jewels amid her black hair and above her brown eyes, and round her white neck and her wrists, and on her waist, even to her ankle, sang as with a kiss upon every word:

> “Sweet ’tis in stillness and bliss to be basking!

He who would have me, may have for the asking.”

And another, with eyebrows like a bow, and arrows of fire in her eyes, and two rosebuds her full moist parted pouting lips, sang, clasping her hands, and voiced like the tremulous passionate bulbul in the shadows of the moon:

> “Love is my life, and with love I live only;

Give me life, lover, and leave me not lonely.”

===Chinese===

少女们听了这番恭维，纷纷甩动青丝娇笑起来，同他嬉闹玩耍；他暗忖：“这群娇娃委实甜美！我且查验一番她们是否如拉贝斯库拉特（Rabesqurat）那般乃是虚幻的假象；”于是趁着嬉戏之际，他伸出右臂揽住其中一位，将百合花凑到她面前，然而除了眨了眨眼、眸中泛起泪光之外，她并无任何变化；其余少女亦是如此，因见他将百合凑到一人面前，便纷纷央求他也这般对待自己。随后他取出小药瓶，以泉水轻触她们的嘴唇，看哪！她们顿时弹起鲁特琴娇笑起来，吟咏诗句，窃窃私语，其间不住相视而笑；其中一个活泼喧闹的少女，披着一头乌黑狂乱的长发，生就一双优美的玉足与俏皮的脚踝，一边翩跹旋转一边唱道：

> “妾心已属他人，难现万种温存；
> 君若强攻入寨，妾身唯有称臣。”

另一位面颊娇艳、金发丰润、顾盼生姿的少女，足弓坚实，香肩挺拔端庄，尖声脆唱道：

> “我本生自崇山峻岭，凡欲求娶妾身之人，
> 须如雄鹰般勇猛夺魁，如狂风般热烈求恩。”

又有一位斜倚在深色丝绒软榻上、宛若花叶下蝴蝶般娇柔圆润的佳人，低吟浅唱道：

> “妾若枝头沉甸之果，随风摇曳难自主；
> 趁我未落速速采撷，莫待蹉跎空遗恨。”

又有一位如山野羚羊般轻盈敏捷的佳人，眼圈描着重重的石榴石眼影，举止羞怯优美，生有一双小巧圆润的雪白耳轮，清脆唱道：

> “妾身轻捷似飞燕，平生最恶庸俗人；
> 紧随妾身莫放手，定教檀郎得芳心。”

又有一位丰腴健美、步态如牧场漫步母牛般安详雍容的佳人，乌发间、褐眸上、雪颈间、手腕上乃至纤腰玉足处皆佩戴着叮当作响的珠宝，唱起歌来字字句句宛若吻痕般甜腻：

> “静谧极乐之中，安享温存多娇！
> 凡欲得妾身者，启齿便可相抱。”

还有一位双眉弯如利弓、眸中喷射炽烈情焰的佳人，丰润微启的湿润双唇宛如两朵含苞待放的玫瑰花蕾，她合十双手，宛若月影下颤动哀鸣的多情夜莺（bulbul）般唱道：

> “情爱乃妾身性命，唯爱方能偷生；
> 乞君赐我性命，莫教妾身孤清。”

===Original===

And a seventh, a very beam of beauty, and the perfection of all that is imagined in fairness and ample grace of expression and proportion, lo! she came straight to Shibli Bagarag, and took him by the hand and pierced him with lightning glances, singing:

> “Were we not destined to meet by one planet?

Can a fate sever us?﻿—can it, ah! can it?”

And she sang tender songs to him, mazing him with blandishments, so that the aim of existence and the summit of ambition now seemed to him the life of a king in that palace among the damsels; and he thought, “Wah! these be no illusions, and they speak the thing that is in them. *Wullahy*, loveliness is their portion; they call me King.”
Then she that had sung to him said, “Surely we have been waiting thee long to crown thee our King! Thou hast been in some way delayed, O glorious one!”
And he answered, “O fair ones, transcending in affability, I have stumbled upon obstructions in my journey hither, and I have met with adventures, but of this crowning that was to follow them I knew nought. *Wullahy*, thrice have I been saluted King; I whom fate selecteth for the shaving of Shagpat, and till now it was a beguilement, all emptiness.”
They marked his bewildered state, and some knelt before him, some held their arms out adoringly, some leaned to him with glistening looks, and he was fast falling a slave to their flatteries, succumbing to them; imagination fired him with the splendours due to one that was a king, and the thought of wearing a crown again took possession of his soul, and he cried, “Crown me, O my handmaidens, and delay not to crown me; for, as the poet says:

> “ ‘The king without his crown

Hath a forehead like the clown’;

===Chinese===

第七位佳人宛若一道绝美的光芒，凝聚了凡人所能想象的一切白皙容颜与曼妙风姿，瞧啊！她径直走到希布利·巴加拉格面前，执起他的手，投以两道如厉电般勾人心魄的目光，唱道：

> “你我同星宿命，岂非前世相约？
> 天命何忍拆散——难道当真能割舍？”

她向他唱起婉转柔肠的情歌，以万般娇媚令他神魂颠倒，使得生存的意义与抱负的顶峰在他眼中，顿化为在这座王宫的群芳簇拥中安享君王之乐；他暗想：“哇！她们绝非虚妄的幻象，句句皆出肺腑。凭安拉起誓（Wullahy），美貌是上苍对她们的恩赐；她们竟尊称我为国王。”
方才向他献唱的佳人开口道：“我们苦候多时，只为加冕您为我们的君王！尊贵无双之人啊，想必您在路上有所耽搁了吧！”
他答道：“和蔼无双的绝色佳人们啊，我此番前来一路关卡重重，经历了诸般离奇的历险，可对于历险之后的加冕盛典，我此前竟一无所知。凭安拉起誓（Wullahy），已有三次（thrice）有人尊称我为国王；我这命中注定要剃光沙格帕特之人，此前所遇的加冕皆是虚幻的骗局与一场空。”
她们窥见了他心神动摇的模样，有的跪倒在他面前，有的深情伸出双臂顶礼膜拜，有的带着秋波流转的娇态倚靠上前；他眼看便要彻底沦为她们阿谀奉承的奴隶，难以自持；狂热的想象以人君应享的无上荣耀将他彻底点燃，头戴王冠的欲念再次霸占了他的灵魂，他高呼道：“快为我加冕吧，我的侍女们，莫要迟延；正如诗人所言：

> ‘王者若无王冠戴，
> 额头宛如小丑颜’；

===Original===

“and the circle of my head itcheth for the symbols of majesty.”
