At these words of Shibli Bagarag they arose quickly and clapped their hands, and danced with the nimble step of gladness, exclaiming, “O our King! pleasant will be the time with him!” And one smoothed his head and poured oil upon it; one brought him garments of gold and silk inwoven; one fetched him slippers like the sun’s beam in brightness; others stood together in clusters, and with lutes and wood-instruments, low-toned, singing odes to him; and lo! one took a needle and threaded it, and gave the thread into the hands of Shibli Bagarag, and with the point of the needle she pricked certain letters on his right wrist, and afterwards pricked the same letters on a door in the wall. Then she said to him, “Is it in thy power to make those letters speak?”
He answered, “We will prove how that may be.”
So he flung some drops from the phial over the letters, and they glowed the colour of blood and flashed with a report, and it was as if a fiery forked-tongue had darted before them and spake the words written, and they were, “This is the crown of him who bath achieved his aim and resteth here.” Thereupon, she stuck the needle in the door, and he pulled the thread, and the door drew apart, and lo! a small chamber, and on a raised cushion of blue satin a glittering crown, thick with jewels as a frost, such as Ambition pineth to wear, and the knees of men weaken and bend beholding, and it lanced lights about it like a living sun. Beside the cushion was a vacant throne, radiant as morning in the East, ablaze with devices in gold and gems, a seat to fill the meanest soul with sensations of majesty and tempt dervishes to the sitting posture. Shibli Bagarag was intoxicated at the sight, and he thought, “Wah! but if I sit on this throne and am a king, with that crown I can command men and things! and I have but to say, ‘Fetch Noorna, my betrothed, from yonder pillar in the midst of the uproarious sea!﻿—Let the hairy Shagpat be shaved!’ and behold, slaves, thousands of them, do my bidding! *Wullahy*, this is greatness!” Now, he made a rush to the throne, but the damsels held him back, crying, “Not for thy life till we have crowned thee, our master and lord!”

===Chinese===

“我头顶的周遭正奇痒难耐，渴盼着象征无上帝王威严的冠冕。”
听到希布利·巴加拉格这番话，少女们迅速起身抚掌欢呼，迈着欢快的轻捷舞步雀跃起舞，高呼道：“我们的王啊！同他相伴的岁月定将无比美妙！”有的为他抚平头发，将香油浇淋在他头上；有的为他捧来金丝银线织就的华服；有的为他取来灿如烈日辉芒的锦履；其余人则三五成群聚拢在一处，抚弄着鲁特琴与木管乐器，低回婉转地向他唱起赞歌；看哪！一名少女取出一枚绣花针穿好丝线，将线头递入希布利·巴加拉格手中，以针尖在他右手腕上刺出几个神秘的字母，随后又在墙壁上的一扇门上刺出了同样的字母。接着她问他道：“你可有能耐令这些字母开口说话？”
他答道：“一试便知。”
于是他从小药瓶中洒出数滴泉水洒在那些字母上，字母顿时泛起如鲜血般的红光，伴随着一声爆响闪耀起来，仿佛一条吞吐着烈焰的分叉蛇信在他们面前疾闪，口吐写就的文字：“此乃功成名就、在此安歇之人的王冠。”随后，少女将绣花针插在门上，他用力一拉丝线，门扉应声滑开，瞧啊！门后是一间小室，在一只凸起的蔚蓝锦缎软垫上，赫然摆放着一顶熠熠生辉的王冠，镶嵌的宝石密匝匝犹如严霜，令世间怀揣野心之人魂牵梦萦，凡人见之无不双膝发软跪拜，周遭迸射出如活生生烈日般的万道神光。在软垫旁立着一座空悬的王座，光彩夺目恰若东方的朝阳，上面镶嵌的金玉图案光华流转，足以令最卑微的灵魂充盈着帝王的崇高之感，令苦行僧（dervish）亦忍不住萌生登基端坐的贪念。希布利·巴加拉格见状心驰神往，暗忖：“哇！我若坐上这王座化身为王，头戴这顶王冠便可号令万民与天地！我只需一声令下：‘去将我的未婚妻努尔娜从狂暴大海中的孤柱上接回来！——把那毛发浓密的沙格帕特彻底剃光！’看哪，成千上万的奴仆自会听我调遣！凭安拉起誓（Wullahy），这才是真正的旷世伟业！”说罢，他猛然朝王座扑去，少女们却死死拉住他，尖叫道：“千万不可，在加冕您为我们的主人与至尊之前，切莫擅自登座！”

===Original===

Then they took the crown and crowned him with it; and he sat upon the throne calmly, serenely, like a Sultan of the great race accustomed to sovereignty, tempering the awfulness of his brows with benignant glances. So, while he sat the damsels hid their faces and started some paces from him, as unable to bear the splendour of his presence, and in a moment, lo! the door closed between him and them, and he was in darkness. Then he heard a voice of the damsels cry in the hall, “The ninety and ninth! Peace now for us and blissfulness with our lords, for now all are filled save the door of the Sword, which maketh the hundredth.” After that he heard the same voice say, “Leave them, O my sisters!”
So he listened to the noise of their departing, and knew he had been duped. Surely his soul cursed him as he sat crowned and throned in that darkness! He seized the crown to dash it to the earth, but the crown was fixed on his forehead and would not come off; neither had he force to rise from the throne. Now, the thought of Noorna, his betrothed, where she rested waiting for him to deliver her, filled Shibli Bagarag with the extremes of anguish; and he lifted his right arm and dashed it above his head in the violence of his grief, striking in the motion a hidden gong that gave forth a burst of thunder and a roll of bellowings, and lo! the door opened before him, and the throne as he sat on it moved out of the chamber into the hall where he had seen the damsels that duped him, and on every side of the hall doors opened; and he marvelled to see men, old and young, beardless and venerable, sitting upon thrones and crowned with crowns, motionless, with eyes like stones in the recesses. He thought, “These be other dupes! *Wullahy!* a drop of the waters of Paravid upon their lips might reveal mysteries, and guide me to the Sword of my seeking.” So, as he considered how to get at them from the seat of his throne, his gaze fell on a mirror, and he beheld the crown on his forehead what it was, bejewelled asses’ ears stiffened upright, and skulls of monkeys grinning with gems! The sight of that crowning his head convulsed Shibli Bagarag with laughter, and, as he laughed, his seat upon the throne was loosened, and he pitched from it, but the crown stuck to him and was tenacious of its hold as the lion that pounceth upon a victim.

===Chinese===

于是她们捧起王冠加冕在他头上；他从容而威严地端坐在王座之上，宛如出身名门望族、久居大宝的苏丹，以慈祥的目光化解眉宇间的凛凛威严。正当他端坐之际，少女们纷纷遮住脸庞，向后倒退了数步，仿佛无法承受他身上散发的逼人荣光，转瞬之间，瞧啊！门扉在他与她们之间轰然关闭，他顿时陷入一片漆黑之中。紧接着他听见大殿中传来一名少女的尖呼：“第九十九扇门（The ninety and ninth）！如今我们终于能与夫君共享安宁与极乐了，因为除了神剑之门这第一百扇门（the hundredth），所有门户皆已圆满！”随后他又听见那个声音喊道：“姐妹们，离开他们吧！”
他倾听着她们离去的脚步声，方知自己中了圈套。在漆黑的幽室中头戴王冠端坐王座的他，灵魂深处忍不住对自己痛骂万分！他一把抓住王冠欲将它摔得粉碎，可王冠却生生嵌在他的额头上纹丝不动；他浑身酸软，连从王座上站起的气力都没有了。此时此刻，一想到未婚妻努尔娜孤零零守候着他的解救，希布利·巴加拉格胸中涌起无尽的痛楚；在剧痛狂乱之中，他挥舞起右臂向头顶猛力一击，这一击恰好撞响了一面暗藏的铜锣，铜锣顿时迸发出一声惊雷般的轰鸣与滚滚咆哮，看哪！眼前的门扉骤然洞开，他所坐的王座自行缓缓滑出暗室，回到了那座他曾目睹欺骗他的少女们的大殿；大殿四壁的门扉齐齐敞开；只见无论男女老幼、皓首长者抑或无须青年，皆头戴王冠端坐在各色王座之上，纹丝不动，双眼宛如石洞深处的顽石，他见状骇异万分。他暗忖：“这些人定是其他的受骗者！凭安拉起誓（Wullahy）！若将一滴帕拉维德之水（Paravid）抹在他们的唇上，或许能揭示玄机，指引我找到苦苦求索的神剑。”正当他思索着如何从王座上挪动靠近他们时，他的目光落在一面明镜之上，赫然看清了自己额头上所戴王冠的真面目——那竟是一对镶满宝石、笔直耸立的驴耳朵，以及一颗颗龇牙咧嘴、嵌满碎钻的猴子骷髅！目睹自己头顶戴着这等滑稽之物，希布利·巴加拉格忍不住浑身发颤哄堂大笑，随着他这一阵大笑，他在王座上的束缚随之松动，整个人从王座上跌落下来，但王冠依然死死扣在他头上，其牢固程度犹如猛扑猎物的雄狮般分毫不放。

===Original===

He bowed to the burden of necessity, and took the phial, and touched the lips of one that sat crowned on a throne with the waters in the phial; and it was a man of exceeding age, whitened with time, and in the long sweep of his beard like a mountain clad with snow from the peak that is in the sky to the base that slopeth to the valley. Then he addressed the old man on his throne, saying, “Tell me, O King! how camest thou here? and in search of what?”
The old man’s lips moved, and he muttered in deep tones, “When cometh he of the ninety-and-ninth door?”
So Shibli Bagarag cried, “Surely he is before thee, in Aklis.”
And the old man said, “Let him ask no secrets; but when he hath reached the Sword forget not to flash it in this hall, for the sake of brotherhood in adventure.”
After that he would answer no word to any questioning.

===Chinese===

他顺应情势低头妥协，取出小药瓶，以瓶中的泉水轻触一位头戴王冠端坐王座之人的嘴唇；那是一位年纪极大的老者，须发在岁月中被染得雪白，长须垂落的弧度恰似一座从插入云霄的峰巅一直覆盖到谷底山脚的皑皑雪山。他对着王座上的老者说道：“告诉我，国王啊！你因何来到此地？又是为了求索何物？”
老者的双唇微微翕动，以低沉的嗓音喃喃自语道：“那开启第九十九扇门之人（the ninety-and-ninth door）何时方至？”
希布利·巴加拉格高呼：“他如今便站在你眼前，在这阿克利斯（Aklis）之中。”
老者道：“莫要探听玄机；但看在同道历险之谊的份上，待他取得神剑之时，切莫忘记在这大殿中挥剑一闪。”
此后，任凭如何发问，老者再也不发一言。

## The Plot / 定计
===Original===

Now, while Shibli Bagarag gazed on Shagpat kindled by the beams of Aklis, lo, the genii Karavejis and Veejravoosh circling each other in swift circles like two sapphire rings toward him, and they whirled to a point above his head, and fell and prostrated themselves at his feet: so he cried, “O ye slaves of the Sword, my servitors! how of the whereabout of Karaz?”
They answered, “O Master of the Event, we found him after many circlings far off, and ’twas by the borders of the Putrid Sea. We came not close on him, for he is stronger than we without the Sword, but it seemed he was distilling drops of an oil from certain substances, large thickened drops that dropped into a phial.”
Then Shibli Bagarag said, “The season of weakness with me is over, and they that confide in my strength, my cunning, my watchfulness, my wielding of the Sword, have nought to fear for themselves. Now, this is my plot, O Feshnavat﻿—that part of it in which thou art to have a share. ’Tis that thou depart forthwith to the City yonder, and enter thy palace by a back entrance, and I will see that thou art joined within an hour of thy arrival there by Baba Mustapha, my uncle, the gabbler. He is there, as I guess by signs; I have had warnings of him. Discover him speedily. Thy task is then to induce him to make an attempt on the head of Shagpat in all wiliness, as he and thou think well to devise. He will fail, as I know, but what is that saying of the poet?

> “ ‘Persist, if thou wouldst truly reach thine ends,

For failures oft are but advising friends.’

“And he says:

> “ ‘Every failure is a step advanced,

To him who will consider how it chanced.’

“Wherefore, will I that this attempt be made, keeping the counsel that is mine. Thou must tell Baba Mustapha I wait without the city to reward him by my powers of reward with all that he best loveth. So, when he has failed in his attempt on Shagpat, and blows fall plenteously upon him, and he is regaled with the accustomed thwacking, as I have tasted it in this undertaking, do thou waste no further word on him, for his part is over, and as is said:

> “ ‘Waste not a word in enterprise!

Against﻿—or for﻿—the minute flies.’

===Chinese===

正当希布利·巴加拉格（Shibli Bagarag）凝视着在阿克利斯（Aklis）神光映照下熠熠生辉的沙格帕特（Shagpat）之际，瞧啊，神怪卡拉韦吉斯与维吉拉武什（Karavejis and Veejravoosh）宛如两枚飞旋的蓝宝石圆环，在空中互相环绕疾驰而来，旋至他头顶上空的一点，随即落下匍匐跪拜在他脚边；于是他高呼道：“神剑之奴仆、我的侍从们啊！卡拉兹（Karaz）如今身在何处？”
二怪答道：“命定大事的主宰（Master of the Event）啊，我们几经盘旋，终于在极遥远的腐臭之海（Putrid Sea）边际发现了他。我们未敢过分逼近，皆因若无神剑在手，他的法力远在我们之上；但他似乎正自某些诡异之物中提炼出滴滴油脂，一颗颗浓稠粗大的油滴正不住滴入一只小药瓶中。”
希布利·巴加拉格道：“我软弱怯懦的时日已然终结，凡信赖我的神力、智谋、警觉与执剑之威能者，皆无须再心怀畏惧。费什纳瓦特（Feshnavat），这便是我定下的计策——其中你当承担的一环。你须当即启程前往前方那座城池，从后门潜入你的府邸；我自会安排你在抵府一个时辰之内同我的叔父、那个饶舌的巴巴·穆斯塔法（Baba Mustapha）汇合。从种种迹象来看，他定然就在城中；我早已察觉到他的踪迹。速速将他找出。届时你的差事便是竭尽机巧，按你二人商定的妙计，诱使他对沙格帕特的头颅动手。我深知他必败无疑，但正如诗人所云：

> ‘若欲矢志成大业，百折不挠莫自弃；
> 屡屡受挫不足惧，败绩常乃良师益。’

“他又道：

> ‘凡能省悟成败由，
> 每经一蹶进一步。’

“正因如此，我才定要让他行此一击，而我自有妙算。你须告知巴巴·穆斯塔法，我正在城外等候，届时将凭我的奖赏大权，赐予他平生最爱之物。待他对沙格帕特下手失败、乱棍如雨点般落在他身上、让他饱尝这桩伟业中我也曾品尝过的皮肉之苦时，你休要再同他多废半句唇舌，因他的戏份已然收场，正如古语所云：

> ‘行大事者莫虚言！
> 顺逆光阴不待人。’

===Original===

“ ’Tis then for thee, O Feshnavat, to speed to the presence of the King in his majesty, and thou wilt find means of coming to him by a disguise. Once in the Hall of Council, challenge the tongue of contradiction to affirm Shagpat other than a baldpate bewigged. This is for thee to do.”
Quoth Feshnavat plaintively, after thought, “And what becometh of me, O thou Master of the Event?”
Shibli Bagarag said, “The clutch of the executioner will be upon thee, O Feshnavat, and a clamouring multitude around; short breathing-time given thee, O father of Noorna, ere the time of breathing is commanded to cease. Now, in that respite the thing that will occur, ’tis for thee to see and mark; sure, never will reverse of things be more complete, and the other side of the picture more rapidly exhibited, if all go as I conceive and plot, and the trap be not premature nor too perfect for the trappers; as the poet has declared:

> “ ‘Ye that intrigue, to thy slaves proper portions adapt;

Perfectest plots burst too often, for all are not apt.’

“And I witness likewise to the excellence of his saying:

> “ ‘To master an Event,

Study men!

The minutes are well spent

Only then.’

“Also ’tis he that says:

> “ ‘The man of men who knoweth men, the Man of men is he!

His army is the human race, and every foe must flee.’

“So have I apportioned to thee thy work, to Baba Mustapha his; reserving to myself the work that is mine!”
Thereat Feshnavat exclaimed, “O Master of the Event, may I be thy sacrifice! on my head be it! and for thee to command is for me to obey! but surely, this Sword of thine that is in thy girdle, the marvellous blade﻿—’tis alone equal to the project and the shave; and the matter might be consummated, the great thing done, even from this point whence we behold Shagpat visible, as ’twere brought forward toward us by the beams! And this Sword swayed by thee, and with thy skill and strength and the hardihood of hand that is thine, *wullahy!* ’twould shear him now, this moment, taking the light of Aklis for a lather.”
Shibli Bagarag knotted the brows of impatience, crying, “Hast thou forgotten Karaz in thy calculations? I know of a surety what this Sword will do, and I wot the oil he distilleth strengtheneth Shagpat but against common blades. Yet shall it not be spoken of me, Shibli Bagarag, that I was tripped by my own conceit; the poet counselleth:

===Chinese===

“到那时，费什纳瓦特，你便须火速奔赴国王御前，想方设法乔装打扮觐见圣颜。一旦步入议事大殿，便公然质问群臣，向一切反对之辞叫阵，咬定沙格帕特不过是个头戴假发的秃驴。这便是你当行之事。”
费什纳瓦特思忖良久，哀怨地问道：“那我自身又将落得何等下场，命定大事的主宰啊？”
希布利·巴加拉格答道：“刽子手的利爪定会将你死死擒住，费什纳瓦特，四周将是喧嚣呐喊的暴民；在下旨处斩之前，努尔娜之父啊，留给你喘息的工夫少之又少。但在那喘息的片刻间将发生何事，你自会亲眼目睹；倘若一切皆按我谋划的连环妙计行事，且陷阱既未提前走漏、亦未因过于精巧而反噬布套之人，那么乾坤颠倒之彻底、风云变幻之迅疾，世间定无出其右者；正如诗人所言：

> ‘凡设密谋者，须量才委奴仆；
> 机关若过巧，常因庸人覆。’

“我亦深信诗人的另一句至理名言：

> ‘若欲成大业，
> 务须洞人心！
> 唯有识人准，
> 方不负寸阴。’

“诗人亦曾言：

> ‘善察人心者，方为豪杰魁！
> 天下皆其兵，万敌尽溃退。’

“因此我为你分派了你的差事，为巴巴·穆斯塔法分派了他的差事；而属于我的重任，我自会一肩挑起！”
费什纳瓦特闻言高呼道：“命定大事的主宰啊，我愿为你肝脑涂地！凡事皆由我一力承担！你一声号令，我自当遵从！然而插在你腰带间的那柄神剑——那柄旷世奇刃，单凭它便足以成就大业、剃光那厮；我们甚至此刻就能大功告成，就在这凭神光将沙格帕特拉至眼前的山巅之上！凭你挥舞此剑的神技与膂力，凭你那双沉稳无畏的巧手，凭安拉起誓（wullahy）！此刻以阿克利斯的神光权作肥皂沫，顷刻间便能将他剃个精光！”
希布利·巴加拉格不耐烦地蹙紧双眉，喝道：“难道你在盘算中把卡拉兹抛到九霄云外了吗？我自然深知此剑的神威，亦深知他提炼的油脂不过是让沙格帕特免受凡铁之伤罢了。但我希布利·巴加拉格绝不能落得被自身傲慢绊倒的话柄；诗人曾告诫道：

===Original===

> “ ‘When for any mighty end thou hast the aid of heaven,

Mount until thy strength shall match those great means which are given’:

“nor that I was overthrown in despising mine enemy, forgetful of the saying of the sage:

> “ ‘Read the features of thy foe, wherever he may find thee,

Small he is, seen face to face, but thrice his size behind thee.’

“*Wullahy!* this Karaz is a genie of craft and resources, one of a mighty stock, and I must close with Shagpat to be sure of him; and that I am not deceived by semblances, opposing guile with guile, and guile deeper than his, for that he awaiteth it not, thinking I have leaped in fancy beyond the Event, and am puffed by the after-breaths of adulation, I!﻿—thinking I pluck the blossoms in my hunger for the fruit, that I eat the chick of the yet unlaid egg, O Feshnavat. As is said, and the warrior beareth witness to the wisdom of it:

> “ ‘His weapon I’ll study; my own conceal;

So with two arms to his one shall I deal.’

“The same also testifieth:

> “ ‘ ’Tis folly of the hero, though resistless in the field,

To stake the victory on his steel, and fling away the shield.’

“And likewise:

> “ ‘Examine thine armour in every joint,

For slain was the Giant, and by a pin’s point.’

“Wah! ’tis certain there will need subtlety in this undertaking, and a plot plotted, so do thou my bidding, and fail not in the part assigned to thee.”
Now, Feshnavat was persuaded by his words, and cried, “In diligence, discretion, and the virtues which characterize subordinates, I go, and I delay not! I will perform the thing required of me, O Master of the Event.” And he repeated in verse:

> “With danger beset, be the path crooked or narrow,

Thou art the bow, and I the arrow.”

===Chinese===

> ‘若得苍天助，欲成旷世功，
> 须修自身力，方配神兵锋’；

“我亦绝不可因轻敌而溃败，忘却智者的训诫：

> ‘凡遇仇敌处，细辨其面貌；
> 当面看似微，背后大三倍。’

“凭安拉起誓（Wullahy）！这卡拉兹是一尊诡计多端、手段通天的神怪，出身强悍名门，我必须近身逼近沙格帕特方能万无一失；我必须确保自己不被假象所惑，以狡计对狡计，且要用远深于他的连环计，皆因他绝料想不到此着，只当我早已在妄想中提前庆祝胜利，被接踵而至的阿谀奉承冲昏了头脑，我！——只当我在对果实的渴望中胡乱采撷花朵，在母鸡尚未下蛋之际便贪婪吃下雏鸡，费什纳瓦特啊。正如勇士见证其明智的古训所云：

> ‘洞悉彼兵刃，深藏己利器；
> 敌唯执一兵，我兼备双刃。’

“古训亦曾明示：

> ‘纵横沙场无敌手，亦莫弃盾单仗剑；
> 孤注一掷倚精铁，实乃英雄至愚念。’

“古语又道：

> ‘严查锁甲每一环，
> 针尖微芒诛巨擘。’

“哇！在这场大业中务须用尽机巧、运筹帷幄，因此你切须遵我号令，休要辜负了托付于你的差事。”
费什纳瓦特被这番话彻底折服，高声喊道：“我必恪守身为部属之勤勉、审慎与忠纯诸般美德，即刻启程，绝不耽搁！我定将完成托付之事，命定大事的主宰啊。”接着他以诗句应和道：

> “纵有万般险阻，前路崎岖狭隘，
> 君乃拉满之弓，仆为离弦之箭。”

===Original===

Then embraced he his daughter, kissing her on the forehead and the eyes, and tightening the girdle of his robe, departed, with the name of Allah on his lips, in the direction of the City.
So Shibli Bagarag called to him the two genii, and his command was, “Soar, ye slaves of the Sword, till the range of Earth and its mountains and seas and deserts are a cluster in the orb of the eye, Shiraz conspicuous as a rose among garlands, and the ruby consorted with other gems in a setting. In Shiraz or the country adjoining ye will come upon one Baba Mustapha by name; and, if he be alone, ye may recognize him by his forlorn look and the hang of his cheeks, his vacancy as of utter abandonment; if in company, ’twill be the only talker that’s he; seize on him, give him a taste of thin air, and deposit him without speech on the roof of a palace, where ye will see Feshnavat in yonder city: this do ere the shadows of the palm-tree by the well in the plain move up the mounds that enclose the fortified parts.”
Cried Karavejis and Veejravoosh, “To hear is to obey.”
Up into the sky, like two bright balls tossed by jugglers, the two genii shot; and, watching them, Noorna bin Noorka said, “My life, there is a third wanting, Ravejoura; and with aid of the three, Earth could have planted no obstruction to thy stroke; but thou wert tempted by the third temptation in Aklis, and left not the hall in triumph, the Hall of the Duping Brides!”
He answered, “That is so, my soul; and the penalty is mine, by which I am made to employ deceits ere I strike.”
And she said, “ ’Tis to the generosity of Gulrevaz thou owest Karavejis and Veejravoosh; and I think she was generous, seeing thee true to me in love, she that hath sorrows!”
So he said, “What of the sorrows of Gulrevaz? Tell me of them.”
But she said, “Nay, O my betrothed! wouldst thou have this tongue blistered, and a consuming spark shot against this bosom?”
Then he: “Make it clear to me.”
She put her mouth to his ear, saying, “There is a curse on whoso telleth of things in Aklis, and to tattle of the Seven and their sister forerunneth wretchedness.”
Surely, he stooped to that fair creature, and folded her to his heart, his whole soul heaving to her; and he cried again and again, “Shall harm hap to thee through me? by Allah, no!”

===Chinese===

随后他拥抱了自己的女儿，亲吻她的额头与双眼，勒紧长袍腰带，口诵安拉圣名，大步流星朝着城池方向而去。
希布利·巴加拉格随即将两尊神怪唤至身前，发号施令道：“神剑之奴仆，速速升空，直飞到大地山川、大海与荒漠皆尽收于眼底、设拉子（Shiraz）如花环中的玫瑰般鲜明夺目、如宝石镶嵌于群珠之中为止。在设拉子或其周边地界，你们将寻得一个名为巴巴·穆斯塔法（Baba Mustapha）之人；若他形单影只，你们便可凭他那落魄的神态、耷拉的双颊以及被彻底抛弃般的失魂落魄认出他来；若他混迹于人群之中，那个唯一的饶舌呱噪之人必定是他；将他一把抓起，让他尝尝九霄高空寒风的滋味，悄无声息地将他扔在前方那座城池中费什纳瓦特所在的王府屋顶之上：务必在平原古井旁棕榈树的倒影移上环绕要塞城垒的土丘之前办妥此事。”
卡拉韦吉斯与维吉拉武什齐声应道：“谨遵法旨。”
两尊神怪如杂耍艺人抛掷的两颗明亮光球般直射苍穹；努尔卡之子努尔娜（Noorna bin Noorka）凝望着他们，说道：“我的心肝，尚缺第三尊神怪拉韦朱拉（Ravejoura）；若得他们三者相助，大地上绝无任何险阻能阻挡你的一挥之威；然而你却在阿克利斯经受不住第三个诱惑，未能以胜利者的姿态走出那座欺诈新娘之殿（the Hall of the Duping Brides）！”
他答道：“确是如此，我的灵魂；我理应承受惩罚，不得不先施机巧谋划方能出刀。”
她道：“你全赖古尔雷瓦兹（Gulrevaz）的慷慨馈赠，方得获卡拉韦吉斯与维吉拉武什；想来她见你对我忠贞不渝，方才这般宽厚相待，她自身亦是满腹悲凄之人！”
于是他问：“古尔雷瓦兹有何悲凄之处？快同我说说。”
她却道：“不可，我的未婚夫！难道你想让我舌头溃烂生疮，胸口遭天火焚烧吗？”
他又道：“快同我说明白。”
她附耳低语道：“凡泄露阿克利斯（Aklis）隐秘之人必遭天谴，多嘴谈论那七子与其胞妹者必招致厄运。”
听到这里，他弯腰抱紧那位绝色佳人，将她紧紧揽入怀中，整颗心皆为之悸动；他连连高呼道：“难道我会因我之故教你蒙难？凭安拉起誓，绝无可能！”

===Original===

And he closed the privileged arm of the bridegroom round her waist, that had the yieldingness of the willow-branchlet, the flowingness of the summer sea-wave, and seemed as ’twere melting honey-like at the first gentle pressure; she leaning her head shyly on his shoulder, yet confiding in his faithfulness; it was that she was shy of the great bliss in her bosom, and was made timid by the fervour of her affection; as is sung:

> “Deeper than the source of blushes

Is the power that makes them start;

Up in floods the red stream rushes,

At one whisper of the heart.”

And it is sung in words present to the youth as he surveyed her:

> “O beauty of the bride! O beauty of the bride!

Her bashful joys like serpents sting her tenderness to tears:

Her hopes are sleeping eagles in the shining of the spheres;

O beauty of the bride! O beauty of the bride!

And she’s a lapping antelope that from her image flees;

And she’s a dove caught in two hands, to pant as she shall please;

O beauty of the bride! O beauty of the bride!

Like torrents over Paradise her lengthy tresses roll:

She moves as doth a swaying rose, and chides her hasty soul;

The thing she will, that will she not, yet can no will control

O beauty, beauty, beauty of the bride!”

===Chinese===

他以新郎独享的臂弯环住她的纤腰，那腰肢如杨柳嫩枝般柔软，似盛夏海波般轻柔流转，仿佛稍加轻触便会如蜜糖般融化；她羞怯地将臻首依偎在他肩头，全身心地信赖着他的坚贞；皆因她对自己胸膛中满溢的巨大极乐感到羞涩，被自身炽烈的情爱弄得胆怯不安；正如诗中所唱：

> “羞红源头深似海，
> 催生红晕力更强；
> 心房方吐半句语，
> 滚滚朱潮上粉庞。”

凝视着她的娇颜，浮现在青年心头的诗句这般唱道：

> “噢新娘之绝色！噢新娘之绝色！
> 羞怯之喜宛若灵蛇，螫得柔肠化作泪痕；
> 满腔希冀似沉睡雄鹰，隐于星宿万道神光；
> 噢新娘之绝色！噢新娘之绝色！
> 她似溪边饮水羚羊，见己倒影惊惶遁逃；
> 她似双手中所执娇鸽，随心所欲急促喘息；
> 噢新娘之绝色！噢新娘之绝色！
> 乌黑秀发如飞瀑长流，倾泻在乐园沃土；
> 步履轻摇如曼舞蔷薇，暗责己心何其急迫；
> 欲拒还迎心绪难平，万般心思难以自控；
> 噢绝色，绝色，新娘之绝色！”

===Original===

They were thus together, Abarak leaning under one wing of Koorookh for shade up the slope of the hill, and Shibli Bagarag called to him, “Ho, Abarak! look if there be aught impending over the City.”
So he arose and looked, crying, “One with plunging legs, high up in air over the City, between two bright bodies.” Shibli Bagarag exclaimed, “ ’Tis well! The second chapter of the Event is opened; so call it, thou that tellest of the Shaving of Shagpat. It will be the shortest.”
Then he said, “The shadow of yonder palm is now a slanted spear up the looped wall of the City. Now, the time of Shagpat’s triumph, and his greatest majesty, will be when yonder walls chase the shadow of the palm up this hill; and then will Baba Mustapha be joining the chorus of creatures that shriek toward even ere they snooze. There’s not an ape in the woods, nor hyena in the forest, nor birds on the branches, nor frogs in the marsh that will outnoise Baba Mustapha under the thong! *Wullahy*, ’twill grieve his soul in aftertime when he sitteth secure in honours, courted, with a thousand ears at his bidding, that so much breath ’scaped him without toll of the tongue! But as the poet says truly:

> “ ‘The chariot of Events lifteth many dusty heels,

And many, high and of renown, it crusheth with its wheels.’

===Chinese===

二人如此温存相伴，阿巴拉克（Abarak）在山坡上方斜倚在神鸟库鲁克（Koorookh）的一只翅膀下乘凉，希布利·巴加拉格向他高呼道：“喂，阿巴拉克！瞧瞧城池上空可有异象。”
阿巴拉克依言起身眺望，大喊道：“有一个双腿乱蹬的人，在两个耀眼光团之间被悬在城池上方的高空中。”希布利·巴加拉格高呼：“妙极！命定大事的第二章（The second chapter of the Event）已然揭幕；叙述剃度沙格帕特（the Shaving of Shagpat）的说书人啊，且这般命名此节吧。这必将是最短的一章。”
随后他道：“远方那株棕榈树的影子，如今已如一杆斜立的长矛投在城池的垛墙上。待到那些城墙将棕榈树的阴影一路逼退至这座山坡上时，便是沙格帕特得享胜利与至高威严的巅峰；到那时，巴巴·穆斯塔法便会加入那些黄昏入眠前尖声啼叫的生灵大合唱。无论树林里的猿猴、密林中的鬣狗、枝头的百鸟，抑或沼泽里的蛙群，在皮鞭抽打之下，叫声绝无任何一种能比得过巴巴·穆斯塔法！凭安拉起誓（Wullahy），日后当他安享尊荣、受人趋奉、号令千万双耳朵倾听之际，定会懊悔自己在惨叫声中白白浪费了那么多没有用来饶舌的宝贵气息！但正如诗人真切所言：

> ‘胜事战车滚滚前，扬起泥尘卷残鞋；
> 无数威名赫赫者，皆被巨轮碾作泥。’

===Original===

“Wah! I have had my share of the thong, and am I, Master of the Event, to be squeamish in attaining an end by its means? Nay, by this Sword!”
Thereat, he strode once again to the summit of the hill, and in a moment the genii fronted him like two shot arrows quivering from the flight. So he cried, “It is done?”
They answered, “In faithfulness.”
So he beckoned to Noorna, and she came forward swiftly to him, exclaiming, “I read the plot, and the thing required of me; so say nought, but embrace me ere I leave thee, my betrothed, my master!”
He embraced her, and led her to where the genii stood. Then said he to the genii, “Convey her to the City, O ye slaves of the Sword, and watch over her there. If ye let but an evil wind ruffle the hair of her head, lo! I sever ye with a stroke that shaketh the under worlds. Remain by her till the shrieks of Baba Mustapha greet ye, and then will follow commotion among the crowd, and cries for Shagpat to show himself to the people, cries also of death to Feshnavat; and there will be an assembly in the King’s Hall of Justice; thither lead ye my betrothed, and watch over her.” And he said to Noorna, “Thou knowest my design?”
So she said, “When condemnation is passed on Feshnavat, that I appear in the hall as bride of Shagpat, and so rescue him that is my father.” And she cried, “Oh, fair delightful time that is coming! my happiness and thy honour on Earth dateth from it. Farewell, O my betrothed, beloved youth! Eyes of mine! these genii will be by, and there’s no cause for fear or sorrow, and ’tis for thee to look like morning that speeds the march of light. Thou, my betrothed, art thou not all that enslaveth the heart of woman?”
Cried Shibli Bagarag, “And thou, O Noorna, all that enraptureth the soul of man! Allah keep thee, my life!”
Lo! while they were wasting the rich love in their hearts, the genii rose up with Noorna, and she, waving her hand to him, was soon distant and as the white breast of a bird turned to the sun. Then went he to where Abarak was leaning, and summoned Koorookh, and the twain mounted him, and rose up high over the City of Shagpat to watch the ripening of the Event, as a vulture watcheth over the desert.

===Chinese===

“哇！我也曾饱尝皮鞭之苦，我身为命定大事的主宰，难道为达目的借助此法还会假装心慈手软吗？凭这柄神剑起誓，绝无可能！”
说罢，他再度阔步迈向山巅，转瞬间两尊神怪如离弦疾射的箭矢般停在他面前微微颤动。他高声问道：“事办妥了吗？”
二怪答道：“分毫不差，忠贞不二。”
于是他向努尔娜招手，她飞步奔上前去，高呼道：“我已参透妙计，亦明晓托付于我的重任；休要多言，在我离去之前拥抱我吧，我的未婚夫，我的主人！”
他紧紧拥抱了她，引她来到两尊神怪跟前。接着他对神怪下令道：“护送她进城，神剑之奴仆，在城中好生守护她。若你们胆敢让任何妖风吹乱她头顶的一根秀发，看哪！我必挥剑一击将你们斩为两段，教九幽地府皆为之震颤。守在她身旁，直至听到巴巴·穆斯塔法的惨叫声，随后人潮中必起骚乱，万民将高呼让沙格帕特出来相见，亦会狂呼处死费什纳瓦特；届时国王审判大殿中必有百官集会；你们便引我的未婚妻前往彼处，寸步不离守护她。”他又对努尔娜道：“你可知我的用意？”
她答道：“待宣判费什纳瓦特死刑之际，我便以沙格帕特新娘的身份现身大殿，以此救下我的生父。”她欢呼道：“噢，美妙欢乐的时刻即将到来！我在人间的幸福与你的无上荣光皆自此刻肇始。别了，我的未婚夫，我至爱的少年！我的心肝啊！有这双神怪相伴，绝无半点可惧可悲之处，你理当意气风发如催生曙光的朝阳。我的未婚夫啊，难道你不是征服女子芳心的一切化身吗？”
希布利·巴加拉格高呼：“而你，努尔娜，乃是令男儿灵魂沉醉的一切化身！愿安拉保佑你，我的生命！”
瞧啊！正当他们倾吐心中丰沛的情爱之际，两尊神怪携着努尔娜腾空而起，她向他挥手作别，不多时便化作远方在朝阳映照下的一点飞鸟白羽。随后他走向阿巴拉克斜倚之处，召来神鸟库鲁克，二人翻身骑上巨鸟，高高盘旋在沙格帕特城上空，宛如大漠上方盘旋的秃鹫般，静静注视着命定大事水到渠成。

## The Revival / 复苏
===Original===

Now, the feathers of Koorookh in his flight were ruffled by a chill breeze, and they were speeding through a light glow of cold rose-colour. Then said Noorna, “ ’Tis the messenger of morning, the blush. Oh, what changes will date from this day!”
The glow of rose became golden, and they beheld underneath them, on one side, the rim of the rising red sun, and rays streaming over the Earth and its waters. And Noorna said, “I must warn Feshnavat, my father, and prepare him for our coming.”
So she plucked a feather from Koorookh and laid the quill downward, letting it drop. Then said she, “Now for the awakening of my betrothed!”
Thereupon she hugged his head a moment, and kissed him on the eyelids, the cheeks, and the lips, crying, “By this means only!” Crying that, she pushed him, sliding, from the back of the bird, and he parted from them, falling headforemost in the air like a stricken eagle. Then she called to Koorookh, “Seize him!” and the bird slanted his beak and closed his wings, the two, Abarak and Noorna, clinging to him tightly; and he was down like an arrow between Shibli Bagarag and the ground, spreading beneath him like a tent, and Noorna caught the youth gently to her lap; then she pushed him off again, intercepting his descent once more, till they were on a level with one of the mountains of the Earth, from which the City of Shagpat is visible among the yellow sands like a white spot in the yolk of an egg. So by this time the eyes of the youth gave symptoms of a desire to look upon the things that be, peeping faintly beneath the lashes, and she exclaimed joyfully, raising her white hands above her head, “One plunge in the lake, and life will be his again!”
Below them was a green lake, tinted by the dawn with crimson and yellow, deep, and with high banks. As they crossed it to the middle, she slipped off the youth from Koorookh, and he with a great plunge was received into the stillness of the lake. Meanwhile Koorookh quivered his wings and seized him when he arose, bearing him to an end of the lake, where stood one dressed like a Dervish, and it was the Vizier Feshnavat, the father of Noorna. So when he saw them, he shouted the shout of congratulation, catching Noorna to his breast, and Shibli Bagarag stretched as doth a heavy sleeper in his last doze, saying, in a yawning voice, “What trouble?

===Chinese===

神鸟库鲁克（Koorookh）振翅疾飞之际，羽翼被一阵清冷的晨风拂乱，他们正穿行在一片冷冽清透的玫瑰色霞光之中。努尔娜（Noorna）说道：“这抹红晕乃是拂晓的信使。噢，自今日始，乾坤将迎来何等巨变！”
玫瑰色的霞光渐渐化为一片金黄，他们看见脚下一侧，一轮红日正跃出地平线，万道金光洒向大地山川与茫茫水泽。努尔娜道：“我须给家父费什纳瓦特（Feshnavat）传信，让他为我们的归来做好接应。”
于是她从库鲁克身上拔下一根翎羽，将羽管朝下轻轻抛落。随后她道：“如今该唤醒我的未婚夫了！”
说罢，她将他的头颅紧紧抱在怀中片刻，亲吻他的眼睑、面颊与双唇，高呼道：“唯有此法方能奏效！”呼喊声中，她猛然一推，将他从鸟背上推滑而下；他脱离了众人，如同一只中箭折翼的雄鹰般头下脚上朝九霄深渊坠落。努尔娜随即向库鲁克喝令道：“接住他！”神鸟俯首收拢双翼，阿巴拉克（Abarak）与努尔娜紧紧攀附在鸟背上；巨鸟如利箭般直射向下，赶在希布利·巴加拉格（Shibli Bagarag）坠地之前稳稳垫在他身下，双翼如帐篷般展开，努尔娜轻柔地将青年揽入怀中；随后她再次将他推落，又一次在半空中截住他的坠落，如此反复，直至他们来到与大地上的一座山峦齐平之处；自那座山峰望去，沙格帕特城（City of Shagpat）座落在茫茫黄沙之中，宛如蛋黄中点缀的一点蛋白。此时，青年的双眼微微显露出苏醒的征兆，在睫毛下若隐若现地张开，她欣喜若狂，举起一双雪白的玉臂欢呼道：“只需往那湖中一浸，他便能重获新生！”
在他们下方是一座碧绿深邃的高岸湖泊，在朝曦映照下泛着绯红与金黄的波光。当他们飞临湖中心之际，她将青年自库鲁克背上轻轻滑落，伴随着扑通一声巨响，他整个人没入了平静沉寂的湖水之中。库鲁克振翼掠过，待他浮出水面之际一把将他衔起，驮往湖畔一角；只见岸边伫立着一个身着托钵苦行僧（Dervish）服饰之人，正是努尔娜之父——宰相费什纳瓦特。费什纳瓦特一见他们，顿时爆发出庆贺的欢呼，一把将努尔娜紧紧搂在怀中；而希布利·巴加拉格则宛如大梦初醒的熟睡之人伸了个长长的懒腰，打着哈欠懒洋洋地嘟囔道：“有何烦扰？

===Original===

I wot there is nought more for us now that Shagpat is shaved! Oh, I have had a dream, a dream! He that is among Houris in Paradise dreameth not a dream like that. And I dreamed﻿—’tis gone!”
Then said he, staring at them, “Who be ye? What is this?”
Noorna, took him again to her bosom, and held him there; and she plucked a herb, and squeezed it till a drop from it fell on either of his lids, applying to them likewise a dew from the serpents of the Sword, and he awoke to the reality of things. Surely, then he prostrated himself and repeated the articles of his faith, taking one hand of his betrothed and kissing her; and he embraced Abarak and Feshnavat, saying to the father of Noorna, “I know, O Feshnavat, that by my folly and through my weakness I have lost time in this undertaking, but it shall be short work now with Shagpat. This thy daughter, the Eclipser of Reason, was ever such a prize as she? I will deserve her. *Wullahy!* I am now a new man, sprung like fire from ashes. Lo, I am revived by her for the great work.”
Said Abarak: “O Master of the Event, secure now without delay the two slaves of the Sword, and lean the blade toward Aklis.”
Upon that, he ran up rapidly to the summit of the mountain and drew the Sword from his girdle, and leaned it toward Aklis, and it lengthened out over lands, the blade of it a beam of solid brilliance. Presently, from forth the invisible remoteness they saw the two genii, Karavejis and Veejravoosh, and they were footing the blade swiftly, like stars, speeding up till they were within reach of the serpents of the hilt, when they dropped to the earth, bowing their heads; so he commanded them to rise, crying, “Search ye the Earth and its confines, and bring hither tidings of the genie Karaz.”
They said, “To hear is to obey.”
Then they began to circle each round the other, circling more and more sharply till beyond the stretch of sight, and Shibli Bagarag said to Feshnavat, “Am I not awake, O Feshnavat? I will know where is Karaz ere I seek to operate on Shagpat, for it is well spoken of the poet:

> “ ‘Obstructions first remove

Ere thou thy cunning prove’;

===Chinese===

如今既然沙格帕特已被剃光，我想世间便再无值得我们操心之事了吧！噢，我方才做了一个梦，好一场大梦！纵使身处乐园仙女（Houris）怀抱之中的人，也未曾做过这般美梦。我梦见了——哎，全散了！”
随后他呆呆瞪着眼前的众人，问道：“你们是何人？这究竟是怎么回事？”
努尔娜再次将他揽入怀中紧紧抱住；她采下一株药草，用力挤压，将草汁各滴了一滴在他的两只眼睑上，复又抹上神剑剑柄灵蛇吐出的甘露，他这才猛然从虚妄中彻底清醒，重归现世。他当即虔诚跪拜在地，反复诵念信仰誓词，执起未婚妻的一只纤手深情亲吻；随后他拥抱了阿巴拉克与费什纳瓦特，对努尔娜之父说道：“费什纳瓦特，我深知皆因我往昔的愚行与软弱，在这场大业中虚掷了诸多光阴，但如今解决沙格帕特不过是指顾之间的事。令媛这位倾国倾城之女（the Eclipser of Reason），世间可曾有过这般无价的至宝？我定当不负她的深情。凭安拉起誓（Wullahy）！我如今已脱胎换骨，宛如从死灰中重生的烈焰。看哪，我承蒙她的恩德重获新生，誓要克成伟业。”
阿巴拉克道：“命定大事的主宰（Master of the Event）啊，如今切莫迟延，速速召齐神剑之双奴，将剑刃对准阿克利斯（Aklis）。”
希布利·巴加拉格闻言飞奔登上山巅，从腰带间抽出神剑，将剑锋斜指阿克利斯的方向；神剑立时向大地极远处无限延伸，剑身化作一道坚实耀眼的纯白华光。不多时，自那目力难及的虚空深处，他们看见两尊神怪卡拉韦吉斯与维吉拉武什（Karavejis and Veejravoosh）如流星般踏着剑脊飞驰而来，疾奔至剑柄灵蛇盘踞的触手可及之处，双双跌落在地躬身叩首；他命二怪起身，喝令道：“速速搜遍大地八荒，将神怪卡拉兹（Karaz）的动向报来！”
二怪齐声应道：“谨遵法旨。”
随后它们开始互相环绕飞旋，越旋越疾，直至消失在视野之外；希布利·巴加拉格对费什纳瓦特道：“我难道还未曾清醒吗，费什纳瓦特？在对沙格帕特下手之前，我务须弄清卡拉兹身在何处，正如诗人所言：

> ‘欲施绝技显神工，
> 须先扫平前路障’；

===Original===

“and I will encounter this Karaz that was our ass, ere I try the great shave.”
Then said he, turning quickly, “Yonder is the light from Aklis striking on the city, and I mark Shagpat, even he, illumined by it, singled out, where he sitteth on the roof of the palace by the marketplace.”
So they looked, and it was as he had spoken, that Shagpat was singled out in the midst of the city by the wondrous beams of the eye of Aklis, and made prominent in effulgence.
Said Abarak, climbing to the level of observation, “He hath a redness like the inside of a halved pomegranate.”
Feshnavat stroked his chin, exclaiming, “He may be likened to a mountain goat in the midst of a forest roaring with conflagration.”
Said Shibli Bagarag, “Now is he the red-maned lion, the bristling boar, the uncombed buffalo, the plumaged cock, but soon will he be like nothing else save the wrinkled kernel of a shaggy fruit. Lo, now, the Sword! it leapeth to be at him, and ’twill be as the keen icicle of winter to that perishing foliage, that doomed crop! So doth the destined minute destroy with a flash the hoarded arrogance of ages; and the destined hand doeth what creation failed to perform; and ’tis by order, destiny, and preordainment, that the works of this world come to pass. This know I, and I witness thereto that am of a surety ordained to the Shaving of Shagpat!”
Then he stood apart and gazed from Shagpat to the city that now began to move with the morning; elephants and coursers saddled by the gates of the King’s palace were visible, and camels blocking the narrow streets, and the markets bustling. Surely, though the sun illumined that city, it was as a darkness behind Shagpat singled by the beams of Aklis.

===Chinese===

“在我施展剃度大业之前，我定要与曾化作我们坐骑毛驴的卡拉兹做个了断。”
随后他霍然转身说道：“阿克利斯的神光正笼罩着城池，我已清清楚楚看见了沙格帕特，正是那厮，在神光的照耀下显露无遗，正端坐在集市旁王府的屋顶之上。”
众人依言望去，果然正如他所言，沙格帕特在阿克利斯神眼的奇幻光芒照耀下，从城池正中被凸显而出，周身散发着夺目的红光。
阿巴拉克爬上高处眺望，说道：“他浑身通红，宛如掰开两半的石榴籽心。”
费什纳瓦特捋着下巴胡须，惊叹道：“他倒像是一头置身于烈火熊熊森林之中的山羊。”
希布利·巴加拉格冷笑道：“此刻他固然是赤鬃雄狮、倒竖鬃毛的野猪、未经梳理的野牛与羽翼华丽的雄鸡，但不多时，他便会沦为干瘪多皱的毛壳果核。看哪，神剑！它早已按捺不住欲扑向那厮，对于那些注定凋零的枝叶与在劫难逃的杂草而言，神剑将化作凛冬的森森寒冰！命定的时刻正是如此以一道雷霆万钧的闪电，将千百年来积聚的傲慢彻底摧毁；命定之手将成就创世以来万物未能克成之伟业；世间万般功业，皆依天序、宿命与冥冥前定而成就。我深知此理，亦身为命定剃度沙格帕特（the Shaving of Shagpat）之人为此见证！”
随后他退到一旁，凝望着沙格帕特与在晨曦中渐渐苏醒的城池；王宫大门前备好鞍辔的大象与骏马清晰可见，骆驼挤满了狭窄的街道，集市上人声鼎沸。尽管旭日照亮了整座城池，但在阿克利斯神光映衬下的沙格帕特身后，城池的万千繁华竟宛若一片晦暗的阴影。

## The Sons of Aklis / 阿克利斯的诸子
===Original===

Now, Shibli Bagarag thought, “The poet is right in Aklis as elsewhere, in his words:

> “ ‘The cunning of our oft-neglected wit

Doth best the keyhole of occasion fit’;

===Chinese===

此时，希布利·巴加拉格（Shibli Bagarag）心想：“无论在阿克利斯（Aklis）还是世间他处，诗人所言皆颠扑不破：

> ‘凡人常弃之机敏才智，
> 恰配良机之锁孔绝契’；

===Original===

“and whoso looketh for help from others looketh the wrong way in an undertaking. Wah! I will be bold and batter at the hundredth door, which is the door of the Sword.” So he advanced straightway to the door, which was one of solid silver, charactered with silver letters, and knocked against it three knocks; and a voice within said, “What spells?”
He answered, “Paravid; Garraveen; and the Lily of the Sea!”
Upon that the voice said, “Enter by virtue of the spells!” and the silver door swung open, discovering a deep pit, lightened by a torch, and across it, bridging it, a string of enormous eggs, rocs’ eggs, hollowed, and so large that a man might walk through them without stooping. At the side of each egg three lamps were suspended from a claw, and the shell passage was illumined with them from end to end. Shibli Bagarag thought, “These eggs are of a surety the eggs of the Roc mastered by Aklis with his sword!” Now, as the sight of Shibli Bagarag grew familiar to the place, he beheld at the bottom of the pit a fluttering mass of blackness and two sickly eyes that glittered below.
Then thought he, “Wah! if that be the Roc, and it not dead, will the bird suffer one to defile its eggs with other than the sole of the foot, naked?” He undid his sandals and kicked off the slippers given him by the damsels that had duped him, and went into the first egg over the abyss, and into the second, and into the third, and into the fourth, and into the fifth. Surely the eggs swung with him, and bent; and the fear of their breaking and he falling into the maw of the terrible bird made him walk unevenly. When he had come to the seventh egg, which was the last, it shook and swung violently, and he heard underneath the flapping of the wings of the Roc, as with eagerness expecting a victim to prey upon. He sustained his soul with the firmness of resolve and darted himself lengthwise to the landing, clutching a hold with his right hand; as he did so, the bridge of eggs broke, and he heard the feathers of the bird in agitation, and the bird screaming a scream of disappointment as he scrambled up the sides of the pit.
Now, Shibli Bagarag failed not to perform two prostrations to Allah, and raised the song of gratitude for his preservation when he found himself in safety. Then he looked up, and lo!

===Chinese===

“凡求成大事者若寄望他人之助，从一开始便走错了方向。哇！我当鼓足勇气去砸开第一百扇门（the hundredth door），那便是神剑之门。”于是他径直走到门前，那是一扇纯银打造的大门，上面铸刻着银色的文字，他上前叩击了三下（three knocks）；门内传来一个声音问道：“有何护身法宝？”
他答道：“帕拉维德（Paravid）；加拉文（Garraveen）；以及迷境之海的百合！”
门内的声音随即道：“凭法宝之威，准予入内！”纯银大门应声洞开，展现在眼前的是一口由火把照亮的深渊巨坑，横跨在深坑上方充当桥梁的，乃是一串连绵巨大的禽蛋——被掏空的大鹏鸟之蛋（rocs’ eggs），巨大到凡人直立穿行其间亦无须弯腰弓身。每枚巨蛋旁皆有一只鸟爪悬吊着三盏明灯（three lamps），将整条蛋壳通道从头到尾照耀得通明澄亮。希布利·巴加拉格暗想：“这些巨蛋定是阿克利斯当年执剑降伏的大鹏鸟之卵无疑！”待双眼逐渐适应了周遭的幽暗，他赫然望见深坑底部有一团不住扑腾的漆黑之物，两只泛着病态凶光的巨眼正在坑底闪烁。
他转念一想：“哇！倘若那真是大鹏鸟且尚未毙命，那猛禽又怎会容忍他人以赤足之外的污秽鞋履玷污它的卵蛋？”他当即解开凉鞋，踢掉了那些欺骗他的少女们所赠的绣履，赤足踏入了横跨深渊的第一只巨蛋，接着走入第二只、第三只、第四只与第五只。巨蛋在他脚下剧烈摇晃弯曲；唯恐蛋壳碎裂坠入恶禽血盆大口的恐惧，令他步履踉跄不稳。待他来到第七只巨蛋——亦即最后一只蛋中时，巨蛋狂暴地摇撼震颤起来，他清晰听见下方传来大鹏鸟振翅的扑棱声，宛若迫不及待欲将猎物拆吃入腹。他以钢铁般的意志稳住心神，纵身一跃直扑对岸的石阶，右手死死扣住岩石边缘；就在这一刹那，巨蛋之桥轰然断裂坍塌，在攀爬登上深坑绝壁之际，他听见巨鸟躁动拍打着羽毛，发出一声猎物逃脱的绝望尖啸。
待希布利·巴加拉格确信自己安然脱险之后，急忙向安拉行了两次叩头大礼（two prostrations to Allah），高唱感恩的赞歌以谢神恩保佑。随后他抬头一瞧，看哪！

===Original===

behind a curtain, steps leading to an anteroom, and beyond that a chamber like the chamber of kings where they sit in state dispensing judgements, like the sun at noon in splendour; and in the chamber seven youths, tall and comely young men, calm as princes in their port, each one dressed in flowing robes, and with a large glowing pearl in the front of their turbans. They advanced to meet him, saying, “Welcome to Aklis, thou that art proved worthy! ’Tis holiday now with us”; and they took him by the hand and led him with them in silence past fountain-jets and porphyry pillars to where a service with refreshments was spread, meats, fowls with rice, sweetmeats, preserves, palateable mixtures, and monuments of the cook’s art, goblets of wine like liquid rubies. Then one of the youths said to Shibli Bagarag, “Thou hast come to us crowned, O our guest! Now, it is not our custom to pay homage, but thou shalt presently behold them that will, so let not thy kingliness droop with us, but feast royally.”
And Shibli Bagarag said, “O my princes, surely it is a silly matter to crown a mouse! Humility hath depressed my stature! *Wullahy*, I have had warning in the sticking of this crown to my brows, and it sticketh like an abomination.”
They laughed at him, saying, “It was the heaviness of that crown which overweighted thee in the bridge of the abyss, and few be they that bear it and go not to feed the Roc.”
Now, they feasted together, interchanging civilities, offering to each other choice morsels, dainties. And the anecdotes of Shibli Bagarag, his simplicity and his honesty, and his vanity and his airiness, and the betraying tongue of the barber, diverted the youths; and they plied him with old wine till his stores of merriment broke forth and were as a river swollen by torrents of the mountain; and the seven youths laughed at him, spluttering with laughter, lurching with it. Surely, he described to them the loquacity of Baba Mustapha his uncle, and they laughed so that their chins were uppermost; but at his mention of Shagpat greater gravity was theirs, and they smoothed their faces solemnly, and the sun of their merriment was darkened for a while. Then they took to flinging about pellets of a sugared preparation, and reciting verses in praise of jovial living, challenging to drink this one and that one, passing the cup with a stanza.

===Chinese===

在帷幔后方，石阶通向一间前厅，前厅之外是一座恢宏的宫殿，宛若帝王升座临朝听政的大殿，璀璨夺目如正午的烈日；殿中伫立着七位青年（seven youths），皆身材魁梧、相貌堂堂，举止从容宛若王孙公子，每人皆身披飘逸的长袍，头巾前端镶嵌着一颗硕大莹润的宝珠。他们迎上前来说道：“欢迎来到阿克利斯（Aklis），经受住考验的有德之士啊！如今我们终于能安享休沐了”；他们执起他的手，默默引着他穿过座座喷泉射流与斑岩石柱，来到一片摆满珍馐美馔的筵席前，桌上陈列着烤肉、抓饭肥禽、糖果、蜜饯、可口各色羹汤，以及大厨烹饪技艺的旷世杰作，杯中美酒色泽宛如流动的红宝石。一位青年对希布利·巴加拉格说道：“尊贵的客人，你头戴王冠莅临寒舍！按我们的规矩，我们本不向人俯首称臣，但稍后你自会见到向你顶礼膜拜之辈，切莫因我们而在王气上有所懈怠，且尽享王者之宴吧。”
希布利·巴加拉格道：“诸位王子，为一只老鼠加冕委实是件可笑荒唐之事！谦逊早已挫败了我的虚骄！凭安拉起誓（Wullahy），这顶王冠死死黏在我额头上，已是对我的警示，它黏在头上恰似令人憎恶的耻辱。”
青年们哄堂大笑，说道：“正是这顶王冠的沉重，令你在深渊巨蛋之桥上险些失足，凡头戴此冠之人，极少能逃脱沦为大鹏鸟口粮的厄运。”
于是众人一同畅饮欢宴，推杯换盏，互献珍馐美味。希布利·巴加拉格讲述的桩桩奇遇、他的质朴与坦率、他的虚荣与浮夸，乃至理发师那兜不住话的饶舌天性，令青年们大开眼界、乐不可支；他们频频向他劝饮陈年佳酿，直引得他肚里的欢声笑语如山洪暴发的大河般奔涌而出；七位青年被他逗得喷饭捧腹，前仰后合。当他向他们绘声绘色描摹叔父巴巴·穆斯塔法（Baba Mustapha）的滔滔不绝时，青年们笑得仰面朝天；然而每当他提及沙格帕特（Shagpat）的名号时，他们便神色骤敛，肃穆端庄，欢快的气氛顿时笼罩上一层阴霾。随后他们开始相互抛掷糖渍蜜丸嬉戏，吟诵赞美快意人生的诗句，彼此劝酒对饮，每传一杯酒便赋诗一首。

===Original===

Shibli Bagarag thought, “What a life is this led by these youths! a fair one! ’Tis they that be the sons of Aklis who sharpen the Sword of Events; yet live they in jollity, skimming from the profusion of abundance that which floateth!”
Now, marking him contemplative, one of the youths shouted, “The King lacketh homage!”
And another called, “Admittance for his people!”
Then the seven arose and placed Shibli Bagarag on an elevation in the midst of them, and lo! a troop of black slaves leading by the collar, asses, and by a string, monkeys. Now, for the asses they brayed to the Evil One, and the monkeys were prankish, pulling against the string, till they caught sight of Shibli Bagarag. Then was it as if they had been awestricken; and they came forward to him with docile steps, eyeing the crown on his head, and prostrated themselves, the asses and the monkeys, like creatures in whom glowed the lamp of reason and the gift of intelligence. So Shibli Bagarag drooped his jaw and was ashamed, and he cried, “my princes! am I a King of these?”
They answered, “A King in mightiness! Sultan of a race!”
So he said, “It is certain I shall need physic to support such a sovereignty! And I must be excused liberal allowances of old wine to sit in state among them. *Wullahy!* they were best gone for a while. Send them from me, O my princes! I sicken.”
And he called to the animals, “Away! begone!” frowning.
Then said the youths, “Well commanded! and like a King! See, they troop from thy presence obediently.”
Now the animals fled from before the brows of Shibli Bagarag, and when the chamber was empty of them the seven young men said, “Of a surety thou wert flattered to observe the aspect of these animals at beholding thee.”
But he cried, “Not so, O my princes; there is nought flattering in the homage of asses and monkeys.”
Then they said, “O Sultan of asses, ruler of monkeys, better that than thyself an ass and an ape! As was said by Shah Kasirwan, ‘I prefer being king of beasts worshipped by beasts, rather than a crowned beast worshipped by men’; and it was well said. *Wullahy!* the kings of Roum quote it.”

===Chinese===

希布利·巴加拉格暗忖：“这群青年过的究竟是何等神仙日子！委实美妙！他们分明是磨砺胜事之剑（the Sword of Events）的阿克利斯之子；却能如此逍遥快活，尽享人间富贵浮华的至味！”
见他陷入沉思，一名青年高呼道：“国王尚未受臣民朝拜呢！”
另一人随即喊道：“宣他的子民觐见！”
七位青年当即起身，将希布利·巴加拉格扶上大殿正中央的一处高台，看哪！一队黑奴用颈圈牵着一群毛驴，用绳索引着一群猴子鱼贯而入。那些毛驴原本正向着恶魔狂暴嘶鸣，猴子亦上蹿下跳顽劣不堪，死死拽着缰绳，可一瞥见希布利·巴加拉格的身影，刹那间仿佛被震慑住了一般；它们盯着他头顶的王冠，迈着驯顺的步伐走上前去，无论是毛驴还是猴子，皆纷纷匍匐跪拜在地，恰若拥有灵性与理智的万物之灵。希布利·巴加拉格目瞪口呆，羞愧难当，高呼道：“诸位王子！难道我竟是这群畜生的国王吗？”
他们齐声答道：“神武非凡之君！一族之苏丹！”
于是他道：“若要执掌这等江山，我必定得服药压惊！更须饮下海量陈酿方能端坐在它们面前。凭安拉起誓（Wullahy）！最好让它们暂且退下。诸位王子，快赶它们走吧！我恶心欲呕。”
他蹙眉对着那群牲畜喝令道：“滚开！速速退下！”
青年们赞道：“旨意下得好！真有帝王气度！瞧，它们已驯服地从御前退下了。”
牲畜群在希布利·巴加拉格的怒容前仓皇退散，待大殿重归肃静，七位青年说道：“眼见这群生灵对你这般膜拜，你心中想必大感脸上有光吧。”
他急忙呼道：“诸位王子，休要取笑；受驴猴朝拜绝无半分荣耀可言。”
他们说道：“驴之苏丹、猴之君主啊，总好过你自己沦为驴猴！正如沙阿·卡西尔万（Shah Kasirwan）昔日所言：‘我宁为受百兽朝拜的万兽之王，亦不愿为受万人膜拜的戴冠禽兽’；此言委实精辟。凭安拉起誓（Wullahy）！鲁姆诸王（kings of Roum）至今仍将其奉为圭臬。”

===Original===

Now Shibli Bagarag was not rendered oblivious of the Sword of his quest by the humour of these youths, or the wine-bibbings, and he exclaimed while they were turning up the heels of their cups, “O ye sons of Aklis, know that I have come hither for the Sword sharpened by your hands, for the releasing of my betrothed, Noorna bin Noorka, daughter of the Vizier Feshnavat, and for the shaving of Shagpat.”
While he was proceeding to recount the story of his search for the Sword, they said, “Enough, O potentate of the braying class and of the scratching tribe! we have seen thee through the eye of Aklis since the time of thy first thwacking. What says the poet?

> “ ‘A day for toil and a day for rest

Gives labour zeal, and pleasure zest.’

===Chinese===

希布利·巴加拉格并未因这群青年的幽默风趣或狂饮烂醉而忘却求取神剑的初衷，乘着他们仰脖饮尽杯中余沥之际，他高声说道：“阿克利斯的诸子啊，可知我跋涉至此，正是为了求取你们亲手磨砺的神剑，以解救我的未婚妻——宰相费什纳瓦特之女努尔卡之子努尔娜（Noorna bin Noorka），并剃度沙格帕特。”
正当他欲细述求剑始末之际，青年们打断他道：“且慢，嘶鸣之邦与乱挠之族的至尊啊！自你初次挨了那顿皮鞭痛打起，我们便通过阿克利斯的神眼注视着你的一举一动。正如诗人所云：

> ‘一日辛劳一日休，
> 劳作愈勇乐愈酬。’

===Original===

“So, of thy seeking let us hear tomorrow; but now drink with us, and make merry, and touch the springs of memory; spout forth verses, quaint ones, suitable to the hour and the entertainment. *Wullahy!* drink with us! taste life! Let the humours flow.”
Then they made a motion to some slaves, and presently a clattering of anklets struck the ear of Shibli Bagarag: and he beheld dancing-girls, moons of beauty and elegance, and they danced wild dances, and dances graceful and leopard-like and serpent-like in movement; and the youths flung flowers at them, applauding them. Then came other sets of dancers even lovelier, more languishing; and again others with tambourines and musical instruments, that sang ravishingly. So the senses of Shibli Bagarag were all taken with what he saw and heard, and ate and drank; and by degrees a mist came before his eyes, and the sweet sounds and voices of the girls grew distant, and it was with difficulty he kept his back from the length of the cushions that were about him. Then he thought of Noorna, and that she sang to him and danced, and when he rose to embrace her she was Rabesqurat by the light of the Lily! And he thought of Shagpat, and that in shaving him the blade was checked in its rapid sweep, and blunted by a stumpy twine of hair that waxed in size and became the head of Karaz that gulped at him a wide devouring gulp, and took him in, and flew up with him, leaving Shagpat half sheared. Then he thought himself struggling halfway down the throat of the monstrous Roc, and that, when he was wholly inside the Roc, he was in a wide-arched passage crowded with lamps, and at the end of the passage Noorna in the clutch of Karaz, she shouting, “The Sword, the Sword!”
Now, while he felt for the Sword wherewith to release her from the genie, his eyes opened, and he saw day through a casement, and that he had reposed on an embroidered couch in the corner of a stately room ornamented with carvings of blue and gold. So while he wondered and yawned, gaping, slaves started up from the floor and led him to a bath of coloured marble, and bathed him in perfumed waters, and dressed him in a dress of yellow silk, rich and ample.

===Chinese===

“关于你求剑之事，且留待明日再议；今朝且同我们痛饮作乐，唤醒记忆的甘泉；赋诵应景成趣的妙诗以佐酒兴。凭安拉起誓（Wullahy）！同我们共饮吧！领略人生的至乐！且放浪形骸任性情流淌。”
说罢，他们向奴仆打了个手势，希布利·巴加拉格耳畔随即传来阵阵脚镯叮当的脆响：只见群姝翩翩起舞，个个容颜皎洁如明月、风姿绰约，时而跳起奔放狂野的舞蹈，时而舞姿曼妙如金钱豹之矫健、长蛇之婀娜；青年们向舞姬抛掷鲜花连连喝彩。紧接着又换上一批更为绝艳、更为风情万种的舞女；随后又有一队手持手鼓与各色乐器的乐伎上前，歌声动人心魄。希布利·巴加拉格目睹耳闻、大嚼畅饮，五感皆被彻底俘获；渐渐地，一团薄雾笼罩了他的双眸，少女们甜美的欢歌笑语变得遥不可及，他竭尽全力才勉强支撑着不倒向身旁的软垫。随后他在恍惚中想起了努尔娜，梦见她向他献唱起舞，可当他起身欲拥抱她时，在妙光百合的映照下她竟化作了拉贝斯库拉特（Rabesqurat）！他又梦见了沙格帕特，梦见在剃发之际刀刃受阻，被一簇粗短盘结的毛发挡住变得钝拙不堪，那簇毛发生长膨胀，化作了卡拉兹（Karaz）的狰狞头颅，张开血盆大口一口将他吞入腹中，挟持着他冲上云霄，任由沙格帕特只剃了一半。接着他又梦见自己在庞大凶恶的大鹏鸟咽喉中拼命挣扎，而当他彻底落入大鹏鸟腹中时，却置身于一条挂满明灯的宽敞拱形长廊，在长廊尽头，努尔娜正被卡拉兹死死攥在爪中，尖叫呼喊道：“神剑，神剑！”
正当他摸索神剑欲自神怪手中解救她之际，他睁开了双眼，只见窗扇外天光大亮，自己正安卧在装饰着蓝金雕花的名贵殿室一角的绣榻之上。正当他惊疑不定、哈欠连天之际，奴仆们自地板上翻身而起，引他来到一座五彩大理石浴池前，以香汤为他沐浴更衣，换上一袭华贵宽大的黄色丝袍。

===Original===

Then they paraded before him through lesser apartments and across terraces, till they came to a great hall; loftier and more spacious than any he had yet beheld, with fountains at the two ends, and in the centre a tree with golden spreading branches and leaves of gold; among the leaves gold-feathered birds, and fruits of all seasons and every description﻿—the drooping grape and the pleasant-smelling quince, and the bloodred pomegranate, and the apricot, and the green and rosy apple, and the gummy date, and the oily pistachio-nut, and peaches, and citrons, and oranges, and the plum, and the fig. Surely, they were countless in number, melting with ripeness, soft, full to bursting; and the birds darted among them like sun-flashes. Now, Shibli Bagarag thought, “This is a wondrous tree! *Wullahy!* there is nought like it save the tree in the hall of the Prophet in Paradise, feeding the faithful!” As he regarded it he heard his name spoken in the hall, and turning he beheld seven youths in royal garments, that were like the youths he had feasted with, and yet unlike them, pale, and stern in their manners, their courtesy as the courtesy of kings. They said, “Sit with us and eat the morning’s meal, O our guest!”
So he sat with them under the low branches of the tree; and they whistled the tune of one bird and of another bird, and of another, and lo! those different birds flew down with golden baskets hanging from their bills, and in the baskets fruits and viands and sweetmeats, and cool drinks. And Shibli Bagarag ate from the baskets of the birds, watching the action of the seven youths and the difference that was in them. He sought to make them recognise him and acknowledge their carouse of the evening that was past, but they stared at him strangely and seemed offended at the allusion, neither would they hear mention of the Sword of his seeking. Presently, one of the youths stood upon his feet and cried, “The time for kings to sit in judgement!”
And the youths arose and led Shibli Bagarag to a hall of ebony, and seated him on the upper seat, themselves standing about him; and lo! asses and monkeys came before him, complaining of the injustice of men and their fellows, in brays and bellows and hoots. Now, at the sight of them again Shibli Bagarag was enraged, and he said to the youths, “How! do ye not mock me, O masters of Aklis!”

===Chinese===

随后奴仆们引着他穿过间间偏殿，跨过重重露台，直至来到一座宏伟的大殿；大殿比他所见过的任何殿堂更为高大宽敞，两端设有喷泉，正中伫立着一株枝桠舒展、通体纯金铸就的巨树，挂满了黄金铸成的叶片；金叶间栖息着生有金羽的神鸟，结满了四时各色的鲜果——垂挂的葡萄、芬芳扑鼻的木瓜、鲜红如血的石榴、杏子、泛着青红两色的苹果、饱满粘稠的蜜枣、油润的开心果，以及鲜桃、枸橼、甜橙、李子与无花果。果实累累数不胜数，熟得几欲融化，饱满欲裂；神鸟穿梭其间，宛如道道烈日闪光。希布利·巴加拉格暗想：“这端的是一株奇树！凭安拉起誓（Wullahy）！除却先知在乐园大殿中赐予信徒食粮的神树，世间绝无任何事物堪与之媲美！”正当他凝神观瞧之际，大殿中传来了呼唤他名讳的声音，他转过身，只见七位身着王族华服的青年伫立在眼前，容貌宛如昨夜同他痛饮的青年，神态却判若两人，面色惨白、神情冷峻，举手投足间带着帝王般的威仪。他们说道：“请与我们同坐，共进晨馔，尊贵的客人！”
于是他随他们在巨树低垂的枝桠下落座；他们分别吹出口哨模仿不同神鸟的鸣声，看哪！那些神鸟嘴里衔着黄金花篮纷纷飞落而下，篮中盛满了鲜果、佳肴、甜点与清凉饮品。希布利·巴加拉格自鸟篮中取食，暗暗打量着七位青年的一举一动及其异样神态。他试图让他们忆起昨夜通宵狂欢的情景，他们却带着陌生的目光冷冷注视着他，似乎对他的旧事重提颇为不悦，更不愿听他提及求取神剑之事。不多时，一名青年霍然起身高呼道：“诸王临朝断案的时辰已到！”
青年们当即起身，引着希布利·巴加拉格步入一座乌木大殿（hall of ebony），奉他坐上首席宝座，他们自己则肃立周遭；瞧啊！群驴与众猴纷纷来到殿前，伴随着阵阵嘶鸣、怪叫与啼声，向他控诉人类与同类的种种不公。希布利·巴加拉格再度目睹这群生灵，不由得勃然大怒，对青年们喝道：“怎么！你们莫不是在戏弄我不成，阿克利斯的主人们！”

===Original===

But they said only, “The burden of his crown is for the King.”
He cooled, thinking, “I will use a spell.” So he touched the lips of an animal with the waters of Paravid, and the animal prated volubly in our language of the kick this ass had given him, and the jibe of that monkey, and of his desire of litigation with such and such a beast for pasture; and the others when they spake had the same complaints to make. Shibli Bagarag listened to them gravely, and it was revealed to him that he who ruleth over men hath a labour and duties of hearing and judging and dispensing judgement similar to those of him who ruleth over apes and asses. Then said he, “O youths, my princes! methinks the sitting in this seat giveth a key to secret sources of wisdom; and I see what it is, the glory and the exaltation coveted by men.” Now, he took from the asses and the monkeys one, and said to it, “Be my chief Vizier,” and to another, “Be my Chamberlain!” and to another, “Be my Treasurer!” and so on, till a dispute arose between the animals, and jealousy of each other was visible in their glances, and they appealed to him clamorously. So he said, “What am I to ye?”
They answered, “Our King!”
And he said, “How so?”
They answered, “By the crowning of the brides of Aklis.”
Then he said, “What be ye, O my subjects?”
They answered, “Men that were searchers of the Sword and plunged into the tank of temptation.”
And he said, “How that?”
They answered, “By the lures of vanity, the blinding of ambition, and tasting the gall of the Roc.”
So Shibli Bagarag leaned to the seven youths, saying, “O my princes, but for not tasting the gall of the Roc I might be as one of these. *Wullahy!* I the King am warned by base creatures.” Then he said to the animals, “Have ye still a longing for the crown?”
And they cried, all of them, “O light of the astonished Earth, we care for nought other than it.”
So he said, “And is it known to ye how to dispossess the wearer of his burden?”
They answered, “By a touch of the gall of the Roc on his forehead.”
Then he lifted his arms, crying, “Hie out of my presence! and whoso of ye fetcheth a drop of the gall, with that one will I exchange the crown.”

===Chinese===

他们却只是淡淡应道：“王冠之重，唯王者自担。”
他按捺下怒火，心想：“我且施展法宝。”于是他以帕拉维德（Paravid）泉水轻触一只牲畜的嘴唇，那牲畜顿时用我们通晓的语言滔滔不绝地告状，控诉这头驴踢了它一脚，那只猴子嘲弄了它，以及它渴望同某头牲畜争夺草场牧地打官司；其余牲畜开口说话时，抱怨的内容亦是大同小异。希布利·巴加拉格面色凝重地倾听着，心中忽然顿悟：统治凡人者所承受的倾听、审断与裁决诸般劳苦与重责，竟同统治猿猴与蠢驴别无二致。于是他道：“诸位青年王子！我发觉端坐此位竟能开启洞悉隐秘智慧的法门；我已然看清了凡人梦寐以求的荣光与显赫究竟是何物。”随后他从驴群与猴群中挑选了一只，说道：“你便做我的宰相（chief Vizier），”又对另一只道：“你便做我的宫务大臣（Chamberlain）！”对又一只道：“你便做我的掌库大臣（Treasurer）！”如此这般逐一封赏，直至牲畜之间爆发了激烈的争端，彼此眼中流露出嫉妒的凶光，吵吵嚷嚷向他哭诉申冤。于是他问道：“在你们眼中，我是何人？”
它们齐声答道：“我们的国王！”
他问：“因何为王？”
它们答道：“因阿克利斯的新娘们为您加冕。”
他又问：“我的臣民们啊，你们原本是何人？”
它们答道：“我们曾是求索神剑的凡人，却跌入了诱惑的深潭。”
他问：“此话怎讲？”
它们答道：“因贪恋虚荣的诱惑，因野心蒙蔽了双眼，并尝到了大鹏鸟的苦胆（the gall of the Roc）。”
希布利·巴加拉格转向那七位青年，叹道：“诸位王子，若非我未曾尝过大鹏鸟的苦胆，恐怕我如今亦沦为了它们的一员。凭安拉起誓（Wullahy）！我这堂堂君王竟受了卑微牲畜的当头棒喝。”随后他对牲畜们说道：“你们心中可还渴望这顶王冠？”
它们齐声高呼道：“惊叹大地的明灯啊，除却王冠，我们一无所求。”
他问道：“你们可知晓该如何为头戴王冠者卸下这重负？”
它们答道：“只需以一滴大鹏鸟的苦胆轻触其额头便可。”
他当即高举双臂喊道：“速速从我眼前退下！你们当中无论何人取来一滴大鹏鸟苦胆，我便将这顶王冠与它交换！”

===Original===

At these words some moved hastily, but the most faltered, as doubting and incredulous that he would propose such an exchange; and one, an old monkey, sat down and crossed his legs, and made a study of Shibli Bagarag, as of a sovereign that held forth a deceiving bargain. But he cried again, “Hie and haste! as my head is now cased I think it not the honoured part.”
Then the old monkey arose with a puzzled look, half scornful, and made for the door slowly, turning his head toward Shibli Bagarag betweenwhiles as he went, and scratching his lower limbs with the mute reflectiveness of age and extreme caution.
Now, when they were gone, Shibli Bagarag looked in the eyes of the seven youths, and saw they were content with him, and his countenance was brightened with approval. So he descended from his seat, and went with them from the hall of ebony to a court where horses were waiting saddled, and slaves with hawks on their wrists stood in readiness; and they mounted each a horse, but he loitered. The seven youths divined his feeling, and cried impatiently, “Come! no lingering in Aklis!” So he mounted likewise, and they emerged from the palace, and entered the hills that glowed under the copper sun, and started a milk-white antelope with ruby spots, and chased it from its cover over the sand-hills, a hawk being let loose to worry it and distress its timid beaming eyes. When the creature was quite overcome, one of the youths struck his heel into his horse’s side and flung a noose over the head of the quarry, and drew it with them, gently petting it the way home to the palace. At the gates of the palace it was released, and lo! it went up the steps, and passed through the halls as one familiar with them. Now, when they were all assembled in the anteroom of the hall, where Shibli Bagarag had first seen the seven youths, sons of Aklis, in their jollity, one of them said to the Antelope, “We have need of thee to speak a word with Aklis, O our sister!”
So the same youth requested the use of the phial of Paravid, and Shibli Bagarag applied it carefully, tenderly, to the mouth of the Antelope. Then the Antelope spake in a silver-ringing voice, saying, “What is it, O my brothers?”

===Chinese===

听闻此言，有几只牲畜仓皇行动起来，但绝大多数却犹豫不决，满腹狐疑，难以置信他竟会提出这等交换；其中一只老猴盘腿坐下，打量着希布利·巴加拉格，仿佛在审视一位开出骗人空头支票的君王。他再次高呼道：“快去快回！我如今头戴此物，根本算不得什么荣耀！”
老猴这才带着半是困惑、半是轻蔑的神色缓缓站起，慢吞吞朝殿门走去，其间不住回头打量希布利·巴加拉格，带着老态龙钟的沉默沉思与极度戒备搔抓着下肢。
待群兽退尽，希布利·巴加拉格凝视着七位青年的双眸，见他们眼中皆流露出欣许之色，他的面容也因得到认可而容光焕发。于是他走下宝座，随他们离开乌木大殿，来到一座备好鞍辔骏马的庭院，手架猎鹰的奴仆早已在一旁恭候；青年们各自翻身上马，他却迟疑不决。七位青年看穿了他的心思，不耐烦地催促道：“快来！阿克利斯不容徘徊迟疑！”于是他也翻身上马，众人策马奔出宫殿，步入在铜色烈日下灼灼生辉的群山，惊起了一头缀满红宝石斑点的乳白羚羊（milk-white antelope with ruby spots），一路将它从藏身处撵过座座沙丘，放飞猎鹰纠缠抓挠它，令那双胆怯明亮的眸子惊恐万状。待那生灵力竭不支之际，一名青年策马加速，将套索准确抛在猎物的头上，将它牵引带回，一路上轻柔抚慰着它返回宫殿。在宫殿大门前，羚羊被松开绳索，瞧啊！它熟门熟路地踏上台阶，穿过座座殿堂，宛若故地重游。当众人齐聚在希布利·巴加拉格初见阿克利斯七子欢宴的大殿前厅时，一名青年对羚羊说道：“我们需要你出面同父亲阿克利斯说句话，我们的妹妹啊！”
随后这名青年请求借用帕拉维德药瓶，希布利·巴加拉格小心翼翼、极尽轻柔地将药水滴在羚羊唇边。羚羊随即以清脆悦耳如银铃般的声音开口道：“兄长们，有何要事？”

===Original===

They answered, “Thou knowest we dare not attempt interchange of speech with Aklis, seeing that we disobeyed him in visiting the kingdoms of the Earth: so it is for thee to question him as to the object of this youth, and it is the Shaving of Shagpat.”
So she said, “ ’Tis well; I wot of it.”
Then she advanced to the curtain concealing the abyss of the Roc and the bridge of its eggs, and went behind it. There was a pause, and they heard her say presently in a grave voice, toned with reverence, “How is it, O our father? is it a good thing that thy Sword be in use at this season?”
And they heard the Voice answer from a depth, “ ’Twere well it rust not!”
They heard her say, “O our father Aklis, and we wish to know if be held in favour by thee, and thou sanction it with thy Sword.”
And they heard the Voice answer, “The Shaving of Shagpat is my Sword alone equal to, and he that shaveth him performeth a service to mankind ranking next my vanquishing of the Roc.”
Then they heard her say, “And it is thy will we teach him the mysteries of the Sword, and that which may be done with it?”
And they heard the Voice answer, “Even so!”
After that the Voice was still, and soon the Antelope returned from behind the curtain, and the youths caressed her with brotherly caresses, and took a circle of hands about her, and so moved to the great hall of the gorgeous Tree, and fed her from the branches. Now, while they were there, Shibli Bagarag advanced to the Antelope, and knelt at her feet, and said, “O Princess of Aklis, surely I am betrothed to one constant as a fixed star, and brighter; a mistress of magic, and innocent as the bleating lamb; and she is now on a pillar, chained there, in the midst of the white wrathful sea, wailing for me to deliver her with this Sword of my seeking. So, now, I pray thee help me to the Sword swiftly, that I may deliver her.”
The youths, her brothers, clamoured and interposed, saying, “Take thy shape ere that, O Gulrevaz, our sister!”
But she cried, “He is betrothed! not till he graspeth the Sword. Tell him, the youth, our conditions, and for what exchange the Sword is yielded.”
And they said, “The conditions are, thou part with thy spells, all of them, O youth!”
And he said, “There is no condition harsh that exchangeth the Sword; O ye Seven, I agree!”
Then she said, “ ’Tis well! nobility is in the soul of this youth.

===Chinese===

他们答道：“你深知我们因当年违抗父命私自巡游人间王国，不敢擅自同阿克利斯搭话；因此须由你出面，向他请示这少年的宏愿——亦即剃度沙格帕特（the Shaving of Shagpat）一事。”
羚羊道：“甚好；我自理会得。”
随后她迈步走向遮掩着大鹏鸟深渊与巨蛋之桥的帷幔，隐入其后。良久寂静之后，众人听见她以庄重崇敬的语调轻声问道：“父亲大人，意下如何？在此时节启封您的神剑，可算得上善举？”
他们听见一个宏亮的声音从幽冥深处应答道：“不教神剑生锈，自是美事！”
又听她问道：“我们的父亲阿克利斯啊，我们欲知晓此事是否合乎您的心意，您是否准允借出神剑。”
那声音自深处答道：“剃度沙格帕特唯有我的神剑方能胜任，凡能将他剃光之人，对人间所建之功勋仅次于我当年降伏大鹏鸟之伟绩。”
接着又听她问道：“那么您是否准允我们传授他神剑的奥秘与驱使之法？”
那声音答道：“正合我意！”
此后那声音便沉寂下去，不多时羚羊自帷幔后步出，青年们以手足之情爱怜地抚摸她，携手将她簇拥在中央，移步来到矗立着黄金巨树的大殿，自树枝上采撷鲜果喂食于她。正当众人置身殿中之际，希布利·巴加拉格走向羚羊，跪拜在她脚下，恳求道：“阿克利斯的公主啊，我已同一位坚定如恒星且比群星更璀璨的女子订下白首之约；她精通巫术，却纯真如咩咩啼鸣的羊羔；此刻她正被铁链锁在怒涛翻滚的惨白大海上的一根石柱上，苦苦哀啼盼我以求索的神剑前去解救。因此，我恳求你速速助我取得神剑，好让我前去搭救于她。”
身为兄长的青年们纷纷嚷嚷着打断道：“在此之前先变回你的真身吧，我们的妹妹古尔雷瓦兹（Gulrevaz）！”
她却高呼道：“他已与人订婚！在他握住神剑之前万不可变身。且告知这少年我们的条件，换取神剑须付出何等代价。”
青年们说道：“条件便是，少年啊，你必须交出你身上的一切护身法宝！”
希布利·巴加拉格道：“为了换取神剑，绝无苛刻之条件可言；七位王子，我全然应允！”
羚羊道：“善哉！这少年的灵魂深处自有一番崇高气节。

===Original===

Go before us now to the Cave of Chrysolites, O my brothers.”
So these departed before, and she in her antelope form followed footing gracefully, and made Shibli Bagarag repeat the story of his betrothal as they went.

===Chinese===

兄长们，如今且引路前往金绿玉洞窟（the Cave of Chrysolites）吧。”
青年们当即在前方引路，羚羊优雅地踏步紧随其后，一路上命希布利·巴加拉格将他定亲的故事原原本本再细述了一遍。

## The Sword of Aklis / 阿克利斯神剑
===Original===

Now, when they had made the passage of many halls, built of different woods, filled with divers wonders, they descended a sloping vault, and came to a narrow way in the earth, hung with black, at the end of it a steadfast blaze like a sun, that grew larger as they advanced, and they heard the sea above them. The noise of it, and its plunging and weltering and its pitilessness, struck on the heart of Shibli Bagarag as with a blow, and he cried, “Haste, haste, O Princess! perchance she is even now calling to me with her tongue, and I not aiding her; delayed by the temptation of this crown and the guile of the Brides.”
She checked him, and said, “In Aklis no haste!” Then she said, “Look!” And lo, fronting them the single blaze became two fires; and drawing nigh, Shibli Bagarag beheld them what they were, angry eyes in the head of a great lion, a model of majesty, and passion was in his mane and power was in his forepaws; so while he lashed his tail as a tempest whippeth the tawny billows at night, and was lifting himself for a roar, she said, “A hair of Garraveen, and touch him with it!”
Shibli Bagarag pushed up his sleeve and broke one of the three sapphire hairs and stepped forward to the lion, holding in his right hand the hair of vivid light. The lion crouched, and was in the vigour of the spring when that hair touched him, and he trembled, tumbling on his knees and letting the twain pass. So they advanced beyond him, and lo! the Cave of Chrysolites irradiate with beams, breaks of brilliance, confluences of lively hues, restless rays, meeting, vanishing, flooding splendours, now scattered in dazzling joints and spars, now uniting in momentary disks of radiance. In the centre of the cave glowed a furnace, and round it he distinguished the seven youths, swarthier and sterner than before, dark sweat standing on the brows of each. Their words were brief, and they wore each a terrible frown, saying to him, without further salutation, “Thrust in the flame of this furnace thy right wrist.”
At the same moment, the Antelope said in his ear, “Do thou their bidding, and be not backward! In Aklis fear is ruin, and hesitation a destroyer.”
He fixed his mind on the devotedness of Noorna, and held his nether lip tightly between his teeth, and thrust his right wrist in the flame of the furnace.

===Chinese===

穿过许多以不同名木建造、陈列着诸般奇珍的大殿之后，他们顺着一座倾斜的地窖拾级而下，来到地下的一条幽窄地道，两侧悬挂着黑幔，在地道尽头闪烁着一团如烈日般恒久炽烈的强光，随着他们举步向前，强光越来越大，上方传来了茫茫大涨的浪涛声。狂暴的大海奔涌轰鸣，巨浪翻腾倾泻，那冷酷无情的回响如重锤般狠狠砸在希布利·巴加拉格（Shibli Bagarag）的心头，他失声喊道：“快，快些，公主！也许她此刻正呼唤着我的名字，而我却未能施以援手；全怪我被那顶王冠的诱惑与新娘们的诡计所耽搁。”
她拦住他道：“身在阿克利斯（Aklis），切莫急躁！”随后她道：“瞧！”看哪，面对着他们的一团强光骤然化作了两道烈火；走近一看，希布利·巴加拉格方才看清那究竟是何物——那分明是一头身形巍峨、王者威仪具足的雄狮头顶愤怒燃烧的双目，它的鬃毛散发着狂暴的野性，前爪蕴含着惊人的神力；正当它如暴风雨在子夜鞭笞黄褐浪涛般狂暴抽甩尾巴、挺身准备发出震天咆哮之际，羚羊低语道：“加拉文（Garraveen）的鬃毛，速用它轻触雄狮！”
希布利·巴加拉格卷起衣袖，扯下三根蓝宝石鬃毛中的一根，跨步走上前去，右手紧握着那根散发着夺目强光的发丝。雄狮伏低身躯，正处于纵身飞扑的猛烈蓄势之中，然而就在发丝触及它的一刹那，它浑身颤抖起来，双膝跪跌在地，任由二人从容通过。他们从它身旁穿过，瞧啊！金绿玉洞窟（the Cave of Chrysolites）内万道神光四射，明艳的光芒交相辉映，绚丽的色彩汇流奔涌，变幻不定的光束相聚、消逝，倾泻下无尽的光华，时而如耀眼的棱角与晶石般四散碎裂，时而又聚合成转瞬即逝的光轮。在洞窟中央燃烧着一座熔炉，在熔炉周遭，他认出了那七位青年（seven youths），神色比先前更为黝黑冷峻，每人额头上皆凝结着沉重的汗珠。他们言语简短，每人皆紧蹙着令人不寒而栗的眉头，连一句招呼也未打，便对他说：“将你的右手腕伸入这熔炉的烈火之中。”
就在这一刻，羚羊在他耳畔低语道：“照他们说的去做，休要退缩！在阿克利斯，恐惧便是毁灭，迟疑便会丧命。”
他心念努尔娜的坚贞挚爱，下唇死死咬在牙齿之间，毅然将右手腕伸入了熔炉的烈焰之中。

===Original===

The wrist reddened, and became transparent with heat, but he felt no pain, only that his whole arm was thrice its natural weight. Then the flame of the furnace fell, and the seven youths made him kneel by a brook of golden waters and dip his forehead up to his eyes in the waters. Then they took him to the other side of the cave, and his sight was strengthened to mark the glory of the Sword, where it hung in slings, a little way from the wall, outshining the lights of the cave, and throwing them back with its superior force and steadfastness of lustre. Lo! the length of it was as the length of crimson across the sea when the sun is sideways on the wave, and it seemed full a mile long, the whole blade sheening like an arrested lightning from the end to the hilt; the hilt two large live serpents twined together, with eyes like sombre jewels, and sparkling spotted skins, points of fire in their folds, and reflections of the emerald and topaz and ruby stones, studded in the bloodstained haft. Then the seven young men, sons of Aklis, said to Shibli Bagarag, “Surrender the Lily!” And when he had given into their hands the Lily, they said, “Grasp the handle of the Sword!”
Now, he beheld the Sword and the ripples of violet heat that were breathing down it, and those two venomous serpents twined together, and the size of it, its ponderousness; and to essay lifting it appeared to him a madness, but he concealed his thought, and, setting his soul on the safety of Noorna, went forward to it boldly, and piercing his right arm between the twists of the serpents, grasped the jewelled haft. Surely, the Sword moved from the slings as if a giant had swayed it! But what amazed him was the marvel of the blade, for its sharpness was such that nothing stood in its way, and it slipped through everything as we pass through still water, the stone columns, blocks of granite by the walls, the walls of earth, and the thick solidity of the ground beneath his feet. They bade him say to the Sword, “Sleep!” and it was no longer than a knife in the girdle. Likewise, they bade him hiss on the heads of the serpents, and say, “Wake!” and while he held it lengthwise it shot lengthening out. Then they bade him hold in one hand the sapphire hair that conquered the lion, and with the edge of the Sword touch one point of it.

===Chinese===

他的手腕被烈火烧得通红，变得晶莹透明，但他却感觉不到一丝疼痛，唯觉得整条右臂沉重了三倍（thrice its natural weight）。随后熔炉的烈火骤然熄灭，七位青年让他跪倒在一条流淌着金色泉水的小溪旁，将额头浸入泉水之中直至没及双眼。随后他们将他引至洞窟的另一侧，他的目力大增，足以直视神剑的万道华光；只见神剑悬挂在离石壁不远处的吊索之上，压倒了洞中所有的光芒，以其无匹的威能与恒久不灭的灿烂将洞中的强光尽数逼退。看哪！神剑之长，恰似夕阳斜照波涛之际横贯海面的道道赤霞，足有整整一里（full a mile long）之遥，整柄剑身从剑尖到剑柄皆如被生生定格的雷霆霹雳般流光溢彩；剑柄由两条缠绕交织的活蛇铸成，蛇眸如阴郁的宝石，覆满斑斓发光的鳞皮，层层盘绕间隐现点点火星，剑柄的血痕之间镶嵌着祖母绿、黄玉与红宝石，折射出万千光华。阿克利斯的七位年轻之子对希布利·巴加拉格说道：“交出百合！”待他将妙光百合递入他们手中，他们便道：“握住神剑的剑柄！”
此时，他凝视着神剑与剑身上缓缓流淌呼吸的层层紫焰，望着两条缠绕的毒蛇与那惊人的尺寸与沉重；试图将它举起在他看来简直是疯了，但他掩饰住心中的惊惶，将全副心神寄托在努尔娜的安危之上，毅然大步上前，右手穿过交缠的双蛇之间，死死握住了镶满宝石的剑柄。瞧啊，神剑从吊索上应手而起，仿佛由一位顶天立地的巨人挥动一般！然而令他惊叹莫名的乃是剑刃的神异，其锋利程度天地间无物可挡，无论石柱、墙边的花岗岩巨石、泥土的岩壁，抑或脚下坚实的厚重大地，剑刃皆一掠而过，恰若穿行在平静的水流之中。他们命他向神剑念诵：“入眠！”神剑顿时缩短得如插在腰带上的一把小刀。随后他们又命他向着蛇头吹出嘶嘶之声，念诵：“苏醒！”当他平端长剑之际，剑刃顿时破空疾射延伸开来。接着他们命他单手握住降伏雄狮的那根蓝宝石鬃毛，以剑锋轻触其尖端。

===Original===

So he did that, and it split in half, and the two halves he also split; and he split those four, and those eight, till the hairs were thin as light and not distinguishable from it. When Shibli Bagarag saw the power of the Sword, he exulted and cried, “Praise be to the science of them that forecast events and the haps of life!” Now, in the meantime he marked the youths take those hairs of Garraveen that he had split, and tie them round the neck of the Antelope, and empty the contents of the phial down her throat; and they put the bulb of the Lily, that was a heart, in her mouth, and she swallowed it till the flower covered her face. Then they took each a handful of the golden waters of the brook flowing through the cave, and flung the waters over her, exclaiming, “By the three spells that have power in Aklis, and by which these waters are a blessing!”
In the passing of a flash she took her shape, and was a damsel taller than the tallest of them that descend from the mountains, a vision of loveliness, with queenly brows, closed red lips, and large full black eyes; her hair black, and on it a net of amber strung with pearls. To look upon her was to feel the tyranny of love, love’s pangs of alarm and hope and anguish; and she was dressed in a dress of white silk, threaded with gold and sapphire, showing in shadowy beams her rounded figure and the stateliness that was hers. So she ran to her brothers and embraced them, calling them by their names, catching their hands, caressing them as one that had been long parted from them. Then, seeing Shibli Bagarag as he stood transfixed with the javelins of loveliness that flew from her on all sides, she cried: “What, O Master of the Event! halt thou nought for the Sword but to gaze before thee in silliness?”
Then he said, “O rare in beauty! marvel of Aklis and the world! surely the paradise of eyes is thy figure and the glory of thy face!”
But she shouted, “To work with the Sword! Shame on thee! is there not one, a bright one, a miracle in faithfulness, that awaiteth thy rescue on the pillar?”
And she repeated the praises he had spoken of Noorna bin Noorka, his betrothed. Then he grasped the Sword firmly, remembering the love of Noorna, and crying, “Lead me from this, O ye sons of Aklis, and thou, Princess Gulrevaz, lead me, that I may come to her.”
So they said, “Follow us!”

===Chinese===

他依言而行，发丝顿时被一剖为二，两半随即又被剖成四份；剖成四根、八根，直至发丝细如光线，同光芒融为一体无法分辨。希布利·巴加拉格目睹神剑的神威，喜不自胜高呼道：“赞美洞悉天机与人世吉凶的大学问！”此时此刻，他望见青年们取来被他劈开的加拉文鬃毛，紧紧系在羚羊的脖颈上，并将药瓶中的药水尽数倒入口中喂它服下；他们又将那朵百合的心脏球茎放入它口中，羚羊将球茎吞入腹中，直至整朵花覆盖住了它的面庞。随后每位青年从流经洞窟的小溪中捧起一捧金色泉水，洒在它的身上，齐声高呼道：“凭在阿克利斯具有无上神威的三大法宝，凭赋予这溪水以庇佑的神力！”
转瞬之间，羚羊褪去了原形，化作了一位身姿比自崇山峻岭款款走下的最婀娜少女更为修长的高挑佳人，宛若绝色仙影，生就一双女王般的娥眉、微闭的朱唇与深邃的大黑眸；乌黑的秀发上罩着缀满珍珠的琥珀发网。凝视于她，便能感受到情爱的霸道统治，尝尽情爱中惊悸、希冀与痛苦的煎熬；她身披织有金丝与蓝宝石的洁白丝裙，在若隐若现的光芒中显露出丰腴玲珑的身段与天生的雍容气度。她奔向自己的兄长们，一一拥抱呼唤他们的名字，执起他们的手，如久别重逢之人般温存相抚。随后，见希布利·巴加拉格被她周身散发出的万千绝美箭矢射得呆若木鸡，她高声喊道：“怎么，命定大事的主宰（Master of the Event）！难道你手握神剑，就只会傻愣愣呆立在原地吗？”
他这才说道：“罕见的倾城绝色啊！阿克利斯与世间的旷世奇观！你的身段与娇颜的荣光，真乃凡人双眼的极乐之境！”
她却厉声呵斥道：“还不快去驱使神剑成就大业！你真不害臊！难道在那孤绝石柱之上，没有一位坚贞不渝的奇女子正苦苦等候着你的营救吗？”
她将他昔日赞美未婚妻努尔卡之子努尔娜（Noorna bin Noorka）的言辞原原本本复述了一遍。想起努尔娜的深情，希布利·巴加拉格紧紧握住神剑，高呼道：“快引我离开此地，阿克利斯的诸子啊，古尔雷瓦兹公主（Princess Gulrevaz），快引我去她身旁吧。”
他们齐声道：“随我们来！”

===Original===

and he sheathed the Sword in his girdle with the word “Sleep!” and followed them, his heart beating violently.

===Chinese===

他口诵“入眠！”，将神剑收入腰带之中，按捺着狂跳的心口紧紧跟随在他们身后。

## The Talking Hawk / 会说话的苍鹰
===Original===

Now, when he awoke he found himself alone in that place, the moon shining over the low meadows and flower-cups fair with night-dew. Odours of night-flowers were abroad, filling the cool air with deliciousness, and he heard in the gardens below songs of the bulbul: it was like a dream to his soul, and he lay somewhile contemplating the rich loveliness of the scene, that showed no moving thing. Then rose he and bethought him of the words of Noorna, and of the City of Oolb, and the phial of the waters of Paravid in his vest; and he drew it forth, and dropped a drop of it on the rock where he had reclined. A deep harmony seemed suddenly to awake inside the rock, and to his interrogation as to the direction of Oolb, he heard, “The path of the shadows of the moon.”
Thereupon he advanced to a prominent part of the rocks above the meadows, and beheld the shadows of the moon thrown forward into dimness across a waste of sand. And he stepped downward to the level of sand, and went the way of the shadows till it was dawn. Then dropped he a drop of the waters of the phial on a spike of lavender, and there was a voice said to him in reply to what he questioned, “The path of the shadows of the sun.”
The shadows of the sun were thrown forward across the same waste of sand, and he turned and pursued his way, resting at noon beneath a date-tree, and refreshing himself at a clear spring beside it. Surely he was joyful as he went, and elated with high prospects, singing:

> “Sun and moon with their bright fingers

Point the hero’s path;
> If in his great work he lingers,

Well may they be wroth.”

===Chinese===

醒来之际，他发现自己形单影只身处彼处，明月清辉洒在低矮的草场与凝着夜露的娇美花盏之上。夜开的花卉芬芳四溢，令清凉的夜空充盈着醉人的馨香，他听见下方园林中传来夜莺（bulbul）婉转啼鸣：宛若一场抚慰心灵的美梦，他静静躺卧了片刻，凝望着这片毫无生灵走动的静谧美景。随后他翻身站起，忆起了努尔娜（Noorna）的叮嘱、乌尔布城（City of Oolb），以及揣在怀里的帕拉维德（Paravid）泉水小药瓶；他掏出药瓶，将一滴泉水滴在他先前斜倚的岩石之上。岩石内部仿佛骤然回荡起一阵深沉悠扬的和鸣，对于他询问前往乌尔布方向的话语，只听那声音答道：“循着月影之路。”
于是他迈步走向草场上方一块突出的岩石，只见月光将道道阴影斜斜投射向前方昏暗无垠的荒漠之中。他走下岩石踏上沙地，循着阴影的指引一路前行，直至拂晓降临。随后他又将一滴药水滴在一束薰衣草的麦穗上，对于他的发问，只听一个声音应答道：“循着日影之路。”
日光将长长的阴影投向前方的同一片茫茫沙海，他转身循着阴影继续赶路，正午时分在一株椰枣树下歇息，以身旁清冽的泉水解渴消乏。一路上他满心欢喜，为光明的前程心潮澎湃，高声放歌道：

> “日月舒展光明指，
> 为君指引英雄路；
> 大业未成若徘徊，
> 日月亦当生雷霆。”

===Original===

Now, the extent of the duration of his travel was four days and an equal number of nights; and it was on the fifth morn that he entered the gates of a city by the sea, even at that hour when the inhabitants were rising from sleep: fair was the sea beyond it, and the harbour was crowded with vessels, ships stored with merchandise﻿—silks, dates, diamonds, Damascus steel, huge bales piled on the decks for the land of Roum and other lands. Shibli Bagarag thought, “There’s scarce a doubt but that one of those sails will set for Oolb shortly. *Wullahy!* if I knew which, I’d board her and win a berth in her.” Presently he thought, “I’ll go to the public fountain and question it with the speech-winning waters.” Thereupon he passed down the streets of the city and came to an open space, where stood the fountain, and sprinkled it with Paravid; and the fountain spake, saying, “Where men are, question not dumb things.”
Cried he, “Faileth Paravid in its power? Have I done aught to baffle myself?”
Then he thought, “ ’Twere nevertheless well to do as the fountain directeth, and question men while I see them.” And he walked about among the people, and came to the quays of the harbour where the ships lay close in, many of them an easy leap from shore, and considered whom to address. So, as he loitered about the quays, meditating on the means at the disposal of the All-Wise, and marking the vessels wistfully, behold, there advanced to him one at a quick pace, in the garb of a sailor. He observed Shibli Bagarag attentively a moment, and exclaimed as it were in the plenitude of respect and with the manner of one that is abashed, “Surely, thou art Shibli Bagarag, the nephew of the barber, him we watch for.”
So Shibli Bagarag marvelled at this recognition, and answered, “Am I then already famous to that extent?”
And he that accosted him said, “ ’Tis certain the trumpet was blown before thy steps, and there is not a man in this city but knoweth of thy destination to the City of Oolb, and that thou art upon the track of great things, one chosen to bring about imminent changes.”
Then said Shibli Bagarag, “For this I praise Noorna bin Noorka, daughter of Feshnavat, Vizier of the King that ruleth in the city of Shagpat! She saw me, that I was marked for greatness.

===Chinese===

他一路跋涉了四日四夜（four days and an equal number of nights）；在第五日清晨（the fifth morn），正是城中百姓自睡梦中起身梳洗的时分，他步入了一座滨海之城的城门：城外海天一色极其秀美，港湾内泊满了千帆巨舶，船上满载着各色货物——丝绸、蜜枣、钻石、大马士革钢剑，甲板上堆叠着运往鲁姆（Roum）以及诸邦异域的沉重货包。希布利·巴加拉格（Shibli Bagarag）暗忖：“这些船只中定有一艘不日便要起航驶向乌尔布（Oolb）。凭安拉起誓（Wullahy）！我若知晓是哪一艘，便能登船在其中谋个舱位。”随后他又想：“我且去城中的公共喷泉，以通灵之水向它问路。”于是他沿着城中街巷穿行，来到一处开阔的广场，喷泉便座落在此，他将帕拉维德（Paravid）泉水洒在喷泉上；喷泉随即开口道：“凡有人烟处，莫向无言死物问路。”
他失声叫道：“难道帕拉维德的神力失灵了？莫非我做了什么自乱阵脚的蠢事？”
转念一想：“不过遵从喷泉的训诫，在目力所及之处向凡人打听亦是好事。”于是他漫步在人群之中，来到船只紧挨着岸边停泊的码头，许多船舷离岸边不过纵身一跃的距离，他思索着该向谁打听。正当他在码头上游荡、琢磨着至智之主的巧妙安排、满怀渴盼地打量着一艘艘船只之际，看哪，一个水手打扮的人迈着急促的步伐朝他走来。那人仔细端详了希布利·巴加拉格片刻，带着极尽恭顺与惶恐不安的神态惊呼道：“千真万确，阁下想必就是理发师的侄儿希布利·巴加拉格，我们苦苦守候之人吧。”
希布利·巴加拉格对被人认出大感惊异，应道：“难道我的威名竟已远播至此了吗？”
那上前搭话之人道：“出征的战角定然早已响彻在您的足迹之前，如今此城之中无人不知您正启程前往乌尔布城，不知您正踏在建功立业的大道之上，乃是被天命选中引动旷世巨变之人。”
希布利·巴加拉格道：“为此我当盛赞统治沙格帕特城之国王的宰相费什纳瓦特（Feshnavat）之女——努尔卡之子努尔娜（Noorna bin Noorka）！她慧眼识珠，看出我是身负大任之人。

===Original===

*Wullahy*, the eagle knoweth me from afar, and proclaimeth me; the antelope of the hills scenteth the coming of one not as other men, and telleth his tidings; the wind of the desert shapeth its gust to a meaning, so that the stranger may wot Shibli Bagarag is at hand!”
He puffed his chest, and straightened his legs like the cock, and was as a man upon whom the Sultan has bestowed a dress of honour, even as the plumed peacock. Then the other said:
“Know that I am captain of yonder vessel, that stands farthest out from the harbour with her sails slackened; and she is laden with figs and fruits which I exchange for silks, spices, and other merchandise, with the people of Oolb. Now, what says the poet?﻿—

> “ ‘Delay in thine undertaking

Is disaster of thy own making’;

“and he says also:

> “ ‘Greatness is solely for them that succeed;

’Tis a rotten applause that gives earlier meed.’

===Chinese===

凭安拉起誓（Wullahy），雄鹰自九霄云外便已认出我、宣告我的名号；山野的羚羊嗅出非同凡俗之人的降临，四处传诵他的佳音；大漠的风沙亦卷起意义深远的狂飙，让异乡异客皆知晓希布利·巴加拉格已近在眼前！”
他挺起胸膛，如雄鸡般绷直双腿，恰似荣获苏丹赐予锦袍的宠臣，又似开屏斗艳的孔雀。此时那人开口道：
“可知我便是远处那艘抛锚在港湾最外侧、收拢风帆的巨舰船长；船上满载着无花果与各色鲜果，我正欲运往乌尔布换取当地人的丝绸、香料与其他各色货物。正如诗人所云：

> ‘大业之中若迟延，
> 祸患皆自自身招’；

“他又道：

> ‘至尊伟业唯属功成名就者；
> 提前逢迎之赞誉腐朽不堪。’

===Original===

“Therefore it is advisable for thee to follow me on board without loss of time, and we will sail this very night for the City of Oolb.”
Now, Shibli Bagarag was ruled by the words of the captain albeit he desired to stay awhile and receive the homage of the people of that city. So he followed him into a boat that was by, and the twain were rowed by sailors to the ship. Then, when they were aboard the captain set sail, and they were soon in the hollows of deep waters. There was a berth in the ship set apart for Shibli Bagarag, and one for the captain. Shibli Bagarag, when he entered his berth, beheld at the head of his couch a hawk; its eyes red as rubies, its beak sharp as the curve of a scimitar. So he called out to the captain, and the captain came to him; but when he saw the hawk, he plucked his turban from his head, and dashed it at the hawk, and afterward ran to it, trying to catch it; and the hawk flitted from corner to corner of the berth, he after it with open arms. Then he took a sword, but the hawk flew past him, and fixed on the back part of his head, tearing up his hair by the talons, and pecking over his forehead at his eyes. And Shibli Bagarag heard the hawk scream the name “Karaz,” and he looked closely at the Captain of the vessel, and knew him for the genie Karaz. Then trembled he with exceeding terror, cursing his credulities, for he saw himself in the hands of the genie, and nothing but this hawk friendly to him on the fearful waters. When the hawk had torn up a certain hair, the genie stiffened, and glowed like copper in the furnace, the whole length of him; and he descended heavily through the bottom of the ship, and sank into the waters beneath, which hissed and smoked as at a bar of heated iron. Then Shibli Bagarag gave thanks to the Prophet, and praised the hawk, but the hawk darted out of the cabin, and he followed it on deck, and, lo! the vessel was in flames, and the hawk in a circle of the flames; and the flames soared with it, and left it no outlet. Now, as Shibli Bagarag watched the hawk, the flames stretched out towards him and took hold of his vestments. So he delayed not to commend his soul to the All-Merciful, and bore witness to his faith, and plunged into the sea headlong.

===Chinese===

“因此你切当刻不容缓随我登船，我们今夜便启航驶向乌尔布城。”
